```
import { EditorCamera } from "./editor-camera.js";
import { BuildingPlacement } from "./building-placement.js";
import { FloorPlanReference } from "./floor-plan-reference.js";
import { FloorPlanBuilder } from "./floor-plan-builder.js";
import {
    FloorPlanHeightControl
} from "./floor-plan-height-control.js";
import {
    BuildingPreviewRenderer
} from "./building-preview-renderer.js";

class BuildingEditor {
    constructor(scene, buildingSystem, spatialTransform) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.system = buildingSystem;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        // Building geometry must use the authoritative
        // world-to-render coordinate transformation.
        this.system.renderer.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.editorCamera = new EditorCamera(
            scene,
            spatialTransform
        );

        this.placement = new BuildingPlacement(
            buildingSystem,
            this.editorCamera
        );

        this.floorPlan = new FloorPlanReference(
            scene,
            spatialTransform
        );
this.floorPlanBuilder = new FloorPlanBuilder({
    wallHeight: 3.2,
    doorHeight: 2.1,
    windowHeight: 1.2,
    windowSill: 0.9
});

        this.mode = "room";
        this.gridSize = 1;
        this.activeBuilding = null;
        this.selectedRoom = null;
        this.startPoint = null;
        this.enabled = false;

        this.createInterface();
this.floorPlanHeightControl =
    new FloorPlanHeightControl(
        this.container,
        this.floorPlanBuilder,
        () => {
            if (this.floorPlan.mesh) {
                this.buildFloorFromPlan();
            }
        }
    );
this.previewRenderer = new BuildingPreviewRenderer(
    scene,
    buildingSystem.geometryRenderer
);

this.floorPlanHeightControl =
    new FloorPlanHeightControl(
        this.container,
        this.floorPlanBuilder,
        () => {
            if (
                this.activeBuilding &&
                this.floorPlan.mesh
            ) {
                this.buildFloorFromPlan();
            }
        }
    );

this.installFloorPlanWorkflowControls();

        this.pointerObserver =
            scene.onPointerObservable.add(info => {
                if (!this.enabled) return;

                if (
                    info.type === BABYLON.PointerEventTypes.POINTERDOWN
                ) {
                    this.pointerDown();
                }

                if (
                    info.type === BABYLON.PointerEventTypes.POINTERUP
                ) {
                    this.pointerUp();
                }
            });
    }

	setGameManager(manager) {
    this.gameManager = manager;

    manager.registerEditor(this.editorCamera.camera);

    this.unsubscribeMode = manager.onModeChanged(mode => {
        const active = mode === "editor";

        this.enabled = active;
        this.container.style.display =
            active ? "block" : "none";

        if (active && this.activeBuilding) {
            this.editorCamera.focus(
                this.activeBuilding.position
            );
        }
    });
}

    createInterface() {
        this.container = document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(this.container.style, {
            position: "fixed",
            top: "70px",
            left: "18px",
            width: "280px",
            maxHeight: "85vh",
            overflowY: "auto",
            padding: "16px",
            background: "rgba(19,29,40,0.97)",
            border: "1px solid #536779",
            borderRadius: "8px",
            color: "#fff",
            fontFamily: "Arial,sans-serif",
            zIndex: "1600",
            display: "none"
        });

        this.container.innerHTML = `
            <h3>BUILDING EDITOR</h3>

<div style="display:grid;gap:7px">
    <button data-mode="room">DRAW ROOM</button>
    <button data-mode="door">ADD DOOR</button>
    <button data-mode="window">ADD WINDOW</button>
    <button data-mode="furniture">PLACE FURNITURE</button>
    <button data-mode="floor-plan">POSITION FLOOR PLAN</button>
</div>
            <hr>

            <h4>FLOOR PLAN</h4>
            <input type="file" id="floor-image" accept="image/*">

            <label>Width
                <input id="floor-width" type="number" min="0.1" value="20">
            </label>
	<label>
    Floor Plan Altitude Offset

    <input
        id="floor-altitude"
        type="number"
        step="0.1"
        value="0"
    >
</label>

	<button id="build-floor" style="width:100%">
		BUILD FLOOR FROM PLAN
	</button>

            <label>Depth
                <input id="floor-depth" type="number" min="0.1" value="20">
            </label>

            <label>Opacity
                <input id="floor-opacity" type="range"
                    min="0" max="1" step="0.05" value="0.8">
            </label>

            <label>
                <input id="floor-visible" type="checkbox" checked>
                Show floor plan
            </label>

            <hr>

            <h4>BUILDING POSITION</h4>

            <label>X
                <input id="position-x" type="number" step="0.1" value="0">
            </label>

            <label>Z
                <input id="position-z" type="number" step="0.1" value="0">
            </label>

            <label>Elevation
                <input id="position-y" type="number" step="0.1" value="0">
            </label>

            <label>Rotation (degrees)
                <input id="position-rotation" type="number"
                    step="1" value="0">
            </label>

            <hr>

            <label>Grid size
                <input id="building-grid" type="number"
                    min="0.1" step="0.1" value="1">
            </label>

            <p id="building-status" style="font-size:12px;color:#b8c8d7">
                Select a construction tool.
            </p>

            <button id="building-close" style="width:100%">
                CLOSE EDITOR
            </button>
        `;

        document.body.appendChild(this.container);

        this.status = this.container.querySelector(
            "#building-status"
        );

        this.container.querySelectorAll("[data-mode]")
            .forEach(button => {
                button.onclick = () => this.setMode(
                    button.dataset.mode
                );
            });

        const element = id =>
            this.container.querySelector(`#${id}`);

        element("building-grid").onchange = event => {
            this.gridSize = Math.max(
                0.1,
                Number(event.target.value) || 1
            );
        };

	element("floor-image").onchange = async event => {
    try {
	if (
    this.activeBuilding?.metadata?.editorPreview
) {
    this.setStatus(
        "Please place the current building before importing another plan."
    );

    event.target.value = "";
    return;
}
        await this.floorPlan.load(
            event.target.files[0]
        );
	const width = Number(
    this.container.querySelector("#floor-width").value
);

const depth = width *
    this.floorPlan.image.naturalHeight /
    this.floorPlan.image.naturalWidth;

this.container.querySelector(
    "#floor-depth"
).value = depth.toFixed(2);

this.aspectLock.checked = true;

this.floorPlan.setDimensions(width, depth);

        this.ensureBuildingForFloorPlan();
        this.updateFloorPlanPosition();
	if (
    this.activeBuilding?.metadata?.editorPreview
) {
    this.previewRenderer.show(this.activeBuilding);
}

        this.setStatus(
            "Floor plan imported. Position it, then build the floor."
        );
    } catch (error) {
        this.setStatus(error.message);
    }
};
element("floor-altitude").addEventListener(
    "input",
    event => {
        this.floorPlan.setAltitudeOffset(
            Number(event.target.value) || 0
        );
    }
);

element("build-floor").onclick = () =>
    this.buildFloorFromPlan();

        element("floor-opacity").oninput = event =>
            this.floorPlan.setOpacity(Number(event.target.value));

        element("floor-visible").onchange = event =>
            this.floorPlan.setVisible(event.target.checked);

        for (const id of ["floor-width", "floor-depth"]) {
            element(id).onchange = () => {
                this.updateFloorPlanPosition({
                    width: Number(element("floor-width").value),
                    depth: Number(element("floor-depth").value)
                });
            };
        }

        const updatePlacement = () => {
            if (!this.activeBuilding) return;

            this.placement.update({
                x: Number(element("position-x").value),
                z: Number(element("position-z").value),
                y: Number(element("position-y").value),
                rotation:
                    Number(element("position-rotation").value) *
                    Math.PI / 180
            });

            this.updateFloorPlanPosition();
        };

        for (const id of [
            "position-x",
            "position-z",
            "position-y",
            "position-rotation"
        ]) {
            element(id).onchange = updatePlacement;
        }

        element("building-close").onclick = () => this.close();

        this.launcher = document.createElement("button");
        this.launcher.textContent = "BUILD";

        Object.assign(this.launcher.style, {
            position: "fixed",
            top: "18px",
            left: "18px",
            zIndex: "1601",
            padding: "12px 20px",
            cursor: "pointer"
        });

        this.launcher.onclick = () => this.open();
        document.body.appendChild(this.launcher);
    }

    setStatus(message) {
        this.status.textContent = message;
    }

    setMode(mode) {
        this.mode = mode;
        this.startPoint = null;
        this.setStatus(`Active tool: ${mode}`);
    }

    open() {
        this.container.style.display = "block";
        this.enabled = true;
        this.gameManager?.startEditor();

        if (this.activeBuilding) {
            this.editorCamera.focus(this.activeBuilding.position);
        }
    }

    close() {
        this.container.style.display = "none";
        this.enabled = false;
        this.startPoint = null;
        this.gameManager?.exitEditor();
    }

    snap(value) {
        return Math.round(value / this.gridSize) *
            this.gridSize;
    }

    pickWorldPosition() {
        const pick = this.scene.pick(
            this.scene.pointerX,
            this.scene.pointerY,
            mesh => mesh.isPickable &&
                mesh.metadata?.terrainChunk === true
        );

        if (!pick?.hit || !pick.pickedPoint) return null;

        return this.transform.renderToWorld({
            x: pick.pickedPoint.x,
            y: pick.pickedPoint.y,
            z: pick.pickedPoint.z
        });
    }

    pointerDown() {
	    if (
    this.mode === "floor-plan" ||
    this.mode !== "room"
) {
    return;
}
        const point = this.pickWorldPosition();

        if (this.mode !== "room") return;

        if (!point) {
            this.setStatus("Cannot pick terrain.");
            return;
        }

        this.startPoint = point;
        this.setStatus("Drag to define the room footprint.");
    }

    pointerUp() {
	if (this.mode === "floor-plan") {
    return;
}
        const point = this.pickWorldPosition();

        if (!point) {
            if (this.startPoint) {
                this.startPoint = null;
                this.setStatus("No surface detected.");
            }
            return;
        }

        if (this.mode === "room") {
            if (!this.startPoint) return;

            const start = this.startPoint;
            this.startPoint = null;

            try {
                this.createRoomFromPoints(start, point);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("[BuildingEditor]", error);
                this.setStatus(error.message);
            }

            return;
        }

        if (this.mode === "door" || this.mode === "window") {
            this.placeOpening(this.mode, point);
        }

        if (this.mode === "furniture") {
            this.placeFurniture(point);
        }
    }

    createRoomFromPoints(a, b) {
        const minX = this.snap(Math.min(a.x, b.x));
        const maxX = this.snap(Math.max(a.x, b.x));
        const minZ = this.snap(Math.min(a.z, b.z));
        const maxZ = this.snap(Math.max(a.z, b.z));

        if (
            maxX - minX < this.gridSize ||
            maxZ - minZ < this.gridSize
        ) {
            this.setStatus("Room is too small.");
            return;
        }

        if (!this.activeBuilding) {
            this.activeBuilding = this.system.createBuilding({
                name: "My Building",
                position: {
                    x: minX,
                    y: a.y,
                    z: minZ
                }
            });

            this.placement.select(this.activeBuilding);
            this.syncPositionInputs();
        }

        const building = this.activeBuilding;

        const room = this.system.createRoom(
            building.id,
            {
                name: `Room ${building.rooms.length + 1}`,
                minX: minX - building.position.x,
                maxX: maxX - building.position.x,
                minZ: minZ - building.position.z,
                maxZ: maxZ - building.position.z
            }
        );

        this.selectedRoom = room;

        this.updateFloorPlanPosition();

        this.setStatus(
            `Created ${room.name}: ` +
            `${maxX - minX} × ${maxZ - minZ}`
        );
    }

    placeOpening(type, point) {
        if (!this.activeBuilding || !this.selectedRoom) {
            this.setStatus("Create a room first.");
            return;
        }

        const building = this.activeBuilding;
        const bounds = this.selectedRoom.bounds;

        const x = point.x - building.position.x;
        const z = point.z - building.position.z;

        const distances = [
            { side: "north", distance: Math.abs(z - bounds.minZ),
                offset: (x - bounds.minX) /
                    (bounds.maxX - bounds.minX) },
            { side: "south", distance: Math.abs(z - bounds.maxZ),
                offset: (x - bounds.minX) /
                    (bounds.maxX - bounds.minX) },
            { side: "west", distance: Math.abs(x - bounds.minX),
                offset: (z - bounds.minZ) /
                    (bounds.maxZ - bounds.minZ) },
            { side: "east", distance: Math.abs(x - bounds.maxX),
                offset: (z - bounds.minZ) /
                    (bounds.maxZ - bounds.minZ) }
        ];

        distances.sort((a, b) => a.distance - b.distance);

        const nearest = distances[0];

        if (nearest.offset < 0 || nearest.offset > 1) {
            this.setStatus("Position is outside the selected wall.");
            return;
        }

        const opening = this.system.addOpening(
            building.id,
            this.selectedRoom.id,
            {
                type,
                side: nearest.side,
                offset: nearest.offset,
                width: type === "door" ? 0.9 : 1.4,
                height: type === "door" ? 2.1 : 1.2,
                sill: type === "door" ? 0 : 0.9
            }
        );

        this.setStatus(`Added ${type}: ${opening.id}`);
    }

    placeFurniture(position) {
        if (!this.activeBuilding) {
            this.setStatus("Create a building first.");
            return;
        }

        this.system.addFurniture(
            this.activeBuilding.id,
            {
                type: "table",
                position: {
                    x: position.x - this.activeBuilding.position.x,
                    y: position.y - this.activeBuilding.position.y + 0.5,
                    z: position.z - this.activeBuilding.position.z
                },
                dimensions: { x: 1.6, y: 0.9, z: 1 },
                material: "wood"
            }
        );

        this.setStatus("Furniture placed.");
    }

    syncPositionInputs() {
        if (!this.activeBuilding) return;

        const p = this.activeBuilding.position;
        const set = (id, value) => {
            this.container.querySelector(`#${id}`).value = value;
        };

        set("position-x", p.x);
        set("position-y", p.y);
        set("position-z", p.z);
        set(
            "position-rotation",
            this.activeBuilding.rotation * 180 / Math.PI
        );
    }


updateFloorPlanPosition(dimensions = {}) {
    if (!this.activeBuilding) return;

    this.floorPlan.updateTransform(
        this.activeBuilding.position,
        dimensions,
        this.activeBuilding.rotation
    );
}

    dispose() {
        this.enabled = false;

        if (this.pointerObserver) {
            this.scene.onPointerObservable.remove(
                this.pointerObserver
            );
        }

        this.floorPlan.dispose();
        this.editorCamera.dispose();
	this.floorPlanHeightControl?.dispose();

        this.container?.remove();
        this.launcher?.remove();

        this.container = null;
        this.launcher = null;

	this.unsubscribeMode?.();
	this.previewRenderer?.dispose();
    }

worldToBuildingLocal(position) {
    const building = this.activeBuilding;
    const dx = position.x - building.position.x;
    const dz = position.z - building.position.z;

    const angle = building.rotation;
    const cos = Math.cos(angle);
    const sin = Math.sin(angle);

    return {
        x: dx * cos + dz * sin,
        y: position.y - building.position.y,
        z: -dx * sin + dz * cos
    };
}


ensureBuildingForFloorPlan() {
    if (this.activeBuilding) return;

    const element = id =>
        this.container.querySelector(`#${id}`);

    this.activeBuilding = this.system.createBuilding({
        name: "Floor Plan Building",
        position: {
            x: Number(element("position-x").value) || 0,
            y: Number(element("position-y").value) || 0,
            z: Number(element("position-z").value) || 0
        },
        rotation:
            Number(element("position-rotation").value || 0) *
            Math.PI / 180
    });
	this.activeBuilding.metadata ??= {};

this.activeBuilding.metadata.editorPreview = true;

    this.placement.select(this.activeBuilding);
    this.syncPositionInputs();
}

async buildFloorFromPlan() {
    try {
        if (!this.floorPlan.mesh) {
            throw new Error(
                "Import a floor-plan image first."
            );
        }

        this.ensureBuildingForFloorPlan();

        const element = id =>
            this.container.querySelector(`#${id}`);

        const imageData = await this.floorPlan.readPixels();

        const result = this.floorPlanBuilder.build(
            this.activeBuilding,
            imageData,
            {
                width: Number(element("floor-width").value),
                depth: Number(element("floor-depth").value),
                floor: 0
            }
        );

        this.system.rebuild(this.activeBuilding);
	this.activeBuilding.metadata ??= {};

this.activeBuilding.metadata.editorPreview = true;

const outlineCount =
    this.previewRenderer.show(this.activeBuilding);

this.placeButton.disabled = false;

this.setStatus(
    `Preview ready: ${result.walls} walls, ` +
    `${result.doors} doors, ${result.windows} windows. ` +
    `${outlineCount} highlighted components. ` +
    `Adjust dimensions or place the building.`
);


        return result;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("[BuildingEditor] Floor construction:", error);
        this.setStatus(error.message);
        return null;
    }
}


installFloorPlanWorkflowControls() {
    const buildButton =
        this.container.querySelector("#build-floor");

    const placeButton = document.createElement("button");

    placeButton.id = "place-building";
    placeButton.textContent =
        "PLACE BUILDING & START NEXT";

    placeButton.disabled = true;

    placeButton.style.width = "100%";
    placeButton.style.marginTop = "8px";

    buildButton.after(placeButton);

    placeButton.onclick = () => {
        this.placeCurrentBuilding();
    };

    this.placeButton = placeButton;

    const depthInput =
        this.container.querySelector("#floor-depth");

    const aspectLabel = document.createElement("label");

    aspectLabel.style.display = "block";
    aspectLabel.style.margin = "8px 0";

    this.aspectLock = document.createElement("input");
    this.aspectLock.type = "checkbox";
    this.aspectLock.checked = true;

    aspectLabel.append(
        this.aspectLock,
        document.createTextNode(
            " Maintain image aspect ratio"
        )
    );

    depthInput.parentElement.after(aspectLabel);

    const updateDimensions = source => {
        const width = Number(
            this.container.querySelector("#floor-width").value
        );

        let depth = Number(depthInput.value);

        if (
            this.aspectLock.checked &&
            this.floorPlan.image
        ) {
            if (source === "depth") {
                this.aspectLock.checked = false;
            } else {
                depth = width *
                    this.floorPlan.image.naturalHeight /
                    this.floorPlan.image.naturalWidth;

                depthInput.value = depth.toFixed(2);
            }
        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(width) ||
            !Number.isFinite(depth) ||
            width <= 0 ||
            depth <= 0
        ) {
            return;
        }

        this.updateFloorPlanPosition({
            width,
            depth
        });

        if (
            this.activeBuilding?.metadata?.editorPreview
        ) {
            this.buildFloorFromPlan();
        }
    };

    this.container.querySelector(
        "#floor-width"
    ).onchange = () => updateDimensions("width");

    depthInput.onchange = () => updateDimensions("depth");

    this.aspectLock.onchange = () => {
        updateDimensions("width");
    };
}


placeCurrentBuilding() {
    const building = this.activeBuilding;

    if (
        !building ||
        building.metadata?.editorPreview !== true
    ) {
        this.setStatus(
            "There is no provisional building to place."
        );
        return false;
    }

    this.previewRenderer.commit(building);

    building.metadata.editorPreview = false;

    this.system.refresh();

    // Release the current selection so the next imported
    // floor plan creates a new independent building.
    this.activeBuilding = null;
    this.selectedRoom = null;
    this.placement.select(null);

    this.placeButton.disabled = true;

    this.setStatus(
        "Building placed successfully. " +
        "Import another floor plan to construct the next building."
    );

    return true;
}


}

export { BuildingEditor };
```

src/editor/building-preview-renderer.js

```
/**
 * BuildingPreviewRenderer
 *
 * Draws temporary architectural outlines without modifying
 * authoritative building geometry or shared render materials.
 */
class BuildingPreviewRenderer {
    constructor(scene, geometryRenderer) {
        if (!scene || !geometryRenderer) {
            throw new Error(
                "Preview renderer requires scene and GeometryRenderer."
            );
        }

        this.scene = scene;
        this.geometryRenderer = geometryRenderer;
        this.lines = [];
        this.buildingId = null;

        this.color = new BABYLON.Color3(
            0.15, 1.0, 0.75
        );
    }

    createComponentLines(component, building) {
        const p = component.position;
        const d = component.dimensions;

        if (
            !p || !d ||
            ![
                p.x, p.y, p.z,
                d.x, d.y, d.z,
                building.position.x,
                building.position.y,
                building.position.z
            ].every(Number.isFinite) ||
            d.x <= 0 || d.y <= 0 || d.z <= 0
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const angle = building.rotation ?? 0;
        const cos = Math.cos(angle);
        const sin = Math.sin(angle);

        const worldPosition = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            building.position.x + p.x * cos - p.z * sin,
            building.position.y + p.y,
            building.position.z + p.x * sin + p.z * cos
        );

        const rotation = BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationAxis(
            BABYLON.Axis.Y,
            angle + (component.rotation ?? 0)
        );

        const matrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
            new BABYLON.Vector3(d.x, d.y, d.z),
            rotation,
            worldPosition
        );

        const projected =
            this.geometryRenderer.transformInstanceMatrix(matrix);

        if (!projected) return null;

        const corners = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            const local = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                (i & 1) ? 0.5 : -0.5,
                (i & 2) ? 0.5 : -0.5,
                (i & 4) ? 0.5 : -0.5
            );

            corners.push(
                BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(
                    local,
                    projected
                )
            );
        }

        const edges = [
            [0, 1], [0, 2], [0, 4],
            [1, 3], [1, 5],
            [2, 3], [2, 6],
            [3, 7], [4, 5],
            [4, 6], [5, 7], [6, 7]
        ];

        return edges.map(([a, b]) => [
            corners[a],
            corners[b]
        ]);
    }

    show(building) {
        this.clear();

        if (!building?.generatedGeometry) {
            throw new Error(
                "Building has no generated geometry to preview."
            );
        }

        this.buildingId = building.id;

        for (const component of building.generatedGeometry) {
            const edges = this.createComponentLines(
                component,
                building
            );

            if (!edges) continue;

            const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateLineSystem(
                "building-preview-outline",
                { lines: edges },
                this.scene
            );

            mesh.color = this.color;
            mesh.alpha = 1;
            mesh.isPickable = false;
            mesh.renderingGroupId = 2;

            mesh.metadata = {
                type: "building-preview-outline",
                buildingId: building.id
            };

            this.lines.push(mesh);
        }

        return this.lines.length;
    }

    clear() {
        for (const mesh of this.lines) {
            mesh.dispose();
        }

        this.lines.length = 0;
        this.buildingId = null;
    }

    commit(building) {
        if (
            building &&
            building.id !== this.buildingId
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Cannot commit a different preview building."
            );
        }

        this.clear();
    }

    dispose() {
        this.clear();
    }
}

export { BuildingPreviewRenderer };
```

src/editor/floor-plan-builder.js

```
class FloorPlanBuilder {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.wallHeight = options.wallHeight ?? 4;
        this.wallThickness = options.wallThickness ?? 0.35;
        this.doorHeight = options.doorHeight ?? 2.1;
        this.windowHeight = options.windowHeight ?? 1.2;
        this.windowSill = options.windowSill ?? 0.9;
        this.minimumSegmentPixels =
            options.minimumSegmentPixels ?? 5;
    }

    setWallHeight(value) {
        if (
            !Number.isFinite(value) ||
            value < 2.4 ||
            value > 1000
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Wall height must be between 2.4 and 1000."
            );
        }

        this.wallHeight = value;
        return value;
    }

    setWallThickness(value) {
        if (
            !Number.isFinite(value) ||
            value < 0.1 ||
            value > 1000
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Wall thickness must be between 0.1 and 1000."
            );
        }

        this.wallThickness = value;
        return value;
    }

    classify(r, g, b, a) {
        if (a < 128) return null;

        if (r < 75 && g < 75 && b < 75) {
            return "wall";
        }

        if (r > 150 && g < 110 && b < 110) {
            return "door";
        }

        if (g > 130 && r < 120 && b < 130) {
            return "window";
        }

        return null;
    }

    extractSegments(imageData) {
        const { data, width, height } = imageData;

        if (
            !data ||
            !Number.isInteger(width) ||
            !Number.isInteger(height) ||
            width <= 0 ||
            height <= 0 ||
            data.length < width * height * 4
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid floor-plan image data.");
        }

        const active = new Map();
        const completed = [];

        for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
            const current = new Set();
            let x = 0;

            while (x < width) {
                const index = (y * width + x) * 4;

                const kind = this.classify(
                    data[index],
                    data[index + 1],
                    data[index + 2],
                    data[index + 3]
                );

                if (!kind) {
                    x++;
                    continue;
                }

                const start = x;

                while (x < width) {
                    const i = (y * width + x) * 4;

                    if (
                        this.classify(
                            data[i],
                            data[i + 1],
                            data[i + 2],
                            data[i + 3]
                        ) !== kind
                    ) {
                        break;
                    }

                    x++;
                }

                const end = x - 1;
                const key = `${kind}:${start}:${end}`;

                current.add(key);

                const previous = active.get(key);

                if (previous) {
                    previous.maxY = y;
                } else {
                    active.set(key, {
                        kind,
                        minX: start,
                        maxX: end,
                        minY: y,
                        maxY: y
                    });
                }
            }

            for (const [key, segment] of active) {
                if (!current.has(key)) {
                    completed.push(segment);
                    active.delete(key);
                }
            }
        }

        completed.push(...active.values());

        return completed.filter(segment => {
            const w = segment.maxX - segment.minX + 1;
            const h = segment.maxY - segment.minY + 1;

            return Math.max(w, h) >= this.minimumSegmentPixels;
        });
    }

    build(building, imageData, options = {}) {
        if (
            !building ||
            typeof building.addComponent !== "function" ||
            !Array.isArray(building.components)
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid building model.");
        }

        const width = options.width ?? 20;
        const depth = options.depth ?? 20;
        const floorIndex = options.floor ?? 0;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(width) ||
            !Number.isFinite(depth) ||
            width <= 0 ||
            depth <= 0 ||
            !Number.isInteger(floorIndex) ||
            floorIndex < 0
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid floor dimensions.");
        }

        const segments = this.extractSegments(imageData);

        if (!segments.length) {
            throw new Error(
                "No architectural lines detected."
            );
        }

        const scaleX = width / imageData.width;
        const scaleZ = depth / imageData.height;

        const floorY = floorIndex * this.wallHeight;
        const prefix = `floor-plan-${floorIndex}-`;

        const generated = [];

        const counts = {
            walls: 0,
            doors: 0,
            windows: 0,
            floors: 0
        };

        const add = (
            kind,
            type,
            position,
            dimensions,
            material
        ) => {
            generated.push({
                kind,
                type: prefix + type,
                position,
                dimensions,
                material
            });
        };

        add(
            "floors",
            "floor",
            { x: 0, y: floorY - 0.075, z: 0 },
            { x: width, y: 0.15, z: depth },
            "floor"
        );

        counts.floors++;

        for (const segment of segments) {
            const pixelWidth =
                segment.maxX - segment.minX + 1;

            const pixelHeight =
                segment.maxY - segment.minY + 1;

            const horizontal = pixelWidth >= pixelHeight;

            const centerPixelX =
                (segment.minX + segment.maxX + 1) * 0.5;

            const centerPixelY =
                (segment.minY + segment.maxY + 1) * 0.5;

            const x =
                (centerPixelX / imageData.width - 0.5) * width;

            const z =
                (centerPixelY / imageData.height - 0.5) * depth;

            const length = horizontal
                ? pixelWidth * scaleX
                : pixelHeight * scaleZ;

            const detectedThickness = Math.max(
                0.08,
                Math.min(
                    0.35,
                    horizontal
                        ? pixelHeight * scaleZ
                        : pixelWidth * scaleX
                )
            );

            const thickness = segment.kind === "wall"
                ? this.wallThickness
                : detectedThickness;

            const dimensions = horizontal
                ? { x: length, z: thickness }
                : { x: thickness, z: length };

            if (segment.kind === "wall") {
                add(
                    "walls",
                    "wall",
                    {
                        x,
                        y: floorY + this.wallHeight * 0.5,
                        z
                    },
                    {
                        ...dimensions,
                        y: this.wallHeight
                    },
                    "wall"
                );

                counts.walls++;
                continue;
            }

            if (segment.kind === "door") {
                add(
                    "doors",
                    "door",
                    {
                        x,
                        y: floorY + this.doorHeight * 0.5,
                        z
                    },
                    {
                        ...dimensions,
                        y: this.doorHeight
                    },
                    "wood"
                );

                counts.doors++;
                continue;
            }

            if (segment.kind === "window") {
                const windowDepth = Math.max(
                    0.035,
                    detectedThickness * 0.25
                );

                add(
                    "glazing",
                    "window",
                    {
                        x,
                        y: floorY + this.windowSill +
                            this.windowHeight * 0.5,
                        z
                    },
                    horizontal
                        ? {
                            x: length,
                            y: this.windowHeight,
                            z: windowDepth
                        }
                        : {
                            x: windowDepth,
                            y: this.windowHeight,
                            z: length
                        },
                    "glass"
                );

                counts.windows++;
            }
        }

        const legacyTypes = new Set([
            "floor-plan-floor",
            "floor-plan-wall",
            "floor-plan-door",
            "floor-plan-window"
        ]);

        building.components = building.components.filter(
            component =>
                !legacyTypes.has(component.type) &&
                !component.type?.startsWith(prefix)
        );

        for (const component of generated) {
            building.addComponent(component);
        }

        return {
            ...counts,
            floor: floorIndex,
            totalSegments: segments.length,
            wallHeight: this.wallHeight,
            wallThickness: this.wallThickness,
            generatedComponents: generated.length
        };
    }
}

export { FloorPlanBuilder };

```

src/editor/floor-plan-reference.js
_______
```
/**
 * FloorPlanReference
 *
 * Manages an independently positioned architectural
 * reference image in the Somnium building editor.
 *
 * Authoritative coordinates remain in simulation space.
 * SpatialTransform handles rendering coordinates.
 *
 * Babylon CreateGround uses local X-Z dimensions.
 */
class FloorPlanReference {
    constructor(scene, spatialTransform) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.mesh = null;
        this.material = null;
        this.texture = null;
        this.objectURL = null;
        this.image = null;

        this.width = 20;
        this.depth = 20;

        this.opacity = 0.65;
        this.visible = true;

        this.position = {
            x: 0,
            y: 0,
            z: 0
        };

        this.altitudeOffset = 0;
        this.rotation = 0;
        this.loaded = false;
    }

    validatePosition(position) {
        if (
            !position ||
            !Number.isFinite(position.x) ||
            !Number.isFinite(position.y) ||
            !Number.isFinite(position.z)
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid floor-plan position."
            );
        }
    }

    async load(file) {
        if (
            !file ||
            typeof file.type !== "string" ||
            !file.type.startsWith("image/")
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Please select a valid image file."
            );
        }

        this.remove();

        try {
            this.objectURL = URL.createObjectURL(file);
            this.image = new Image();

            await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                this.image.onload = resolve;
                this.image.onerror = () => reject(
                    new Error(
                        "Could not load floor-plan image."
                    )
                );

                this.image.src = this.objectURL;
            });

            if (
                !this.image.naturalWidth ||
                !this.image.naturalHeight
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Floor-plan image has invalid dimensions."
                );
            }

            this.texture = new BABYLON.Texture(
                this.objectURL,
                this.scene,
                false,
                false,
                BABYLON.Texture.TRILINEAR_SAMPLINGMODE
            );

            await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                if (this.texture.isReady()) {
                    resolve();
                    return;
                }

                this.texture.onLoadObservable.addOnce(resolve);

                this.texture.onError = message => {
                    reject(
                        new Error(
                            message ||
                            "Floor-plan texture failed."
                        )
                    );
                };
            });

            this.material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
                "building-floor-plan-material",
                this.scene
            );

            this.material.diffuseTexture = this.texture;
            this.material.emissiveTexture = this.texture;
            this.material.disableLighting = true;
            this.material.backFaceCulling = false;
            this.material.alpha = this.opacity;
            this.material.transparencyMode =
                BABYLON.Material.MATERIAL_ALPHABLEND;

            this.mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateGround(
                "building-floor-plan",
                {
                    width: 1,
                    height: 1,
                    subdivisions: 1
                },
                this.scene
            );

            this.mesh.material = this.material;
            this.mesh.isPickable = false;
            this.mesh.receiveShadows = false;
            this.mesh.renderingGroupId = 1;

            this.mesh.metadata = {
                type: "floor-plan-reference",
                reference: true
            };

            this.loaded = true;

            this.setVisible(this.visible);
            this.updateTransform();

            return this;

        } catch (error) {
            this.remove();
            throw error;
        }
    }

    async readPixels() {
        if (!this.image || !this.loaded) {
            throw new Error(
                "Load a floor-plan image first."
            );
        }

        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");

        canvas.width = this.image.naturalWidth;
        canvas.height = this.image.naturalHeight;

        const context = canvas.getContext("2d", {
            willReadFrequently: true
        });

        if (!context) {
            throw new Error(
                "Image processing canvas unavailable."
            );
        }

        context.drawImage(this.image, 0, 0);

        return context.getImageData(
            0,
            0,
            canvas.width,
            canvas.height
        );
    }

    setPosition(position) {
        this.validatePosition(position);

        this.position = {
            x: position.x,
            y: position.y,
            z: position.z
        };

        this.updateTransform();
    }

    setAltitudeOffset(offset) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(offset)) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid altitude offset."
            );
        }

        this.altitudeOffset = offset;
        this.updateTransform();
    }

    setDimensions(width, depth) {
        if (
            !Number.isFinite(width) ||
            !Number.isFinite(depth) ||
            width <= 0 ||
            depth <= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid floor-plan dimensions."
            );
        }

        this.width = width;
        this.depth = depth;

        this.updateTransform();
    }

    setRotation(rotation) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(rotation)) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid floor-plan rotation."
            );
        }

        this.rotation = rotation;
        this.updateTransform();
    }

    updateTransform(
        position = this.position,
        dimensions = {},
        rotation = this.rotation
    ) {
        if (!this.mesh) return;

        if (!this.transform) {
            throw new Error(
                "SpatialTransform is unavailable."
            );
        }

        if (position) {
            this.validatePosition(position);

            this.position = {
                x: position.x,
                y: position.y,
                z: position.z
            };
        }

        const width = dimensions.width ?? this.width;
        const depth = dimensions.depth ?? this.depth;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(width) ||
            !Number.isFinite(depth) ||
            width <= 0 ||
            depth <= 0 ||
            !Number.isFinite(rotation)
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid floor-plan transformation."
            );
        }

        this.width = width;
        this.depth = depth;
        this.rotation = rotation;

        const worldPosition = {
            x: this.position.x,
            y: this.position.y + this.altitudeOffset,
            z: this.position.z
        };

        const rendered = this.transform.worldToRender(
            worldPosition
        );

        const cos = Math.cos(rotation);
        const sin = Math.sin(rotation);

        const xAxis = this.transform.transformDirection(
            worldPosition,
            {
                x: cos,
                y: 0,
                z: -sin
            }
        );

        const zAxis = this.transform.transformDirection(
            worldPosition,
            {
                x: sin,
                y: 0,
                z: cos
            }
        );

        const xScale = Math.hypot(
            xAxis.x,
            xAxis.y,
            xAxis.z
        );

        const zScale = Math.hypot(
            zAxis.x,
            zAxis.y,
            zAxis.z
        );

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(xScale) ||
            !Number.isFinite(zScale) ||
            xScale <= 0 ||
            zScale <= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid projected floor-plan dimensions."
            );
        }

        this.mesh.position.set(
            rendered.x,
            rendered.y + 0.08,
            rendered.z
        );

        // CreateGround occupies local X-Z, not X-Y.
        this.mesh.scaling.set(
            width * xScale,
            1,
            depth * zScale
        );

        // Align the ground plane to its projected X axis.
        const projectedYaw = Math.atan2(
            -xAxis.z,
            xAxis.x
        );

        this.mesh.rotation.set(
            0,
            projectedYaw,
            0
        );

        this.mesh.isVisible = this.visible;
        this.mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
    }

    setOpacity(value) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid floor-plan opacity."
            );
        }

        this.opacity = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(1, value)
        );

        if (this.material) {
            this.material.alpha = this.opacity;
        }
    }

    setVisible(value) {
        this.visible = Boolean(value);

        if (this.mesh) {
            this.mesh.setEnabled(this.visible);
        }
    }

    toggleVisibility() {
        this.setVisible(!this.visible);
        return this.visible;
    }

    remove() {
        this.mesh?.dispose();
        this.material?.dispose();
        this.texture?.dispose();

        this.mesh = null;
        this.material = null;
        this.texture = null;

        if (this.objectURL) {
            URL.revokeObjectURL(this.objectURL);
            this.objectURL = null;
        }

        this.image = null;
        this.loaded = false;
    }

    dispose() {
        this.remove();
    }
}

export { FloorPlanReference };
export default FloorPlanReference;
```

src/environment/environment.js
_______
```
class Environment {

    constructor(lightDirection) {

        this.sunDirection = lightDirection
            .negate()
            .normalize();

        // Environmental lighting configuration
        this.baseLight = 1.0;

        this.minimumLight = 0.12;

        // How strongly surrounding trees reduce light
        this.shadingStrength = 0.85;

        // Spatial grid for efficient canopy sampling
        this.canopyCellSize = 10;

        this.canopyGrid = new Map();
    }


    getCellKey(x, z) {

        const cx = Math.floor(x / this.canopyCellSize);
        const cz = Math.floor(z / this.canopyCellSize);

        return `${cx},${cz}`;
    }


    rebuildCanopyGrid(trees) {

        this.canopyGrid.clear();

        for (const tree of trees) {

            if (!tree.alive) continue;

            const age = tree.ageFactor;

            if (age < 0.1) continue;

            const height =
                tree.species.trunkLength *
                age *
                tree.genome.maxHeight;

            const radius =
                height *
                tree.species.canopy.width *
                tree.genome.canopyWidth;

            const key = this.getCellKey(
                tree.position.x,
                tree.position.z
            );

            if (!this.canopyGrid.has(key)) {
                this.canopyGrid.set(key, []);
            }

            this.canopyGrid.get(key).push({
                x: tree.position.x,
                z: tree.position.z,
                radius,
                height
            });

        }

    }

sampleLight(position) {

    const key = this.getCellKey(
        position.x,
        position.z
    );

    const cell = this.canopyGrid.get(key) || [];

    let shade = 0;

    for (const canopy of cell) {

        // Ignore canopies that are below the sample
        if (canopy.height <= position.y) continue;

        const dx = position.x - canopy.x;
        const dz = position.z - canopy.z;

        const distanceSquared =
            dx * dx + dz * dz;

        const radiusSquared =
            canopy.radius * canopy.radius;

        if (distanceSquared < radiusSquared) {

            const influence =
                1 - Math.sqrt(
                    distanceSquared / radiusSquared
                );

            shade += influence *
                this.shadingStrength *
                Math.min(1, canopy.height / 5);

        }

    }

    // Start with generous available light
    const intensity = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        1.0 - shade * 0.35,
        0.15,
        1.0
    );

    return {
        direction: this.sunDirection,
        intensity
    };

}

}
export { Environment }
```

src/environment/moisture-field.js
_______
```

class MoistureField {
    constructor(terrain, options = {}) {
        this.terrain = terrain;

        this.seaLevel = options.seaLevel ?? -100;

        this.baseMoisture = options.baseMoisture ?? 0.08;
        this.oceanMoisture = options.oceanMoisture ?? 0.82;

        this.coastalRadius = options.coastalRadius ?? 24000;
        this.atmosphericScale = options.atmosphericScale ?? 7000;

        this.cloudContribution = options.cloudContribution ?? 0.25;
    }

    clamp(value) {
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value));
    }

    getSurfaceHeight(x, z) {
        const height = this.terrain.sampleHeight(x, z);

        if (height === null || !Number.isFinite(height)) {
            return null;
        }

        return height;
    }

    getOceanInfluence(x, z) {
        const centerHeight = this.getSurfaceHeight(x, z);

        if (centerHeight === null) {
            return 0;
        }

        // Directly over the ocean.
        if (centerHeight <= this.seaLevel) {
            return 1;
        }

        // Sample the surrounding terrain to estimate
        // proximity to ocean water.
        const directions = [
            [1, 0],
            [-1, 0],
            [0, 1],
            [0, -1],
            [0.7071, 0.7071],
            [-0.7071, 0.7071],
            [0.7071, -0.7071],
            [-0.7071, -0.7071]
        ];

        let influence = 0;

        const distances = [
            this.coastalRadius * 0.4,
            this.coastalRadius
        ];

        for (let distanceIndex = 0;
             distanceIndex < distances.length;
             distanceIndex++) {

            const distance = distances[distanceIndex];

            const weight = distanceIndex === 0 ? 0.65 : 0.35;

            let oceanSamples = 0;

            for (const direction of directions) {
                const height = this.getSurfaceHeight(
                    x + direction[0] * distance,
                    z + direction[1] * distance
                );

                if (height !== null && height <= this.seaLevel) {
                    oceanSamples++;
                }
            }

            influence += (
                oceanSamples / directions.length
            ) * weight;
        }

        return this.clamp(influence);
    }

    sample(position, cloudMoisture = 0) {
        if (!position) {
            return this.baseMoisture;
        }

        const surfaceHeight = this.getSurfaceHeight(
            position.x,
            position.z
        );

        if (surfaceHeight === null) {
            return this.baseMoisture;
        }

        const referenceHeight = Math.max(
            surfaceHeight,
            this.seaLevel
        );

        const altitude = Math.max(
            0,
            position.y - referenceHeight
        );

        // Moisture availability decreases with altitude.
        const altitudeFactor = Math.exp(
            -altitude / this.atmosphericScale
        );

        const oceanInfluence = this.getOceanInfluence(
            position.x,
            position.z
        );

        const oceanContribution =
            oceanInfluence *
            this.oceanMoisture *
            altitudeFactor;

        const cloudContribution =
            this.clamp(cloudMoisture) *
            this.cloudContribution;

        return this.clamp(
            this.baseMoisture +
            oceanContribution +
            cloudContribution
        );
    }

    sampleSurface(x, z) {
        const position = {
            x,
            y: this.seaLevel,
            z
        };

        return this.sample(position);
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            seaLevel: this.seaLevel,
            baseMoisture: this.baseMoisture,
            oceanMoisture: this.oceanMoisture,
            coastalRadius: this.coastalRadius,
            atmosphericScale: this.atmosphericScale
        };
    }
}

export { MoistureField };

```

src/environment/height-map-terrain.js

```
export class HeightmapTerrain {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.url = options.url ??
            "./assets/0_somnium_map_topographic.png";

        this.worldSize = options.worldSize ?? 1000000;

        this.minHeight = options.minHeight ?? -2000;
        this.maxHeight = options.maxHeight ?? 1000;

        this.heightChannel = options.heightChannel ?? "average";

        this.min = {
            x: -this.worldSize / 2,
            y: this.minHeight,
            z: -this.worldSize / 2
        };

        this.max = {
            x: this.worldSize / 2,
            y: this.maxHeight,
            z: this.worldSize / 2
        };

        this.width = 0;
        this.height = 0;
        this.pixels = null;
        this.ready = false;

        this.diagnostics = null;
    }

    async load() {
        const image = new Image();

        image.crossOrigin = "anonymous";

        await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            image.onload = resolve;

            image.onerror = () => reject(
                new Error(
                    `Unable to load heightmap: ${this.url}`
                )
            );

            image.src = this.url;
        });

        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");

        canvas.width = image.naturalWidth;
        canvas.height = image.naturalHeight;

        const context = canvas.getContext(
            "2d",
            { willReadFrequently: true }
        );

        if (!context) {
            throw new Error(
                "Unable to initialize heightmap canvas."
            );
        }

        context.drawImage(image, 0, 0);

        this.width = canvas.width;
        this.height = canvas.height;

        this.pixels = context.getImageData(
            0,
            0,
            this.width,
            this.height
        ).data;

        this.ready = true;

        this.diagnostics = this.getDiagnostics(32);

        console.log(
            "[HeightmapTerrain] Loaded:",
            this.getDiagnostics()
        );

        return this;
    }

    getNormalizedCoordinates(x, z) {
        return {
            u: (x - this.min.x) / this.worldSize,
            v: (z - this.min.z) / this.worldSize
        };
    }

    getPixelValue(x, z) {
        x = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(this.width - 1, x)
        );

        z = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(this.height - 1, z)
        );

        const index = (
            Math.floor(z) * this.width +
            Math.floor(x)
        ) * 4;

        const r = this.pixels[index];
        const g = this.pixels[index + 1];
        const b = this.pixels[index + 2];

        switch (this.heightChannel) {
            case "red":
                return r / 255;

            case "green":
                return g / 255;

            case "blue":
                return b / 255;

            case "luminance":
                return (
                    0.2126 * r +
                    0.7152 * g +
                    0.0722 * b
                ) / 255;

            case "average":
            default:
                return (r + g + b) / 765;
        }
    }

    getHeightPixel(x, z) {
        return this.getPixelValue(x, z);
    }

    sampleHeight(x, z) {
        if (!this.ready) {
            throw new Error("Heightmap not loaded.");
        }

        if (
            x < this.min.x ||
            x > this.max.x ||
            z < this.min.z ||
            z > this.max.z
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const { u, v } =
            this.getNormalizedCoordinates(x, z);

        const px = u * (this.width - 1);
        const pz = v * (this.height - 1);

        const x0 = Math.floor(px);
        const z0 = Math.floor(pz);

        const x1 = Math.min(
            x0 + 1,
            this.width - 1
        );

        const z1 = Math.min(
            z0 + 1,
            this.height - 1
        );

        const tx = px - x0;
        const tz = pz - z0;

        const a = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.getPixelValue(x0, z0),
            this.getPixelValue(x1, z0),
            tx
        );

        const b = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.getPixelValue(x0, z1),
            this.getPixelValue(x1, z1),
            tx
        );

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.minHeight,
            this.maxHeight,
            BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(a, b, tz)
        );
    }

    sampleAt(x, z) {
        const y = this.sampleHeight(x, z);

        if (y === null) return null;

        return new BABYLON.Vector3(x, y, z);
    }

    sample() {
        return this.sampleAt(
            BABYLON.Scalar.RandomRange(
                this.min.x,
                this.max.x
            ),
            BABYLON.Scalar.RandomRange(
                this.min.z,
                this.max.z
            )
        );
    }

    sampleNear(center, radius) {
        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

        const distance =
            radius * Math.sqrt(Math.random());

        return this.sampleAt(
            center.x + Math.cos(angle) * distance,
            center.z + Math.sin(angle) * distance
        );
    }

    sampleSlopeAt(x, z, spacing = 4) {
        const left = this.sampleHeight(x - spacing, z);
        const right = this.sampleHeight(x + spacing, z);

        const back = this.sampleHeight(x, z - spacing);
        const front = this.sampleHeight(x, z + spacing);

        if (
            [left, right, back, front].some(
                value => value === null
            )
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const dx = (right - left) / (2 * spacing);
        const dz = (front - back) / (2 * spacing);

        const magnitude = Math.hypot(dx, dz);

        const angle = Math.atan(magnitude);

        return {
            gradientX: dx,
            gradientZ: dz,
            magnitude,
            angle,
            degrees: angle * 180 / Math.PI,

            normal: new BABYLON.Vector3(
                -dx, 1, -dz
            ).normalize()
        };
    }

    sampleProfileAt(x, z, spacing = 4) {
        const position = this.sampleAt(x, z);

        if (!position) return null;

        const slope = this.sampleSlopeAt(
            x, z, spacing
        );

        if (!slope) return null;

        return {
            position,

            elevation: BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                (position.y - this.minHeight) /
                (this.maxHeight - this.minHeight),
                0, 1
            ),

            slope: slope.magnitude,
            slopeDegrees: slope.degrees,
            normal: slope.normal
        };
    }

    getDiagnostics(gridSize = 32) {
        if (!this.ready) {
            return {
                ready: false
            };
        }

        let minimum = Infinity;
        let maximum = -Infinity;
        let sum = 0;
        let count = 0;

        const samples = [];

        for (let z = 0; z <= gridSize; z++) {
            for (let x = 0; x <= gridSize; x++) {
                const wx = this.min.x +
                    (x / gridSize) * this.worldSize;

                const wz = this.min.z +
                    (z / gridSize) * this.worldSize;

                const height = this.sampleHeight(wx, wz);

                minimum = Math.min(minimum, height);
                maximum = Math.max(maximum, height);

                sum += height;
                count++;

                if (
                    x === 0 ||
                    x === Math.floor(gridSize / 2) ||
                    x === gridSize
                ) {
                    if (
                        z === 0 ||
                        z === Math.floor(gridSize / 2) ||
                        z === gridSize
                    ) {
                        samples.push({
                            x: wx,
                            z: wz,
                            height
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return {
            ready: true,

            imageWidth: this.width,
            imageHeight: this.height,

            worldSize: this.worldSize,

            heightChannel: this.heightChannel,

            configuredHeightRange: {
                min: this.minHeight,
                max: this.maxHeight
            },

            sampledHeightRange: {
                min: minimum,
                max: maximum,
                variation: maximum - minimum
            },

            averageHeight: sum / count,

            sampleCount: count,
            samples
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        this.pixels = null;
        this.ready = false;
    }
}

```

src/environment/heightmap-terrain.js

```
export class HeightmapTerrain {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.url = options.url ??
            "./assets/0_somnium_map_topographic.png";

        this.worldSize = options.worldSize ?? 1000000;

        this.minHeight = options.minHeight ?? -2000;
        this.maxHeight = options.maxHeight ?? 1000;

        this.heightChannel = options.heightChannel ?? "average";

        this.min = {
            x: -this.worldSize / 2,
            y: this.minHeight,
            z: -this.worldSize / 2
        };

        this.max = {
            x: this.worldSize / 2,
            y: this.maxHeight,
            z: this.worldSize / 2
        };

        this.width = 0;
        this.height = 0;
        this.pixels = null;

        this.ready = false;
        this.diagnostics = null;
    }

    async load() {
        const image = new Image();

        image.crossOrigin = "anonymous";

        await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            image.onload = resolve;

            image.onerror = () => {
                reject(
                    new Error(
                        `Unable to load heightmap: ${this.url}`
                    )
                );
            };

            image.src = this.url;
        });

        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");

        canvas.width = image.naturalWidth;
        canvas.height = image.naturalHeight;

        const context = canvas.getContext("2d", {
            willReadFrequently: true
        });

        if (!context) {
            throw new Error(
                "Unable to initialize heightmap canvas."
            );
        }

        context.drawImage(image, 0, 0);

        this.width = canvas.width;
        this.height = canvas.height;

        this.pixels = context.getImageData(
            0,
            0,
            this.width,
            this.height
        ).data;

        this.ready = true;

        this.diagnostics = this.getDiagnostics(32);

        console.log(
            "[HeightmapTerrain] Loaded:",
            this.diagnostics
        );

        return this;
    }

    getNormalizedCoordinates(x, z) {
        return {
            u: (x - this.min.x) / this.worldSize,
            v: (z - this.min.z) / this.worldSize
        };
    }

    getPixelValue(x, z) {
        if (!this.pixels) {
            throw new Error("Heightmap pixels are unavailable.");
        }

        x = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(this.width - 1, Math.floor(x))
        );

        z = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(this.height - 1, Math.floor(z))
        );

        const index = (z * this.width + x) * 4;

        const r = this.pixels[index];
        const g = this.pixels[index + 1];
        const b = this.pixels[index + 2];

        switch (this.heightChannel) {
            case "red":
                return r / 255;

            case "green":
                return g / 255;

            case "blue":
                return b / 255;

            case "luminance":
                return (
                    0.2126 * r +
                    0.7152 * g +
                    0.0722 * b
                ) / 255;

            case "average":
            default:
                return (r + g + b) / 765;
        }
    }

    getHeightPixel(x, z) {
        return this.getPixelValue(x, z);
    }

    sampleHeight(x, z) {
        if (!this.ready) {
            throw new Error("Heightmap not loaded.");
        }

        if (
            x < this.min.x ||
            x > this.max.x ||
            z < this.min.z ||
            z > this.max.z
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const { u, v } =
            this.getNormalizedCoordinates(x, z);

        const px = u * (this.width - 1);
        const pz = v * (this.height - 1);

        const x0 = Math.floor(px);
        const z0 = Math.floor(pz);

        const x1 = Math.min(
            x0 + 1,
            this.width - 1
        );

        const z1 = Math.min(
            z0 + 1,
            this.height - 1
        );

        const tx = px - x0;
        const tz = pz - z0;

        const a = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.getPixelValue(x0, z0),
            this.getPixelValue(x1, z0),
            tx
        );

        const b = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.getPixelValue(x0, z1),
            this.getPixelValue(x1, z1),
            tx
        );

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
            this.minHeight,
            this.maxHeight,
            BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(a, b, tz)
        );
    }

    sampleAt(x, z) {
        const y = this.sampleHeight(x, z);

        if (y === null) return null;

        return new BABYLON.Vector3(x, y, z);
    }

    sample() {
        return this.sampleAt(
            BABYLON.Scalar.RandomRange(
                this.min.x,
                this.max.x
            ),
            BABYLON.Scalar.RandomRange(
                this.min.z,
                this.max.z
            )
        );
    }

    sampleNear(center, radius) {
        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

        const distance =
            radius * Math.sqrt(Math.random());

        return this.sampleAt(
            center.x + Math.cos(angle) * distance,
            center.z + Math.sin(angle) * distance
        );
    }

    sampleSlopeAt(x, z, spacing = 4) {
        const left = this.sampleHeight(x - spacing, z);
        const right = this.sampleHeight(x + spacing, z);

        const back = this.sampleHeight(x, z - spacing);
        const front = this.sampleHeight(x, z + spacing);

        if (
            [left, right, back, front].some(
                value => value === null
            )
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const dx = (right - left) / (2 * spacing);
        const dz = (front - back) / (2 * spacing);

        const magnitude = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
        const angle = Math.atan(magnitude);

        return {
            gradientX: dx,
            gradientZ: dz,
            magnitude,
            angle,
            degrees: angle * 180 / Math.PI,

            normal: new BABYLON.Vector3(
                -dx,
                1,
                -dz
            ).normalize()
        };
    }

    sampleProfileAt(x, z, spacing = 4) {
        const position = this.sampleAt(x, z);

        if (!position) return null;

        const slope = this.sampleSlopeAt(
            x,
            z,
            spacing
        );

        if (!slope) return null;

        return {
            position,

            elevation: BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                (position.y - this.minHeight) /
                (this.maxHeight - this.minHeight),
                0,
                1
            ),

            slope: slope.magnitude,
            slopeDegrees: slope.degrees,
            normal: slope.normal
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // HEIGHTMAP DIAGNOSTICS
    // ------------------------------------------------

    getDiagnostics(gridSize = 32) {
        if (!this.ready || !this.pixels) {
            return {
                ready: false,
                message: "Heightmap has not finished loading."
            };
        }

        gridSize = Math.max(
            1,
            Math.floor(gridSize)
        );

        let minimum = Infinity;
        let maximum = -Infinity;
        let sum = 0;
        let count = 0;

        const samples = [];

        for (let z = 0; z <= gridSize; z++) {
            for (let x = 0; x <= gridSize; x++) {
                const wx = this.min.x +
                    (x / gridSize) * this.worldSize;

                const wz = this.min.z +
                    (z / gridSize) * this.worldSize;

                const height = this.sampleHeight(
                    wx,
                    wz
                );

                minimum = Math.min(minimum, height);
                maximum = Math.max(maximum, height);

                sum += height;
                count++;

                if (
                    (
                        x === 0 ||
                        x === Math.floor(gridSize / 2) ||
                        x === gridSize
                    ) &&
                    (
                        z === 0 ||
                        z === Math.floor(gridSize / 2) ||
                        z === gridSize
                    )
                ) {
                    samples.push({
                        x: wx,
                        z: wz,
                        height
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        return {
            ready: true,

            image: {
                width: this.width,
                height: this.height
            },

            worldSize: this.worldSize,

            heightChannel: this.heightChannel,

            configuredHeightRange: {
                min: this.minHeight,
                max: this.maxHeight
            },

            sampledHeightRange: {
                min: minimum,
                max: maximum,
                variation: maximum - minimum
            },

            averageHeight: sum / count,

            sampleCount: count,
            samples
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        this.pixels = null;
        this.ready = false;
        this.diagnostics = null;
    }

	sampleMapValue(x, z) {
    if (!this.ready || !this.pixels) {
        return null;
    }

    if (
        x < this.min.x ||
        x > this.max.x ||
        z < this.min.z ||
        z > this.max.z
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const { u, v } = this.getNormalizedCoordinates(x, z);

    const px = u * (this.width - 1);
    const pz = v * (this.height - 1);

    const x0 = Math.floor(px);
    const z0 = Math.floor(pz);

    const x1 = Math.min(x0 + 1, this.width - 1);
    const z1 = Math.min(z0 + 1, this.height - 1);

    const tx = px - x0;
    const tz = pz - z0;

    const a = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
        this.getPixelValue(x0, z0),
        this.getPixelValue(x1, z0),
        tx
    );

    const b = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
        this.getPixelValue(x0, z1),
        this.getPixelValue(x1, z1),
        tx
    );

    return BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(a, b, tz);
}

}
```

src/environment/celestial-system.js
