class CelestialSystem {
    constructor(scene, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;

        this.dayLength = options.dayLength ?? 900;
        this.timeScale = 1;
        this.elapsed = options.initialTime ?? 0;

        this.orbitDistance = options.orbitDistance ?? 45000;
        this.skyRadius = options.skyRadius ?? 90000;
        this.sunSize = options.sunSize ?? 1800;
        this.moonSize = options.moonSize ?? 1300;

        this.sunLight = options.sunLight ??
            new BABYLON.DirectionalLight(
                "celestialSunLight",
                new BABYLON.Vector3(0, -1, 0),
                scene
            );

        this.ambientLight = options.ambientLight ??
            new BABYLON.HemisphericLight(
                "celestialAmbientLight",
                new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1, 0),
                scene
            );

        this.moonLight = new BABYLON.DirectionalLight(
            "celestialMoonLight",
            new BABYLON.Vector3(0, -1, 0),
            scene
        );

        this.sunLight.diffuse =
            new BABYLON.Color3(1.0, 0.94, 0.78);

        this.sunLight.specular =
            new BABYLON.Color3(1.0, 0.95, 0.85);

        this.moonLight.diffuse =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.48, 0.58, 0.85);

        this.moonLight.specular =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.35, 0.42, 0.65);

        this.sunDirection = new BABYLON.Vector3();
        this.moonDirection = new BABYLON.Vector3();

        this.daylight = 0;
        this.twilight = 0;
        this.starVisibility = 1;

	// Initialize rendering resources before the first update.
this.createSky();

// Initialize the celestial simulation and shader uniforms.
this.update(0);

console.log("[CelestialSystem] Initialized.");

        console.log("[CelestialSystem] Initialized.");
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // ATMOSPHERIC SKY
    // -----------------------------------------

    createSky() {
        BABYLON.Effect.ShadersStore[
            "somniumSkyVertexShader"
        ] = `
            precision highp float;

            attribute vec3 position;

            uniform mat4 world;
            uniform mat4 worldViewProjection;

            varying vec3 vDirection;

            void main(void) {
                vec4 worldDirection =
                    world * vec4(position, 0.0);

                vDirection = normalize(worldDirection.xyz);

                gl_Position =
                    worldViewProjection * vec4(position, 1.0);
            }
        `;

        BABYLON.Effect.ShadersStore[
            "somniumSkyFragmentShader"
        ] = `
            precision highp float;

            varying vec3 vDirection;

            uniform vec3 sunDirection;
            uniform vec3 moonDirection;

            uniform float daylight;
            uniform float twilight;
            uniform float starVisibility;
            uniform float time;

            float hash31(vec3 p) {
                p = fract(p * 0.1031);
                p += dot(p, p.yzx + 33.33);
                return fract(
                    (p.x + p.y) * p.z
                );
            }

            void main(void) {
                vec3 direction = normalize(vDirection);

                float height = clamp(
                    direction.y,
                    -1.0,
                    1.0
                );

                // Daytime atmospheric colors.

                vec3 zenithDay = vec3(
                    0.16, 0.42, 0.78
                );

                vec3 horizonDay = vec3(
                    0.65, 0.79, 0.92
                );

                // Nighttime atmospheric colors.

                vec3 zenithNight = vec3(
                    0.003, 0.006, 0.022
                );

                vec3 horizonNight = vec3(
                    0.018, 0.027, 0.065
                );

                float horizon = pow(
                    1.0 - max(height, 0.0),
                    2.0
                );

                vec3 daySky = mix(
                    zenithDay,
                    horizonDay,
                    horizon
                );

                vec3 nightSky = mix(
                    zenithNight,
                    horizonNight,
                    horizon
                );

                // Blend day and night.

                vec3 sky = mix(
                    nightSky,
                    daySky,
                    daylight
                );

                // Sunrise and sunset palette.

                vec3 sunsetZenith = vec3(
                    0.24, 0.10, 0.25
                );

                vec3 sunsetHorizon = vec3(
                    1.0, 0.24, 0.065
                );

                vec3 sunset = mix(
                    sunsetZenith,
                    sunsetHorizon,
                    pow(horizon, 0.65)
                );

                float sunsetBand = exp(
                    -pow(height * 5.0, 2.0)
                );

                sky = mix(
                    sky,
                    sunset,
                    twilight * sunsetBand
                );

                // Sun disc and atmospheric glow.

                float sunDot = dot(
                    direction,
                    normalize(sunDirection)
                );

                float sunDisc = smoothstep(
                    0.99965,
                    0.99985,
                    sunDot
                );

                float sunGlow = pow(
                    max(sunDot, 0.0),
                    24.0
                );

                vec3 sunColor = mix(
                    vec3(1.0, 0.22, 0.06),
                    vec3(1.0, 0.91, 0.68),
                    daylight
                );

                sky += sunColor *
                    sunGlow *
                    (0.12 + twilight * 0.35);

                sky = mix(
                    sky,
                    sunColor,
                    sunDisc
                );

                // Procedural star field.

                vec3 starCell = floor(
                    direction * 550.0
                );

                float starRandom = hash31(starCell);

                float star = step(
                    0.9985,
                    starRandom
                );

                float starBrightness = mix(
                    0.45,
                    1.0,
                    hash31(starCell + 17.3)
                );

                float twinkle = 0.85 +
                    0.15 * sin(
                        time * 2.0 +
                        starRandom * 40.0
                    );

                float stars = star *
                    starBrightness *
                    twinkle *
                    starVisibility;

                vec3 starColor = mix(
                    vec3(0.55, 0.68, 1.0),
                    vec3(1.0, 0.96, 0.86),
                    starRandom
                );

                sky += starColor * stars;

                // Subtle lunar glow.

float moonDot = dot(
    direction,
    normalize(moonDirection)
);

float moonDisc = smoothstep(
    0.99972,
    0.99988,
    moonDot
);

float moonGlow = pow(
    max(moonDot, 0.0),
    18.0
);

vec3 moonColor = vec3(0.78, 0.83, 0.96);

sky += vec3(0.12, 0.16, 0.30)
    * moonGlow
    * starVisibility
    * 0.3;

sky = mix(
    sky,
    moonColor,
    moonDisc * starVisibility
);
                gl_FragColor = vec4(sky, 1.0);
            }
        `;

        this.skyMesh =
            BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
                "somniumAtmosphere",
                {
                    diameter: this.skyRadius * 2,
                    segments: 32
                },
                this.scene
            );

        this.skyMaterial =
            new BABYLON.ShaderMaterial(
                "somniumSkyMaterial",
                this.scene,
                {
                    vertex: "somniumSky",
                    fragment: "somniumSky"
                },
                {
                    attributes: ["position"],
                    uniforms: [
                        "world",
                        "worldViewProjection",
                        "sunDirection",
                        "moonDirection",
                        "daylight",
                        "twilight",
                        "starVisibility",
                        "time"
                    ]
                }
            );

        this.skyMaterial.backFaceCulling = false;
        this.skyMaterial.disableDepthWrite = true;
        this.skyMaterial.fogEnabled = false;

        this.skyMaterial.setVector3(
            "sunDirection",
            this.sunDirection
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setVector3(
            "moonDirection",
            this.moonDirection
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setFloat("daylight", 0);
        this.skyMaterial.setFloat("twilight", 0);
        this.skyMaterial.setFloat("starVisibility", 1);
        this.skyMaterial.setFloat("time", 0);

        this.skyMesh.material = this.skyMaterial;
        this.skyMesh.isPickable = false;
        this.skyMesh.infiniteDistance = false;
        this.skyMesh.alwaysSelectAsActiveMesh = true;

        this.skyMesh.renderingGroupId = 0;
    }

	
    // -----------------------------------------
    // VISIBLE SUN
    // -----------------------------------------

    createSun() {
        this.sunMesh =
            BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
                "visibleSun",
                {
                    diameter: this.sunSize,
                    segments: 24
                },
                this.scene
            );

        this.sunMaterial =
            new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
                "visibleSunMaterial",
                this.scene
            );

        this.sunMaterial.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(1, 0.85, 0.45);

        this.sunMaterial.emissiveColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(1, 0.72, 0.28);

        this.sunMaterial.specularColor =
            BABYLON.Color3.Black();

        this.sunMaterial.disableLighting = true;

        this.sunMesh.material = this.sunMaterial;
        this.sunMesh.isPickable = false;
        this.sunMesh.renderingGroupId = 1;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // VISIBLE MOON
    // -----------------------------------------

    createMoon() {
        this.moonMesh =
            BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
                "visibleMoon",
                {
                    diameter: this.moonSize,
                    segments: 24
                },
                this.scene
            );

        this.moonMaterial =
            new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
                "visibleMoonMaterial",
                this.scene
            );

        this.moonMaterial.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.72, 0.78, 0.92);

        this.moonMaterial.emissiveColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.16, 0.19, 0.28);

        this.moonMaterial.specularColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.2, 0.2, 0.25);

        this.moonMaterial.disableLighting = true;

        this.moonMesh.material = this.moonMaterial;
        this.moonMesh.isPickable = false;
        this.moonMesh.renderingGroupId = 1;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TRANSITION FUNCTIONS
    // -----------------------------------------

    smoothstep(min, max, value) {
        const t = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            (value - min) / (max - min),
            0,
            1
        );

        return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DAY/NIGHT UPDATE
    // -----------------------------------------

    update(deltaSeconds) {
        if (
            !Number.isFinite(deltaSeconds) ||
            deltaSeconds < 0
        ) {
            return;
        }

        this.elapsed +=
            deltaSeconds * this.timeScale;

        const phase =
            (this.elapsed / this.dayLength) *
            Math.PI * 2 +
            Math.PI / 2;

        const horizontal = Math.cos(phase);
        const vertical = Math.sin(phase);

        this.sunDirection.set(
            horizontal,
            vertical,
            0
        ).normalize();

        this.moonDirection.copyFrom(
            this.sunDirection
        ).scaleInPlace(-1);

        // Directional light directions.

        this.sunLight.direction.copyFrom(
            this.sunDirection
        ).scaleInPlace(-1);

        this.moonLight.direction.copyFrom(
            this.moonDirection
        ).scaleInPlace(-1);

        // -------------------------------------
        // LIGHT INTENSITIES
        // -------------------------------------

        this.daylight = this.smoothstep(
            -0.12,
            0.28,
            vertical
        );

        const moonlight = this.smoothstep(
            -0.12,
            0.28,
            -vertical
        );

        this.sunLight.intensity =
            1.5 * this.daylight;

        this.moonLight.intensity =
            0.22 * moonlight;

        this.ambientLight.intensity =
            BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(
                0.10,
                0.52,
                this.daylight
            );

        // -------------------------------------
        // ATMOSPHERIC TRANSITIONS
        // -------------------------------------

        this.twilight =
            1.0 - this.smoothstep(
                0.015,
                0.28,
                Math.abs(vertical)
            );

        this.starVisibility =
            1.0 - this.smoothstep(
                -0.18,
                0.12,
                vertical
            );

        // -------------------------------------
        // UPDATE SKY AND CELESTIAL POSITIONS
        // -------------------------------------

const camera = this.scene.activeCamera;

if (camera) {
    this.skyMesh.position.copyFrom(camera.position);

    // Keep the sky inside the camera far plane.
    const farPlane = camera.maxZ || 100000;

    const effectiveRadius = Math.min(
        this.skyRadius,
        farPlane * 0.45
    );

    const skyScale =
        effectiveRadius / this.skyRadius;

    this.skyMesh.scaling.setAll(skyScale);
}

        // -------------------------------------
        // UPDATE SKY SHADER
        // -------------------------------------

        this.skyMaterial.setVector3(
            "sunDirection",
            this.sunDirection
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setVector3(
            "moonDirection",
            this.moonDirection
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setFloat(
            "daylight",
            this.daylight
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setFloat(
            "twilight",
            this.twilight
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setFloat(
            "starVisibility",
            this.starVisibility
        );

        this.skyMaterial.setFloat(
            "time",
            this.elapsed
        );

        // Nighttime fallback clear color.

        this.scene.clearColor =
            new BABYLON.Color4(
                0.003,
                0.006,
                0.022,
                1
            );
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TIME CONTROLS
    // -----------------------------------------

    setTimeScale(scale) {
        this.timeScale =
            BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(scale, 0, 100);
    }

    setDayLength(seconds) {
        this.dayLength = Math.max(60, seconds);
    }

    setTimeOfDay(normalizedTime) {
        this.elapsed =
            BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                normalizedTime,
                0,
                1
            ) * this.dayLength;

        this.update(0);
    }

    getTimeOfDay() {
        return (this.elapsed / this.dayLength) % 1;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DEBUG INFORMATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    getStats() {
        return {
            timeOfDay: this.getTimeOfDay(),
            dayLength: this.dayLength,
            timeScale: this.timeScale,
            daylight: this.daylight,
            twilight: this.twilight,
            starVisibility: this.starVisibility,
            sunIntensity: this.sunLight.intensity,
            moonIntensity: this.moonLight.intensity,
            sunDirection: this.sunDirection.asArray(),
            moonDirection: this.moonDirection.asArray()
        };
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CLEANUP
    // -----------------------------------------
dispose() {
    if (this.skyMesh) {
        this.skyMesh.dispose();
    }

    if (this.skyMaterial) {
        this.skyMaterial.dispose();
    }

    // Retain compatibility if independent celestial
    // meshes are reintroduced in a future implementation.
    this.sunMesh?.dispose();
    this.moonMesh?.dispose();
    this.sunMaterial?.dispose();
    this.moonMaterial?.dispose();

    this.sunLight?.dispose();
    this.moonLight?.dispose();
    this.ambientLight?.dispose();
}
}

export { CelestialSystem };

class TerrainSampler {

    constructor(scene, ground) {

        this.scene = scene;
        this.ground = ground;

        ground.isPickable = true;
        ground.computeWorldMatrix(true);

        const bounds = ground.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox;

        this.min = bounds.minimumWorld.clone();
        this.max = bounds.maximumWorld.clone();
    }


    sample() {

        const x = BABYLON.Scalar.RandomRange(
            this.min.x,
            this.max.x
        );

        const z = BABYLON.Scalar.RandomRange(
            this.min.z,
            this.max.z
        );

        const origin = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            x,
            this.max.y + 100,
            z
        );

        const ray = new BABYLON.Ray(
            origin,
            new BABYLON.Vector3(0, -1, 0),
            this.max.y - this.min.y + 200
        );

        const hit = this.scene.pickWithRay(
            ray,
            mesh => mesh === this.ground,
            true
        );

        if (!hit || !hit.hit || !hit.pickedPoint) {
            return null;
        }

        return hit.pickedPoint.clone();
    }


	
sampleNear(center, radius) {

    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

    // Uniform distribution over a circular area
    const distance = radius * Math.sqrt(Math.random());

    const x = center.x + Math.cos(angle) * distance;
    const z = center.z + Math.sin(angle) * distance;

    return this.sampleAt(x, z);
}


sampleAt(x, z) {

    if (
        x < this.min.x || x > this.max.x ||
        z < this.min.z || z > this.max.z
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const origin = new BABYLON.Vector3(
        x,
        this.max.y + 100,
        z
    );

    const ray = new BABYLON.Ray(
        origin,
        new BABYLON.Vector3(0, -1, 0),
        this.max.y - this.min.y + 200
    );

    const hit = this.scene.pickWithRay(
        ray,
        mesh => mesh === this.ground,
        true
    );

    if (!hit || !hit.hit || !hit.pickedPoint) {
        return null;
    }

    return hit.pickedPoint.clone();
}


sampleSlopeAt(x, z, spacing = 4) {

    const left = this.sampleAt(
        x - spacing,
        z
    );

    const right = this.sampleAt(
        x + spacing,
        z
    );

    const back = this.sampleAt(
        x,
        z - spacing
    );

    const front = this.sampleAt(
        x,
        z + spacing
    );

    if (!left || !right || !back || !front) {
        return null;
    }

    const dx = (
        right.y - left.y
    ) / (spacing * 2);

    const dz = (
        front.y - back.y
    ) / (spacing * 2);

    const magnitude = Math.sqrt(
        dx * dx + dz * dz
    );

    const angle = Math.atan(magnitude);

    return {

        gradientX: dx,

        gradientZ: dz,

        magnitude,

        angle,

        degrees: angle * 180 / Math.PI,

        normal: new BABYLON.Vector3(
            -dx,
            1,
            -dz
        ).normalize()

    };

}


sampleProfileAt(x, z, spacing = 4) {

    const position = this.sampleAt(x, z);

    if (!position) return null;

    const slope = this.sampleSlopeAt(
        x,
        z,
        spacing
    );

    if (!slope) return null;

    const elevation = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(

        (position.y - this.min.y) /
        Math.max(1, this.max.y - this.min.y),

        0,
        1

    );

    return {

        position,

        elevation,

        slope: slope.magnitude,

        slopeDegrees: slope.degrees,

        normal: slope.normal

    };

}



}
export { TerrainSampler }

class SpatialGrid {

    constructor(cellSize = 2.5) {

        this.cellSize = cellSize;
        this.cells = new Map();

    }

    getKey(x, z) {

        const cx = Math.floor(x / this.cellSize);
        const cz = Math.floor(z / this.cellSize);

        return `${cx},${cz}`;
    }

    insert(tree) {

        const key = this.getKey(
            tree.position.x,
            tree.position.z
        );

        if (!this.cells.has(key)) {
            this.cells.set(key, []);
        }

        this.cells.get(key).push(tree);

    }

    isAvailable(position, minSpacing) {

        const cx = Math.floor(
            position.x / this.cellSize
        );

        const cz = Math.floor(
            position.z / this.cellSize
        );

        const searchRadius = Math.ceil(
            minSpacing / this.cellSize
        );

        const minSpacingSquared =
            minSpacing * minSpacing;

        for (
            let x = cx - searchRadius;
            x <= cx + searchRadius;
            x++
        ) {

            for (
                let z = cz - searchRadius;
                z <= cz + searchRadius;
                z++
            ) {

                const key = `${x},${z}`;
                const cell = this.cells.get(key);

                if (!cell) continue;

                for (const tree of cell) {

                    if (!tree.alive) continue;

                    const dx =
                        position.x - tree.position.x;

                    const dz =
                        position.z - tree.position.z;

                    if (
                        dx * dx + dz * dz <
                        minSpacingSquared
                    ) {
                        return false;
                    }

                }

            }

        }

        return true;

    }

}
export { SpatialGrid }
```

src/scene/create-scene.js

```

import { HeightmapTerrain } from "../environment/heightmap-terrain.js";
import { WorldManager } from "../world/world-manager.js";
import { ChunkEcologySystem } from "../world/chunk-ecology-system.js";
import { ChunkGeologySystem } from "../world/chunk-geology-system.js";
import { AdaptiveGraphicsController } from "../rendering/adaptive-graphics-controller.js";
import { WaterSystem } from "../water/water-system.js";
import { Hydrology } from "../water/hydrology.js";
import { RainSystem } from "../water/rain-system.js";
import { ChunkCloudSystem } from "../world/chunk-cloud-system.js";

import { SpatialTransform } from "../world/fields/geometry/spatial-transform.js";
import { GeometryRenderer } from "../world/fields/geometry/geometry-renderer.js";
import { GeometryShaderManager } from "../world/fields/geometry/geometry-shader-manager.js";
import { HyperbolicCameraController } from "../world/fields/geometry/hyperbolic-camera-controller.js";

import { GameManager } from "../game/game-manager.js";
import { PlayerCharacter } from "../game/player-character.js";
import { PlayerController } from "../game/player-controller.js";
import { CharacterCameraController } from "../game/camera-controller.js";
import { MainMenu } from "../ui/main-menu.js";
import { CelestialSystem } from "../environment/celestial-system.js";
import { WorldPersistence } from "../world/persistence/world-persistence.js";
import { WorldSession } from "../world/persistence/world-session.js";
import { WorldLibraryUI } from "../world/persistence/world-library-ui.js";
import { BuildingSystem } from "../buildings/building-system.js";
import { BuildingEditor } from "../editor/building-editor.js";


async function createScene(engine, canvas) {
    console.log("[Scene] Initializing Somnium...");

    const scene = new BABYLON.Scene(engine);

	scene.clearColor = new BABYLON.Color4(
		0.003, 0.006, 0.022, 1
	);

    const WORLD_SIZE = 40000;
    const CHUNK_SIZE = 2000;

    // -----------------------------------------
    // LIGHTING
    // -----------------------------------------


	// -----------------------------------------
// CELESTIAL LIGHTING
// -----------------------------------------

const sunDirection = new BABYLON.Vector3(
    -0.5, -1, -0.3
).normalize();

const sun = new BABYLON.DirectionalLight(
    "sun",
    sunDirection.clone(),
    scene
);

const ambient = new BABYLON.HemisphericLight(
    "ambient",
    new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1, 0),
    scene
);

const celestial = new CelestialSystem(
    scene,
    {
        sunLight: sun,
        ambientLight: ambient,
        dayLength: 900,
        orbitDistance: 45000,
        skyRadius: 90000,
        sunSize: 1800,
        moonSize: 1300
    }
);

scene.metadata = {
    ...(scene.metadata || {}),
    celestial
};
    // -----------------------------------------
    // HEIGHTMAP
    // -----------------------------------------

    const terrain = new HeightmapTerrain({
        url: "./assets/0_somnium_map_topographic.png",
        worldSize: WORLD_SIZE,
        minHeight: -2000,
        maxHeight: 1000,
        heightChannel: "average"
    });

    await terrain.load();

    // -----------------------------------------
    // SPATIAL TRANSFORM
    // -----------------------------------------

    const spatialTransform = new SpatialTransform({
        geometry: "poincare",
        renderScale: 10000,

        bounds: {
            minX: terrain.min.x,
            maxX: terrain.max.x,
            minY: -WORLD_SIZE / 2,
            maxY: WORLD_SIZE / 2,
            minZ: terrain.min.z,
            maxZ: terrain.max.z,
            characteristicLength: 0.25
        }
    });

    // -----------------------------------------
    // GEOMETRY RENDERER
    // -----------------------------------------

    const geometryRenderer = new GeometryRenderer(
        scene,
        spatialTransform
    );

const buildingSystem = new BuildingSystem(
    scene,
    geometryRenderer,
    { terrain }
);

const buildingEditor = new BuildingEditor(
    scene,
    buildingSystem,
    spatialTransform
);

scene.metadata = {
    ...(scene.metadata || {}),
    buildingSystem,
    buildingEditor
};

    const geometryShaderManager =
        new GeometryShaderManager(
            scene,
            spatialTransform,
            {
                enabled: true,
                renderScale: spatialTransform.renderScale
            }
        );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // FLIGHT CAMERA
    // -----------------------------------------

    const camera = new BABYLON.FreeCamera(
        "FlightCamera",
        BABYLON.Vector3.Zero(),
        scene
    );

    camera.minZ = 1;
    camera.maxZ = 100000;
    camera.speed = 500;
    camera.angularSensibility = 3000;

    const initialCameraPosition =
        spatialTransform.worldToRender({
            x: 0,
            y: 1800,
            z: -3000
        });

    camera.position.set(
        initialCameraPosition.x,
        initialCameraPosition.y,
        initialCameraPosition.z
    );

    camera.setTarget(BABYLON.Vector3.Zero());

    // -----------------------------------------
    // HYPERBOLIC FLIGHT CAMERA
    // -----------------------------------------

    const hyperbolicCamera =
        new HyperbolicCameraController(
            camera,
            spatialTransform,
            {
                maxHyperbolicSpeed: 2.5,
                boundaryMargin: 1e-7
            }
        );

    // Synchronize after initial camera positioning.
    hyperbolicCamera.synchronize();

    // -----------------------------------------
    // THIRD-PERSON CAMERA
    // -----------------------------------------

    const thirdPersonCamera = new BABYLON.FreeCamera(
        "ThirdPersonCamera",
        BABYLON.Vector3.Zero(),
        scene
    );

    thirdPersonCamera.minZ = 0.5;
    thirdPersonCamera.maxZ = 100000;

    // -----------------------------------------
    // PLAYER CHARACTER
    // -----------------------------------------

    const playerCharacter = new PlayerCharacter(
        scene,
        spatialTransform,
        {
            x: 0,
            z: 0,
            terrain
        }
    );

    const playerController = new PlayerController(
        playerCharacter,
        terrain,
        canvas,
        {
            walkSpeed: 180,
            runSpeed: 360,
            turnSpeed: 2.2
        }
    );

	const characterCameraController =
    new CharacterCameraController(
        scene,
        thirdPersonCamera,
        playerCharacter,
        spatialTransform,
        {
            distance: 24,
            height: 8,

            minDistance: 6,
            maxDistance: 120,

            zoomSensitivity: 0.001,
            smoothing: 8
        }
    );
    // -----------------------------------------
    // GAME MANAGER
    // -----------------------------------------

    const gameManager = new GameManager(
        scene,
        {
            canvas,
            flightCamera: camera
        }
    );

	buildingEditor.setGameManager(gameManager);

    gameManager.registerThirdPerson(
        thirdPersonCamera,
        playerController,
        characterCameraController
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // MAIN MENU
    // -----------------------------------------

    const mainMenu = new MainMenu(gameManager);

scene.metadata = {
    ...(scene.metadata || {}),
    celestial,
    spatialTransform,
    geometryRenderer,
    geometryShaderManager,
    hyperbolicCamera,
    gameManager,
    mainMenu,
    playerCharacter,
    playerController,
    characterCameraController
};
    // -----------------------------------------
    // HEIGHTMAP DIAGNOSTICS
    // -----------------------------------------

    const heightmapDiagnostics =
        terrain.getDiagnostics(64);

    console.log(
        "[Scene] Heightmap diagnostics:",
        heightmapDiagnostics
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TERRAIN STREAMING
    // -----------------------------------------

    const world = new WorldManager(
        scene,
        terrain,
        {
            chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
            renderRadius: 3,
            highDetailRadius: 2,
            maxChunksPerUpdate: 4
        }
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // ADAPTIVE GRAPHICS
    // -----------------------------------------

    const graphics = new AdaptiveGraphicsController(
        engine,
        camera,
        world,
        {
            targetFPS: 55,
            minimumFPS: 35
        }
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CHUNK ECOLOGY
    // -----------------------------------------

    const ecology = new ChunkEcologySystem(
        scene,
        terrain,
        {
            worldSeed: 91827,
            chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
            renderRadius: 3,
            treesPerChunk: 60,
            seaLevel: -100,
            sunDirection
        }
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CHUNK GEOLOGY
    // -----------------------------------------

    const geology = new ChunkGeologySystem(
        scene,
        terrain,
        {
            worldSeed: 91827,
            chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
            renderRadius: 3,
            rocksPerChunk: 10,
            seaLevel: -100
        }
    );

    console.log("[Scene] Chunk geology connected.");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // WATER
    // -----------------------------------------

    const water = new WaterSystem(
        scene,
        terrain,
        -100,
        {
            chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
            renderRadius: 3
        }
    );

    // -----------------------------------------
    // HYDROLOGY
    // -----------------------------------------

    const hydrology = new Hydrology(
        terrain,
        water,
        20
    );
// -----------------------------------------
// SHARED GEOLOGICAL AND ECOLOGICAL FIELDS
// -----------------------------------------

// One authoritative geological field is shared
// by hydrology and the active chunk ecology.
hydrology.geologicalField = geology.field;

ecology.geologicalField = geology.field;
ecology.hydrology = hydrology;

console.log(
    "[Scene] Geological soil systems connected."
);

    // -----------------------------------------
    // RAIN
    // -----------------------------------------

    const rain = new RainSystem(scene);

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CLOUDS
    // -----------------------------------------

    const clouds = new ChunkCloudSystem(
        scene,
        terrain,
        water,
        hydrology,
        rain,
        {
            chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
            renderRadius: 4,
            cloudsPerChunk: 8,
            maxChunksPerUpdate: 2,
            worldSeed: 91827,
            renderInterval: 0.5,
            simulationInterval: 0.25
        }
    );

// -----------------------------------------
// PERSISTENT WORLD LIBRARY
// -----------------------------------------

const worldPersistence = new WorldPersistence();

await worldPersistence.open();

const worldSession = new WorldSession(ecology);

let activeWorld = null;
let simulationElapsed = 0;

const worldLibrary = new WorldLibraryUI(
    worldPersistence,
    {

onLoad: async world => {
    const result = worldSession.restore(world);

    buildingSystem.restoreWorld(world);

    activeWorld = world;
    simulationElapsed = result.elapsedTime;

    geology.worldSeed = result.worldSeed;
    clouds.worldSeed = result.worldSeed;
    geology.field.seed = result.worldSeed;

    geology.records.clear();
    geology.activeChunks.clear();

    for (const renderer of Object.values(geology.renderers)) {
        renderer.clear();
        renderer.update();
    }

    clouds.records.clear();
    clouds.activeChunks.clear();
    clouds.renderer.clear();
    clouds.renderer.update();

    ecology.renderDirty = true;

    console.log("[World] Loaded:", world.metadata.name, result);
},

onCapture: async world => {
    worldSession.capture(world, simulationElapsed);
    buildingSystem.captureWorld(world);
}


    }
);

scene.metadata = {
    ...(scene.metadata || {}),
    worldPersistence,
    worldSession,
    worldLibrary
};

    console.log("[Scene] Environmental systems connected.");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // UPDATE LOOP
    // -----------------------------------------

    let ecologyAccumulator = 0;

    scene.onBeforeRenderObservable.add(() => {
        const deltaSeconds = Math.min(
            engine.getDeltaTime() / 1000,
            0.1
        );

	if (activeWorld) {
		simulationElapsed += deltaSeconds;
	}
	
	celestial.update(deltaSeconds);

        graphics.update(deltaSeconds);

        let position;
        let forward;

        if (gameManager.mode === "flight") {
            // Only the flight camera receives hyperbolic
            // camera updates.
            hyperbolicCamera.update(deltaSeconds);

            position =
                hyperbolicCamera.getWorldPosition();

            forward =
                camera.getForwardRay().direction;

        } else if (gameManager.mode === "thirdPerson") {
            // Character movement operates in original
            // authoritative world coordinates.
            playerController.update(deltaSeconds);

            characterCameraController.update(
                deltaSeconds
            );

            position = {
                ...playerCharacter.worldPosition
            };

            const yaw = playerCharacter.yaw;

            forward = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                Math.sin(yaw),
                0,
                Math.cos(yaw)
            );

        } else {
            // Menu and pause: preserve the world at its
            // current camera position without advancing
            // either navigation controller.
            position = hyperbolicCamera.getWorldPosition();
            forward = camera.getForwardRay().direction;
        }

        // -------------------------------------
        // WORLD STREAMING
        // -------------------------------------

        world.update(position, forward);

        // -------------------------------------
        // GEOLOGY
        // -------------------------------------

        geology.update(position);

        // -------------------------------------
        // REGIONAL WATER
        // -------------------------------------

        water.update(position, world.renderRadius);

        // -------------------------------------
        // ATMOSPHERE
        // -------------------------------------

        clouds.update(position, deltaSeconds);

        // -------------------------------------
        // HYDROLOGY
        // -------------------------------------

        const deltaYears = deltaSeconds * 0.05;

        hydrology.update(deltaYears);
        rain.update();

        // -------------------------------------
        // ECOLOGY
        // -------------------------------------

        ecologyAccumulator += deltaSeconds;

        if (ecologyAccumulator >= 0.25) {
            const elapsed = ecologyAccumulator;
            ecologyAccumulator = 0;

            ecology.update(position, elapsed);
        }
    });

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DIAGNOSTICS
    // -----------------------------------------

    scene.metadata = {
        ...(scene.metadata || {}),

        worldSize: terrain.worldSize,

        spatialTransform,
        geometryRenderer,
        geometryShaderManager,

        getGeometryStats: () =>
            geometryRenderer.getStats(),

	getCelestialStats: () => celestial.getStats(),

        getWorldStats: () =>
            world.getStats(),

        getEcologyStats: () =>
            ecology.getStats(),

        getGeologyStats: () =>
            geology.getStats(),

        getGraphicsStats: () =>
            graphics.getStats(),

        getHeightmapStats: () =>
            terrain.getDiagnostics(64),

        getWaterStats: () =>
            water.getStats(),

        getCloudStats: () =>
            clouds.getStats(),

        getGeometryShaderStats: () =>
            geometryShaderManager.getStats(),

        getHydrologyStats: () =>
            hydrology.getStatistics(),

        getGameStats: () =>
            gameManager.getState()
    };

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CLEANUP
    // -----------------------------------------

    scene.onDisposeObservable.add(() => {
        console.log("[Scene] Disposing Somnium systems.");

        mainMenu.dispose();
        gameManager.dispose();

        playerController.dispose();
        characterCameraController.dispose();
        playerCharacter.dispose();

        geometryRenderer.dispose(world);

        graphics.dispose();
        ecology.dispose();
        geology.dispose();
        world.dispose();

        terrain.dispose();
        rain.dispose();
        water.dispose();
        clouds.dispose();

        geometryShaderManager.dispose();
        hyperbolicCamera.dispose();
	celestial.dispose();


	buildingEditor.dispose();
	buildingSystem.dispose();


	worldLibrary.dispose();
	worldPersistence.close();
    });

    // -----------------------------------------
    // INITIALIZATION COMPLETE
    // -----------------------------------------

    console.log("[Scene] Somnium initialized.");

    console.log("[Scene] Configuration:", {
        worldSize: WORLD_SIZE,
        chunkSize: CHUNK_SIZE,
        chunksAcross: WORLD_SIZE / CHUNK_SIZE,
        initialPerspective: gameManager.mode,
        treesPerChunk: ecology.treesPerChunk,
        rocksPerChunk: geology.rocksPerChunk
    });

    return scene;
}

export default createScene;

src/buildings/building-generator.js

```

class BuildingGenerator {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.defaultWallHeight = options.wallHeight ?? 3.2;
        this.defaultWallThickness = options.wallThickness ?? 0.18;
    }

    createBox(output, kind, position, dimensions, rotation = 0, material = "wall") {
        if (
            dimensions.x <= 0 ||
            dimensions.y <= 0 ||
            dimensions.z <= 0
        ) return;

        output.push({
            kind,
            position,
            dimensions,
            rotation,
            material
        });
    }

    getRoomEdges(room) {
        const b = room.bounds;

        return [
            {
                side: "north",
                x1: b.minX, z1: b.minZ,
                x2: b.maxX, z2: b.minZ
            },
            {
                side: "south",
                x1: b.minX, z1: b.maxZ,
                x2: b.maxX, z2: b.maxZ
            },
            {
                side: "west",
                x1: b.minX, z1: b.minZ,
                x2: b.minX, z2: b.maxZ
            },
            {
                side: "east",
                x1: b.maxX, z1: b.minZ,
                x2: b.maxX, z2: b.maxZ
            }
        ];
    }

    edgeKey(edge, floor) {
        const a = `${edge.x1},${edge.z1}`;
        const b = `${edge.x2},${edge.z2}`;

        return [
            floor,
            ...[a, b].sort()
        ].join(":");
    }

    generateWall(output, edge, openings, floorY, height, thickness) {
        const horizontal = edge.side === "north" ||
            edge.side === "south";

        const length = horizontal
            ? Math.abs(edge.x2 - edge.x1)
            : Math.abs(edge.z2 - edge.z1);

        const centerX = (edge.x1 + edge.x2) * 0.5;
        const centerZ = (edge.z1 + edge.z2) * 0.5;

        const wallRotation = horizontal ? 0 : Math.PI / 2;

        const centerY = floorY + height * 0.5;

        const validOpenings = openings
            .filter(o => o.side === edge.side)
            .map(o => ({
                ...o,
                center: o.offset * length
            }))
            .filter(o =>
                o.center - o.width * 0.5 >= 0 &&
                o.center + o.width * 0.5 <= length
            )
            .sort((a, b) => a.center - b.center);

        let cursor = 0;

        const addSegment = (start, end) => {
            if (end - start <= 0.01) return;

            const middle = (start + end) * 0.5;
            const along = middle - length * 0.5;

            this.createBox(
                output,
                "walls",
                {
                    x: centerX + (horizontal ? along : 0),
                    y: centerY,
                    z: centerZ + (horizontal ? 0 : along)
                },
                {
                    x: horizontal ? end - start : thickness,
                    y: height,
                    z: horizontal ? thickness : end - start
                },
                0,
                "wall"
            );
        };

        for (const opening of validOpenings) {
            const start = opening.center - opening.width * 0.5;
            const end = opening.center + opening.width * 0.5;

            // Full-height wall before the opening.
            addSegment(cursor, start);

            const along = opening.center - length * 0.5;
            const x = centerX + (horizontal ? along : 0);
            const z = centerZ + (horizontal ? 0 : along);

            // Windows retain their sill and lintel.
            if (opening.type === "window") {
                if (opening.sill > 0) {
                    this.createBox(
                        output,
                        "walls",
                        { x, y: floorY + opening.sill * 0.5, z },
                        {
                            x: horizontal ? opening.width : thickness,
                            y: opening.sill,
                            z: horizontal ? thickness : opening.width
                        }
                    );
                }

                const top = opening.sill + opening.height;
                const remaining = height - top;

                if (remaining > 0) {
                    this.createBox(
                        output,
                        "walls",
                        {
                            x,
                            y: floorY + top + remaining * 0.5,
                            z
                        },
                        {
                            x: horizontal ? opening.width : thickness,
                            y: remaining,
                            z: horizontal ? thickness : opening.width
                        }
                    );
                }

                this.createBox(
                    output,
                    "glazing",
                    {
                        x,
                        y: floorY + opening.sill + opening.height * 0.5,
                        z
                    },
                    {
                        x: horizontal ? opening.width * 0.88 : 0.035,
                        y: opening.height * 0.88,
                        z: horizontal ? 0.035 : opening.width * 0.88
                    },
                    0,
                    "glass"
                );
            } else {
                // Door frame components.
                const frame = 0.075;

                for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
                    this.createBox(
                        output,
                        "trim",
                        {
                            x: x + (horizontal ? side * (opening.width * 0.5 - frame * 0.5) : 0),
                            y: floorY + opening.height * 0.5,
                            z: z + (horizontal ? 0 : side * (opening.width * 0.5 - frame * 0.5))
                        },
                        {
                            x: horizontal ? frame : thickness * 0.75,
                            y: opening.height,
                            z: horizontal ? thickness * 0.75 : frame
                        },
                        0,
                        "wood"
                    );
                }

                this.createBox(
                    output,
                    "trim",
                    { x, y: floorY + opening.height, z },
                    {
                        x: horizontal ? opening.width : thickness * 0.75,
                        y: frame,
                        z: horizontal ? thickness * 0.75 : opening.width
                    },
                    0,
                    "wood"
                );

                // Door leaf is a separate component.
                this.createBox(
                    output,
                    "doors",
                    {
                        x,
                        y: floorY + opening.height * 0.5,
                        z
                    },
                    {
                        x: horizontal ? opening.width * 0.94 : 0.07,
                        y: opening.height * 0.94,
                        z: horizontal ? 0.07 : opening.width * 0.94
                    },
                    0,
                    "wood"
                );
            }

            cursor = end;
        }

        addSegment(cursor, length);
    }

    generate(building) {
        building.validate();

        const output = [];

        const edges = new Map();

        for (const room of building.rooms) {
            const floorY = room.floor * room.wallHeight;
            const b = room.bounds;

            this.createBox(
                output,
                "floors",
                {
                    x: (b.minX + b.maxX) * 0.5,
                    y: floorY - room.floorThickness * 0.5,
                    z: (b.minZ + b.maxZ) * 0.5
                },
                {
                    x: b.maxX - b.minX,
                    y: room.floorThickness,
                    z: b.maxZ - b.minZ
                },
                0,
                "floor"
            );

            this.createBox(
                output,
                "ceilings",
                {
                    x: (b.minX + b.maxX) * 0.5,
                    y: floorY + room.wallHeight,
                    z: (b.minZ + b.maxZ) * 0.5
                },
                {
                    x: b.maxX - b.minX,
                    y: 0.12,
                    z: b.maxZ - b.minZ
                },
                0,
                "ceiling"
            );

            for (const edge of this.getRoomEdges(room)) {
                const key = this.edgeKey(edge, room.floor);

                if (!edges.has(key)) {
                    edges.set(key, {
                        edge,
                        openings: [],
                        floorY,
                        height: room.wallHeight,
                        thickness: room.wallThickness
                    });
                }

		edges.get(key).openings.push(
    ...(room.openings ?? [])
        .filter(o => o.side === edge.side)
        .map(o => ({
            ...o,
            side: edges.get(key).edge.side
        }))
);

            }
        }

        for (const item of edges.values()) {
            this.generateWall(
                output,
                item.edge,
                item.openings,
                item.floorY,
                item.height,
                item.thickness
            );
        }

        // Explicitly placed modular objects.
        const components = [
            ...building.components,
            ...building.furniture,
            ...building.fixtures
        ];

        for (const component of components) {
            const kind = component.kind === "furniture"
                ? "furniture"
                : component.kind === "fixture"
                    ? "fixtures"
                    : component.kind;

            this.createBox(
                output,
                kind,
                component.position,
                component.dimensions,
                component.rotation,
                component.material
            );
        }

        return output;
    }
}

export { BuildingGenerator };

```

src/buildings/building-model.js
_______
```

function createId(prefix = "building") {
    return `${prefix}-${globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.() ??
        `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`}`;
}

function clone(value) {
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
}

class BuildingModel {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.id = options.id ?? createId();

        this.name = options.name ?? "New Building";
        this.type = options.type ?? "residential";

        this.position = {
            x: options.position?.x ?? 0,
            y: options.position?.y ?? 0,
            z: options.position?.z ?? 0
        };

        this.rotation = options.rotation ?? 0;

        this.rooms = clone(options.rooms ?? []);
        this.furniture = clone(options.furniture ?? []);
        this.fixtures = clone(options.fixtures ?? []);

        // Independently placed architectural elements.
        this.components = clone(options.components ?? []);

        this.metadata = {
            version: 1,
            createdAt: options.metadata?.createdAt ??
                new Date().toISOString()
        };
    }

    addRoom(options = {}) {
        const room = {
            id: createId("room"),
            name: options.name ?? "Room",
            floor: options.floor ?? 0,

            bounds: {
                minX: options.minX ?? 0,
                maxX: options.maxX ?? 8,
                minZ: options.minZ ?? 0,
                maxZ: options.maxZ ?? 8
            },

            wallHeight: options.wallHeight ?? 3.2,
            wallThickness: options.wallThickness ?? 0.18,

            floorThickness: options.floorThickness ?? 0.15,

            openings: clone(options.openings ?? [])
        };

        this.rooms.push(room);
        return room;
    }

    addOpening(roomId, options = {}) {
        const room = this.rooms.find(r => r.id === roomId);

        if (!room) {
            throw new Error("Room not found.");
        }

        const opening = {
            id: createId("opening"),
            type: options.type ?? "door",
            side: options.side ?? "north",
            offset: options.offset ?? 0.5,
            width: options.width ?? 0.9,
            height: options.height ?? 2.1,
            sill: options.sill ?? 0
        };

        room.openings.push(opening);
        return opening;
    }

    addComponent(options = {}) {
        const component = {
            id: createId("component"),
            kind: options.kind ?? "furniture",
            type: options.type ?? "custom",

            position: {
                x: options.position?.x ?? 0,
                y: options.position?.y ?? 0,
                z: options.position?.z ?? 0
            },

            dimensions: {
                x: options.dimensions?.x ?? 1,
                y: options.dimensions?.y ?? 1,
                z: options.dimensions?.z ?? 1
            },

            rotation: options.rotation ?? 0,
            material: options.material ?? "wood"
        };

        this.components.push(component);
        return component;
    }

    addFurniture(options = {}) {
        return this.addComponent({
            ...options,
            kind: "furniture"
        });
    }

    validate() {
        if (!this.id || !Array.isArray(this.rooms)) {
            throw new Error("Invalid building.");
        }

        for (const room of this.rooms) {
            const b = room.bounds;

            if (
                !b ||
                ![b.minX, b.maxX, b.minZ, b.maxZ].every(Number.isFinite) ||
                b.maxX <= b.minX ||
                b.maxZ <= b.minZ ||
                !Number.isFinite(room.floor) ||
                !Number.isFinite(room.wallHeight) ||
                room.wallHeight <= 0
            ) {
                throw new Error(`Invalid room: ${room.id}`);
            }

            for (const opening of room.openings ?? []) {
                if (
                    !["north", "south", "east", "west"].includes(opening.side) ||
                    !["door", "window"].includes(opening.type) ||
                    !Number.isFinite(opening.offset) ||
                    opening.offset < 0 ||
                    opening.offset > 1 ||
                    !Number.isFinite(opening.width) ||
                    opening.width <= 0 ||
                    !Number.isFinite(opening.height) ||
                    opening.height <= 0 ||
                    !Number.isFinite(opening.sill) ||
                    opening.sill < 0 ||
                    opening.sill + opening.height > room.wallHeight
                ) {
                    throw new Error(`Invalid opening in room ${room.id}`);
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    serialize() {
        this.validate();

        return {
            id: this.id,
            type: "building",
            version: 1,
            name: this.name,
            buildingType: this.type,
            position: clone(this.position),
            rotation: this.rotation,
            rooms: clone(this.rooms),
            furniture: clone(this.furniture),
            fixtures: clone(this.fixtures),
            components: clone(this.components),
            metadata: clone(this.metadata)
        };
    }

    static deserialize(data) {
        if (!data || data.type !== "building" ||
            ![1].includes(data.version)) {
            throw new Error("Unsupported building record.");
        }

        const building = new BuildingModel({
            id: data.id,
            name: data.name,
            type: data.buildingType,
            position: data.position,
            rotation: data.rotation,
            rooms: data.rooms,
            furniture: data.furniture,
            fixtures: data.fixtures,
            components: data.components,
            metadata: data.metadata
        });

        building.validate();
        return building;
    }
}

export { BuildingModel, createId };

```

src/buildings/building-renderer.js
_______
```
import {
    ThinInstanceRenderer
} from "../rendering/thin-instance-renderer.js";

const MATERIALS = {
    wall: [0.72, 0.69, 0.62],
    floor: [0.48, 0.34, 0.22],
    ceiling: [0.83, 0.81, 0.75],
    glass: [0.35, 0.70, 0.86],
    wood: [0.34, 0.20, 0.11],
    furniture: [0.48, 0.31, 0.20]
};

const BUCKETS = [
    "walls",
    "floors",
    "ceilings",
    "glazing",
    "trim",
    "doors",
    "furniture",
    "fixtures"
];

/**
 * BuildingRenderer
 *
 * Converts authoritative architectural components into
 * thin-instance rendering batches.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * This class never applies SpatialTransform directly.
 * ThinInstanceRenderer delegates projection to the
 * existing GeometryRenderer.
 */
class BuildingRenderer {
    constructor(scene, spatialTransform = null) {
        if (!scene) {
            throw new Error(
                "BuildingRenderer requires a scene."
            );
        }

        this.scene = scene;

        // Retained for compatibility with existing integration.
        // Projection ownership belongs to GeometryRenderer.
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.buckets = new Map();
        this.disposed = false;

        this.statistics = {
            submittedComponents: 0,
            rejectedComponents: 0,
            renderedInstances: 0
        };

        for (const kind of BUCKETS) {
            this.createBucket(kind);
        }
    }

    createBucket(kind) {
        const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateBox(
            `building-prototype-${kind}`,
            { size: 1 },
            this.scene
        );

        const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            `building-material-${kind}`,
            this.scene
        );

        const colorName =
            kind === "walls" ? "wall" :
            kind === "floors" ? "floor" :
            kind === "ceilings" ? "ceiling" :
            kind === "glazing" ? "glass" :
            kind === "trim" || kind === "doors" ? "wood" :
            "furniture";

        material.diffuseColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
            ...MATERIALS[colorName]
        );

        if (kind === "glazing") {
            material.alpha = 0.42;

            material.transparencyMode =
                BABYLON.Material.MATERIAL_ALPHABLEND;

            material.backFaceCulling = false;

            material.specularColor =
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.8, 0.9, 1);
        }

        mesh.material = material;

        const renderer = new ThinInstanceRenderer(
            this.scene,
            `building-${kind}`,
            mesh
        );

        this.buckets.set(kind, {
            mesh,
            material,
            renderer
        });
    }

    clear() {
        for (const bucket of this.buckets.values()) {
            bucket.renderer.clear();
        }

        this.statistics.submittedComponents = 0;
        this.statistics.rejectedComponents = 0;
        this.statistics.renderedInstances = 0;
    }

    addComponent(component, building) {
        if (
            !component ||
            !building ||
            !component.position ||
            !component.dimensions
        ) {
            this.statistics.rejectedComponents++;
            return false;
        }

        const bucket = this.buckets.get(component.kind);

        if (!bucket) {
            this.statistics.rejectedComponents++;
            return false;
        }

        const values = [
            component.position.x,
            component.position.y,
            component.position.z,
            component.dimensions.x,
            component.dimensions.y,
            component.dimensions.z,
            building.position.x,
            building.position.y,
            building.position.z
        ];

        if (
            !values.every(Number.isFinite) ||
            values.slice(3, 6).some(value => value <= 0)
        ) {
            this.statistics.rejectedComponents++;
            return false;
        }

        const angle = building.rotation ?? 0;

        if (!Number.isFinite(angle)) {
            this.statistics.rejectedComponents++;
            return false;
        }

        const cos = Math.cos(angle);
        const sin = Math.sin(angle);

        const local = component.position;

        const rotated = {
            x: local.x * cos - local.z * sin,
            y: local.y,
            z: local.x * sin + local.z * cos
        };

        // Authoritative world coordinates.
        // Do not project this position here.
        const world = {
            x: building.position.x + rotated.x,
            y: building.position.y + rotated.y,
            z: building.position.z + rotated.z
        };

        const rotation = BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationAxis(
            BABYLON.Axis.Y,
            angle + (component.rotation ?? 0)
        );

        // Dimensions remain in authoritative units.
        // GeometryRenderer applies the spatial projection.
        const scale = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            component.dimensions.x,
            component.dimensions.y,
            component.dimensions.z
        );

        const matrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
            scale,
            rotation,
            new BABYLON.Vector3(
                world.x,
                world.y,
                world.z
            )
        );

        bucket.renderer.addMatrix(matrix);

        this.statistics.submittedComponents++;

        return true;
    }

    update(buildings) {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.clear();

        for (const building of buildings) {
            if (!Array.isArray(building.generatedGeometry)) {
                continue;
            }

            for (const component of building.generatedGeometry) {
                this.addComponent(component, building);
            }
        }

        for (const bucket of this.buckets.values()) {
            bucket.renderer.update();

            this.statistics.renderedInstances +=
                bucket.renderer.matrices.length;
        }
    }

    getStats() {
        const result = {};

        for (const [kind, bucket] of this.buckets) {
            result[kind] = {
                instances: bucket.renderer.matrices.length
            };
        }

        return {
            buckets: result,
            submittedComponents:
                this.statistics.submittedComponents,
            rejectedComponents:
                this.statistics.rejectedComponents,
            renderedInstances:
                this.statistics.renderedInstances
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        for (const bucket of this.buckets.values()) {
            bucket.renderer.dispose?.();
            bucket.mesh.dispose();
            bucket.material.dispose();
        }

        this.buckets.clear();
    }
}

export { BuildingRenderer };
```

src/buildings/building-system.js
_______
```

import { BuildingModel } from "./building-model.js";
import { BuildingGenerator } from "./building-generator.js";
import { BuildingRenderer } from "./building-renderer.js";

class BuildingSystem {
    constructor(scene, geometryRenderer, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.geometryRenderer = geometryRenderer;

        this.generator = new BuildingGenerator();

        this.renderer = new BuildingRenderer(
            scene,
            options.spatialTransform ?? null
        );

        this.buildings = new Map();
        this.disposed = false;
        this.terrain = options.terrain ?? null;
    }

    createBuilding(options = {}) {
        const building = new BuildingModel(options);

        building.validate();

        this.buildings.set(building.id, building);
        this.rebuild(building);

        return building;
    }

    addBuilding(building) {
        if (!(building instanceof BuildingModel)) {
            building = BuildingModel.deserialize(building);
        }

        building.validate();
        this.buildings.set(building.id, building);
        this.rebuild(building);

        return building;
    }

    removeBuilding(id) {
        const removed = this.buildings.delete(id);

        if (removed) this.refresh();

        return removed;
    }

    getBuilding(id) {
        return this.buildings.get(id) ?? null;
    }

    rebuild(building) {
        building.validate();

        building.generatedGeometry =
            this.generator.generate(building);

        this.refresh();
    }

    refresh() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.renderer.update(
            Array.from(this.buildings.values())
        );
    }

    createRoom(buildingId, options = {}) {
        const building = this.getBuilding(buildingId);

        if (!building) {
            throw new Error("Building not found.");
        }

        const room = building.addRoom(options);

        this.rebuild(building);

        return room;
    }

    addOpening(buildingId, roomId, options = {}) {
        const building = this.getBuilding(buildingId);

        if (!building) {
            throw new Error("Building not found.");
        }

        const opening = building.addOpening(
            roomId,
            options
        );

        this.rebuild(building);

        return opening;
    }

    addFurniture(buildingId, options = {}) {
        const building = this.getBuilding(buildingId);

        if (!building) {
            throw new Error("Building not found.");
        }

        const furniture = building.addFurniture(options);

        this.rebuild(building);

        return furniture;
    }

    captureWorld(world) {
        if (!world?.entities) {
            throw new Error("Invalid world state.");
        }

        const other = world.entities.manual.filter(
            entity => entity.type !== "building"
        );

        const committed = Array.from(
            this.buildings.values()
        ).filter(
            building =>
                building.metadata?.editorPreview !== true
        );

        world.entities.manual = [
            ...other,
            ...committed.map(
                building => building.serialize()
            )
        ];

        return world;
    }

    restoreWorld(world) {
        if (
            !world?.entities ||
            !Array.isArray(world.entities.manual)
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid world entity collection."
            );
        }

        this.buildings.clear();

        for (const entity of world.entities.manual) {
            if (entity.type === "building") {
                this.addBuilding(entity);
            }
        }

        this.refresh();
    }

    getStats() {
        const buildings = Array.from(
            this.buildings.values()
        );

        return {
            buildings: buildings.length,
            provisional: buildings.filter(
                building =>
                    building.metadata?.editorPreview === true
            ).length,
            committed: buildings.filter(
                building =>
                    building.metadata?.editorPreview !== true
            ).length,
            rooms: buildings.reduce(
                (sum, building) =>
                    sum + building.rooms.length,
                0
            ),
            instances: this.renderer.getStats()
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;
        this.buildings.clear();
        this.renderer.dispose();
    }
}

export { BuildingSystem };

src/main.js
_______
```

import createScene from "./scene/create-scene.js";

async function main() {
    console.log("[Main] Initializing Somnium...");

    const canvas = document.getElementById("renderCanvas");

    if (!canvas) {
        throw new Error("Render canvas not found.");
    }

    const engine = new BABYLON.Engine(
        canvas,
        true,
        {
            useLargeWorldRendering: true
        }
    );

    let scene = null;

    try {
        console.log("[Main] Engine initialized.");

        scene = await createScene(engine, canvas);

        console.log("[Main] Scene initialized.");

        engine.runRenderLoop(() => {
            if (scene && !scene.isDisposed) {
                scene.render();
            }
        });

        const handleResize = () => {
            engine.resize();
        };

        window.addEventListener("resize", handleResize);

        window.addEventListener("beforeunload", () => {
            window.removeEventListener("resize", handleResize);

            scene?.dispose();
            engine.dispose();
        }, { once: true });

    } catch (error) {
        scene?.dispose();
        engine.dispose();
        throw error;
    }
}

main().catch(error => {
    console.error(
        "[Main] Application initialization failed:",
        error
    );
});

import { fractalNoise2D } from "../core/noise.js";

    anomaly: {
