        hardness: 0.85,
        cohesion: 0.35,
        porosity: 0.20,
        moistureCapacity: 0.50,
        infiltration: 0.35,
        drainage: 0.45,
        fertility: 0.40,
        roughness: 0.95,
        color: [0.22, 0.12, 0.48]
    }
};

const clamp = (value, min = 0, max = 1) =>
    Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));

class GeologicalField {
    constructor(terrain, seed = 91827) {
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.seed = seed;

        this.regionFrequency = 0.0025;
        this.detailFrequency = 0.009;
        this.slopeSamplingDistance = 4;
    }

    noise(x, z, salt, frequency, octaves = 3) {
        return fractalNoise2D(
            x * frequency,
            z * frequency,
            this.seed + salt,
            octaves
        );
    }

    sample(x, z) {
        const profile = this.terrain.sampleProfileAt(
            x,
            z,
            this.slopeSamplingDistance
        );

        if (!profile) return null;

        const elevation = profile.elevation;
        const slope = profile.slopeDegrees;

        const moistureNoise = this.noise(
            x, z, 1000, this.regionFrequency
        );

        const geologyNoise = this.noise(
            x, z, 2000, this.regionFrequency * 1.5
        );

        const detail = this.noise(
            x, z, 3000, this.detailFrequency
        );

        const strata = this.noise(
            x, z, 4000, 0.004, 4
        );

        const surfaceVariation = this.noise(
            x, z, 5000, 0.025
        );

        const anomalyNoise = this.noise(
            x, z, 6000, 0.0015
        );

        const moisture = clamp(
            moistureNoise * 0.7 + detail * 0.3
        );

        // Determine the underlying geological formation.
        let bedrockType;

        if (anomalyNoise > 0.985) {
            bedrockType = "anomaly";
        } else if (geologyNoise < 0.28) {
            bedrockType = "limestone";
        } else if (geologyNoise < 0.48) {
            bedrockType = "sandstone";
        } else if (geologyNoise > 0.84) {
            bedrockType = "basalt";
        } else {
            bedrockType = "granite";
        }

        if (elevation > 0.70 && geologyNoise > 0.65) {
            bedrockType = "granite";
        }

        // Bedrock exposure increases on steep slopes.
        const exposure = clamp(
            slope / 42 * 0.78 +
            elevation * 0.12 +
            (1 - moisture) * 0.10
        );

	// -----------------------------------------
// TOPOGRAPHIC SOIL DISTRIBUTION
// -----------------------------------------

const mapValue = this.terrain.sampleMapValue(x, z);

let surfaceSoil;

if (mapValue === null) {
    surfaceSoil = "loam";
} else if (mapValue < 0.18) {
    surfaceSoil = "clay";
} else if (mapValue < 0.38) {
    surfaceSoil = "sand";
} else if (mapValue < 0.58) {
    surfaceSoil = "fertileSoil";
} else if (mapValue < 0.76) {
    surfaceSoil = "loam";
} else if (mapValue < 0.90) {
    surfaceSoil = "organic";
} else {
    surfaceSoil = "scree";
}

// Local geology still controls exposed bedrock.
if (exposure > 0.88 || (slope > 32 && exposure > 0.65)) {
    surfaceSoil = "scree";
}

        const surface = MATERIALS[surfaceSoil];
        const bedrock = MATERIALS[bedrockType];

        // Variable soil-horizon depths.
        const soilDepth = Math.max(
            0.08,
            (1 - exposure) *
            (0.8 + moisture * 2.2) *
            (0.75 + surfaceVariation * 0.5)
        );

        const organicThickness = soilDepth *
            (surfaceSoil === "organic" ? 0.22 : 0.06);

        const topsoilThickness = soilDepth * 0.42;
        const subsoilThickness = soilDepth * 0.34;
        const parentThickness = soilDepth -
            organicThickness -
            topsoilThickness -
            subsoilThickness;

        const horizons = {
            O: {
                material: "organic",
                thickness: organicThickness
            },
            A: {
                material: surfaceSoil,
                thickness: topsoilThickness
            },
            B: {
                material: geologyNoise > 0.58 ? "clay" : "silt",
                thickness: subsoilThickness
            },
            C: {
                material: bedrockType,
                thickness: parentThickness
            }
        };

        const weathering = clamp(
            moisture * 0.45 +
            surfaceVariation * 0.25 +
            (1 - bedrock.hardness) * 0.30
        );

        return {
            position: profile.position,
            normal: profile.normal,
            elevation,
            slope,
            slopeDegrees: slope,

            speciesName: exposure > 0.82
                ? bedrockType
                : surfaceSoil,

            bedrockType,
            surfaceSoil,
            horizons,

            soilDepth,
            exposure,
            weathering,
            moisture,
            geology: geologyNoise,
            strata,
            surfaceVariation,

            density: surface.density,
            hardness: surface.hardness,
            cohesion: surface.cohesion,
            porosity: surface.porosity,
            moistureCapacity: surface.moistureCapacity,
            infiltration: surface.infiltration,
            drainage: surface.drainage,
            fertility: surface.fertility,
            roughness: surface.roughness,

            bedrockProperties: { ...bedrock },
            soilProperties: { ...surface },
            material: { ...surface },
            color: surface.color
        };
    }

    getMaterialAt(x, z) {
        return this.sample(x, z);
    }

    getSoilProfileAt(x, z) {
        const sample = this.sample(x, z);

        if (!sample) return null;

        return {
            horizons: sample.horizons,
            soilDepth: sample.soilDepth,
            surfaceSoil: sample.surfaceSoil,
            bedrockType: sample.bedrockType,
            fertility: sample.fertility,
            moistureCapacity: sample.moistureCapacity,
            infiltration: sample.infiltration,
            drainage: sample.drainage,
            exposure: sample.exposure
        };
    }

    getMaterialProperties(x, z) {
        const sample = this.sample(x, z);

        if (!sample) return null;

        return {
            density: sample.density,
            hardness: sample.hardness,
            cohesion: sample.cohesion,
            porosity: sample.porosity,
            moistureCapacity: sample.moistureCapacity,
            infiltration: sample.infiltration,
            drainage: sample.drainage,
            fertility: sample.fertility,
            roughness: sample.roughness
        };
    }
}

export { GeologicalField, MATERIALS };

```

src/geology/geological-manager.js
_______
```
import { GeologicalField } from "./geological-field.js";
import { SurfaceDepositSystem } from "./surface-deposit-system.js";
import { RockSpecies } from "./rock-species.js";
import { ThinInstanceRenderer } from "../rendering/thin-instance-renderer.js";
import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";
import { RockGenerator } from "../geology/rock-generator.js";

let nextFormationId = 0;

class GeologicalManager {

    constructor(scene, terrain, shadowGenerator) {

        this.scene = scene;

        this.terrain = terrain;

        this.shadowGenerator = shadowGenerator;

        this.regionSize = 250;

        this.seed = 91827;

        this.field = new GeologicalField(
            terrain,
            this.seed
        );

	this.surfaceDeposits =
    new SurfaceDepositSystem(
        scene,
        terrain,
        this.field
    );

        this.regions = new Map();

        this.formations = [];

        this.maxFormations = 15000;

        this.minFragmentScale = 0.35;

        this.maxFragmentDepth = 2;

        this.renderDirty = true;

        this.simulationSteps = 0;

        this.renderEverySteps = 5;

        this.renderers = {};


        // One independent rendering batch per material.
        for (const speciesName in RockSpecies) {

            const config = RockSpecies[speciesName];

            const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
                "geology_" + speciesName,
                {
                    diameter: 1,
                    segments: 2
                },
                scene
            );

            const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
                "geologyMaterial_" + speciesName,
                scene
            );

            material.diffuseColor =
                config.color.clone();

            material.specularColor =
                new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.06,
                    0.06,
                    0.06
                );

            if (speciesName === "anomaly") {

                material.emissiveColor =
                    config.color.scale(0.35);

            }

            mesh.material = material;

            this.renderers[speciesName] =
                new ThinInstanceRenderer(
                    scene,
                    "geology_" + speciesName,
                    mesh,
                    shadowGenerator
                );

        }

    }


    getRegionKey(x, z) {

        return `${x},${z}`;

    }


    createFormation(
        speciesName,
        position,
        scale,
        seed,
        fragmentDepth = 0
    ) {

        if (
            !position ||
            this.formations.length >= this.maxFormations
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        const formation = {

            id: nextFormationId++,

            speciesName,

            position: position.clone(),

            scale,

            age: 0,

            integrity: 1.0,

            erosionProgress: 0,

            erosionEvents: 0,

            fragmentDepth,

            seed: seed >>> 0,

            alive: true

        };

        this.formations.push(formation);

        return formation;

    }


    createRegion(regionX, regionZ) {

        const key = this.getRegionKey(
            regionX,
            regionZ
        );

        if (this.regions.has(key)) {

            return this.regions.get(key);

        }

        const seed = (
            Math.imul(regionX, 73856093) ^
            Math.imul(regionZ, 19349663) ^
            this.seed
        ) >>> 0;

        const centerX =
            (regionX + 0.5) * this.regionSize;

        const centerZ =
            (regionZ + 0.5) * this.regionSize;

        const centerSample =
            this.field.sample(centerX, centerZ);

        const region = {

            x: regionX,

            z: regionZ,

            seed,

            dominantSpecies:
                centerSample
                    ? centerSample.speciesName
                    : "granite",

            generated: false,

            formationIds: []

        };

        this.regions.set(key, region);

        return region;

    }


    generateRegion(regionX, regionZ) {

        const region = this.createRegion(
            regionX,
            regionZ
        );

        if (region.generated) return;

        const random =
            CreateSeededRandom(region.seed);

        const originX =
            regionX * this.regionSize;

        const originZ =
            regionZ * this.regionSize;


        const samplePosition = () => {

            const x = originX +
                random() * this.regionSize;

            const z = originZ +
                random() * this.regionSize;

            return this.field.sample(x, z);

        };


        const addFormation = (
            sample,
            scale,
            depth,
            salt
        ) => {

            if (!sample) return;
		// These materials have their own surface renderer.
    if (
        sample.speciesName === "sand" ||
        sample.speciesName === "clay" ||
        sample.speciesName === "fertileSoil"
    ) {
        return;
    }

            const speciesName =
                sample.speciesName;

            const config =
                RockSpecies[speciesName];

            // Small local variations.
            const variation =
                0.8 + random() * 0.4;

            const formation = this.createFormation(

                speciesName,

                sample.position,

                scale * variation,

                region.seed ^ salt,

                depth

            );

            if (formation) {

                region.formationIds.push(
                    formation.id
                );

            }

        };


        // PRIMARY GEOLOGICAL FORMATIONS
        // Large formations establish the region.

        const primaryCount = 7;

        for (let i = 0; i < primaryCount; i++) {

            const sample = samplePosition();

            if (!sample) continue;

            const config =
                RockSpecies[sample.speciesName];

            addFormation(

                sample,

                config.scale * 4.0,

                Math.min(2, config.recursionDepth),

                i + 1000

            );

        }


        // SECONDARY FORMATIONS
        // Medium-scale geological structures.

        const secondaryCount = 7;

        for (let i = 0; i < secondaryCount; i++) {

            const sample = samplePosition();

            if (!sample) continue;

            const config =
                RockSpecies[sample.speciesName];

            addFormation(

                sample,

                config.scale * 1.7,

                Math.min(2, config.recursionDepth),

                i + 2000

            );

        }


        // FINE GEOLOGICAL DETAIL

        const detailCount = 32;

        for (let i = 0; i < detailCount; i++) {

            const sample = samplePosition();

            if (!sample) continue;

            const config =
                RockSpecies[sample.speciesName];

            addFormation(

                sample,

                config.scale * (
                    0.35 + random() * 0.4
                ),

                Math.min(1, config.recursionDepth),

                i + 3000

            );

        }


        region.generated = true;

        this.renderDirty = true;

    }


    generateAll() {

        const minX = Math.floor(
            this.terrain.min.x / this.regionSize
        );

        const maxX = Math.floor(
            this.terrain.max.x / this.regionSize
        );

        const minZ = Math.floor(
            this.terrain.min.z / this.regionSize
        );

        const maxZ = Math.floor(
            this.terrain.max.z / this.regionSize
        );

        for (let x = minX; x <= maxX; x++) {

            for (let z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++) {

                this.generateRegion(x, z);

            }

        }

	this.surfaceDeposits.generate();

this.rebuildRendering();


    }


    // ---------------------------------
    // GEOLOGICAL EROSION
    // ---------------------------------

    erodeFormation(formation, deltaYears) {

        if (!formation.alive) return false;

        const config =
            RockSpecies[formation.speciesName];

        formation.age += deltaYears;

        // Hardness and cohesion both resist erosion.
        const resistance =
            0.5 + config.hardness * 0.5;

        const cohesionResistance =
            0.5 + config.cohesion * 0.5;

        const erosion =
            config.erosionRate *
            deltaYears /
            (resistance * cohesionResistance);

        formation.erosionProgress += erosion;

        if (formation.erosionProgress < 1.0) {

            return false;

        }

        formation.erosionProgress -= 1.0;

        this.fragmentFormation(formation);

        return true;

    }


    fragmentFormation(parent) {

        const config =
            RockSpecies[parent.speciesName];

        parent.erosionEvents++;

        // Smaller parent after fracturing.
        parent.scale *= 0.72;

        parent.integrity = 1.0;

        const canFragment =

            parent.fragmentDepth <
                this.maxFragmentDepth &&

            parent.scale >
                this.minFragmentScale &&

            parent.erosionEvents <= 3 &&

            this.formations.length <
                this.maxFormations;


        if (!canFragment) {

            if (
                parent.erosionEvents >= 3 ||
                parent.scale <= this.minFragmentScale
            ) {

                parent.alive = false;

            }

            return;

        }


        const random = CreateSeededRandom(
            parent.seed ^
            Math.imul(parent.erosionEvents, 7919)
        );


        const fragmentCount = Math.max(

            2,

            Math.round(
                2 + config.fragmentation * 3
            )

        );


        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < fragmentCount;
            i++
        ) {

            const angle =
                random() * Math.PI * 2;

            const distance =
                parent.scale *
                (1.0 + random() * 2.0) *
                config.fragmentation;

            const x =
                parent.position.x +
                Math.cos(angle) * distance;

            const z =
                parent.position.z +
                Math.sin(angle) * distance;

            const position =
                this.terrain.sampleAt(x, z);

            if (!position) continue;


            const fragmentScale =
                parent.scale *
                (0.25 + random() * 0.18);


            const fragmentSeed =
                (
                    parent.seed ^
                    Math.imul(i + 1, 2654435761) ^
                    parent.erosionEvents
                ) >>> 0;


            this.createFormation(

                parent.speciesName,

                position,

                fragmentScale,

                fragmentSeed,

                parent.fragmentDepth + 1

            );

        }


        // Fine sediment is gradually incorporated
        // into the surrounding ground.
        if (
            parent.fragmentDepth >=
                this.maxFragmentDepth ||
            parent.scale < this.minFragmentScale * 2
        ) {

            parent.alive = false;

        }

    }


    advance(deltaYears) {

        let changed = false;

        // Only process formations that existed
        // at the beginning of this simulation step.
        const initialCount =
            this.formations.length;

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < initialCount;
            i++
        ) {

            const formation =
                this.formations[i];

            if (!formation.alive) continue;

            if (
                this.erodeFormation(
                    formation,
                    deltaYears
                )
            ) {

                changed = true;

            }

        }


        if (changed) {

            this.renderDirty = true;

        }


        this.simulationSteps++;

        if (
            this.renderDirty ||
            this.simulationSteps >=
                this.renderEverySteps
        ) {

            this.rebuildRendering();

            this.simulationSteps = 0;

        }

    }


    // ---------------------------------
    // RENDERING
    // ---------------------------------

    rebuildRendering() {

        for (const speciesName in this.renderers) {

            this.renderers[speciesName].clear();

        }


        for (const formation of this.formations) {

            if (!formation.alive) continue;

            const config =
                RockSpecies[formation.speciesName];

            const generator =
                new RockGenerator(
                    config,
                    formation.seed
                );

            const matrices = generator.generate(

                formation.position,

                formation.scale,

                formation.fragmentDepth === 0
                    ? config.recursionDepth
                    : Math.max(
                        0,
                        config.recursionDepth -
                        formation.fragmentDepth
                    )

            );


            this.renderers[
                formation.speciesName
            ].addMatrices(matrices);

        }


        for (const speciesName in this.renderers) {

            this.renderers[speciesName].update();

        }

        this.renderDirty = false;

    }


    getLivingFormationCount() {

        return this.formations.reduce(

            (count, formation) =>

                count + (formation.alive ? 1 : 0),

            0

        );

    }

	getMaterialAt(position) {

    return this.field.sample(
        position.x,
        position.z
    );

}


getSoilQualityAt(position) {

    const sample = this.getMaterialAt(position);

    if (!sample) return 0.5;

    const quality = {

        fertileSoil: 1.0,

        clay: 0.55,

        limestone: 0.45,

        granite: 0.20,

        sand: 0.12,

        anomaly: 0.65

    };

    return quality[sample.speciesName] ?? 0.5;

}

}
export { GeologicalManager }

src/geology/rock-generator.js
_______
```

import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";

class RecursiveObjectGenerator {
    constructor(config) {
        this.config = config;
        this.matrices = [];
    }

    generate(position, scale, depth) {
        this.matrices = [];

        this.generateRecursive(
            position,
            scale,
            depth,
            0
        );

        return this.matrices;
    }

    generateRecursive() {
        throw new Error(
            "generateRecursive must be implemented"
        );
    }
}

class RockGenerator extends RecursiveObjectGenerator {
    constructor(config, seed = 1) {
        super(config);
        this.random = CreateSeededRandom(seed);
    }

    generateRecursive(
        position,
        scale,
        maxDepth,
        currentDepth
    ) {
        const c = this.config;
        const random = this.random;

        const irregularity = c.irregularity ?? 0.5;

        // Angular, asymmetric rock body.
        const sx = scale * (
            0.75 + random() * irregularity
        );

        const sy = scale * (
            0.65 + random() * irregularity
        ) * (c.verticalScale ?? 1);

        const sz = scale * (
            0.75 + random() * irregularity
        );

        // Fractured formations tend to have tilted faces.
        const rotation =
            BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationYawPitchRoll(
                random() * Math.PI * 2,
                (random() - 0.5) * 0.8,
                (random() - 0.5) * 0.8
            );

        const buriedFraction = currentDepth === 0
            ? 0.30
            : 0.05;

        const center = position.add(
            new BABYLON.Vector3(
                0,
                sy * (0.5 - buriedFraction),
                0
            )
        );

        this.matrices.push(
            BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
                new BABYLON.Vector3(sx, sy, sz),
                rotation,
                center
            )
        );

        if (currentDepth >= maxDepth) {
            return;
        }

        const count = Math.max(
            2,
            Math.round(
                c.branchCount *
                (1 - currentDepth * 0.10)
            )
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const phi = random() * Math.PI * 2;

            // Bias protrusions toward the upper surface.
            const vertical = random() * 1.4 - 0.15;

            const radial = Math.sqrt(
                Math.max(0, 1 - vertical * vertical)
            );

            const direction = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                Math.cos(phi) * radial,
                vertical,
                Math.sin(phi) * radial
            ).normalize();

            const displacement =
                (c.displacement ?? 0.8) *
                (0.65 + random() * 0.55);

            const childPosition = position.add(
                direction.scale(scale * displacement)
            );

            const childScale = scale *
                (c.scaleDecay ?? 0.6) *
                (0.75 + random() * 0.35);

            this.generateRecursive(
                childPosition,
                childScale,
                maxDepth,
                currentDepth + 1
            );
        }
    }
}

export {
    RockGenerator,
    RecursiveObjectGenerator
};

src/geology/surface-deposit-system.js
_______
```
import { SurfaceDepositRenderer } from "../rendering/surface-deposit-renderer.js"
import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";

class SurfaceDepositSystem {

    constructor(scene, terrain, geologicalField) {

        this.scene = scene;

        this.terrain = terrain;

        this.field = geologicalField;

        this.spacing = 28;

        this.seed = 48391;

        this.renderers = {

            sand: new SurfaceDepositRenderer(
                scene,
                "sand",
                new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.76,
                    0.65,
                    0.43
                )
            ),

            clay: new SurfaceDepositRenderer(
                scene,
                "clay",
                new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.48,
                    0.25,
                    0.17
                )
            ),

            fertileSoil: new SurfaceDepositRenderer(
                scene,
                "fertileSoil",
                new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.22,
                    0.16,
                    0.10
                )
            )

        };

        this.generated = false;

    }


    generate() {

        if (this.generated) return;

        for (const speciesName in this.renderers) {

            this.renderers[speciesName].clear();

        }


        const minX = this.terrain.min.x;
        const maxX = this.terrain.max.x;

        const minZ = this.terrain.min.z;
        const maxZ = this.terrain.max.z;


        const startX =
            Math.floor(minX / this.spacing) *
            this.spacing;

        const startZ =
            Math.floor(minZ / this.spacing) *
            this.spacing;


        for (
            let x = startX;
            x <= maxX;
            x += this.spacing
        ) {

            for (
                let z = startZ;
                z <= maxZ;
                z += this.spacing
            ) {

                const cellX = Math.floor(
                    x / this.spacing
                );

                const cellZ = Math.floor(
                    z / this.spacing
                );

                const seed = (

                    this.seed ^

                    Math.imul(cellX, 73856093) ^

                    Math.imul(cellZ, 19349663)

                ) >>> 0;

                const random =
                    CreateSeededRandom(seed);


                // Small deterministic displacement.
                const sampleX =
                    x + (random() - 0.5) *
                    this.spacing * 0.5;

                const sampleZ =
                    z + (random() - 0.5) *
                    this.spacing * 0.5;


                const sample = this.field.sample(
                    sampleX,
                    sampleZ
                );

                if (!sample) continue;


                const speciesName =
                    sample.speciesName;

const renderer =
    this.renderers[speciesName];

if (!renderer) continue;

// Surface deposits should not cover steep terrain.
if (sample.slopeDegrees > 12) {
    continue;
}

// Avoid large deposits at high elevations.
if (
    sample.elevation > 0.72 &&
    speciesName !== "clay"
) {
    continue;
}

const baseRadius =
    this.spacing *
    (0.85 + random() * 0.45);

                // Slightly flatten and vary each deposit.
                const radiusX =
                    baseRadius *
                    (0.85 + random() * 0.35);

                const radiusZ =
                    baseRadius *
                    (0.85 + random() * 0.35);

                const rotation =
                    random() * Math.PI * 2;


                renderer.addPatch(

                    sample.position,

                    radiusX,

                    radiusZ,

                    rotation,

                    0.025 + random() * 0.025,
			sample.normal

                );


                // Secondary overlapping patches
                // create less regular boundaries.
                if (random() > 0.35) {

                    const offsetX =
                        (random() - 0.5) *
                        this.spacing * 0.65;

                    const offsetZ =
                        (random() - 0.5) *
                        this.spacing * 0.65;

                    const secondaryPosition =
                        this.terrain.sampleAt(
                            sampleX + offsetX,
                            sampleZ + offsetZ
                        );

			const secondaryProfile =
    this.terrain.sampleProfileAt(
        sampleX + offsetX,
        sampleZ + offsetZ
    );

if (
    secondaryProfile &&
    secondaryProfile.slopeDegrees < 12
) {

    renderer.addPatch(
        secondaryProfile.position,
        radiusX * 0.5,
        radiusZ * 0.5,
        random() * Math.PI * 2,
        0.02,
        secondaryProfile.normal
    );

}

                }

            }

        }


        this.update();

        this.generated = true;

    }


    update() {

        for (const speciesName in this.renderers) {

            this.renderers[speciesName].update();

        }

    }


}
export { SurfaceDepositSystem }
```

src/rendering/thin-instance-renderer.js
_______
```

class ThinInstanceRenderer {
    constructor(scene, name, mesh, shadowGenerator) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.mesh = mesh;
        this.matrices = [];
        this.buffer = null;

        this.localMatrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Identity();

        this.mesh.position.y = -10000;
        this.mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);

        this.inverseMeshWorld =
            this.mesh.getWorldMatrix().clone();

        this.inverseMeshWorld.invert();

        this.mesh.isPickable = false;

        if (shadowGenerator) {
            shadowGenerator.addShadowCaster(
                this.mesh,
                true
            );
        }
    }

    clear() {
        this.matrices.length = 0;
    }

    addMatrix(matrix) {
        this.matrices.push(matrix);
    }

    addMatrices(matrices) {
        this.matrices.push(...matrices);
    }

    add(
        position,
        scale,
        rotation = BABYLON.Quaternion.Identity()
    ) {
        const matrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
            new BABYLON.Vector3(scale, scale, scale),
            rotation,
            position
        );

        this.matrices.push(matrix);
    }

    update() {
        const geometryRenderer =
            this.scene.metadata?.geometryRenderer;

        const capacity = this.matrices.length * 16;

        // Retain buffer capacity between updates.
        if (
            !this.buffer ||
            this.buffer.length < capacity
        ) {
            this.buffer = new Float32Array(
                Math.max(1024, capacity)
            );
        }

        let writeIndex = 0;

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < this.matrices.length;
            i++
        ) {
            const sourceMatrix = this.matrices[i];

            if (!sourceMatrix || !sourceMatrix.m) {
                continue;
            }

            // Validate directly without allocating an array.
            const values = sourceMatrix.m;
            let valid = true;

            for (let j = 0; j < 16; j++) {
                if (!Number.isFinite(values[j])) {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (!valid) continue;

            const transformedMatrix =
                geometryRenderer
                    ? geometryRenderer.transformInstanceMatrix(
                        sourceMatrix
                    )
                    : sourceMatrix;

            if (!transformedMatrix) continue;

            transformedMatrix.multiplyToRef(
                this.inverseMeshWorld,
                this.localMatrix
            );

            this.localMatrix.copyToArray(
                this.buffer,
                writeIndex * 16
            );

            writeIndex++;
        }

        const requiredSize = writeIndex * 16;

        this.mesh.thinInstanceSetBuffer(
            "matrix",
            this.buffer.subarray(0, requiredSize),
            16,
            true
        );

        this.mesh.thinInstanceRefreshBoundingInfo(true);
    }
}

export { ThinInstanceRenderer };

```

src/rendering/surface-deposit-renderer.js
_______
```
import { DirectionRotation } from "../ecology/tree-generator.js";

class SurfaceDepositRenderer {

    constructor(scene, speciesName, color) {

        this.scene = scene;

        this.speciesName = speciesName;

        this.mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateCylinder(
            "surface_" + speciesName,
            {
                height: 1,
                diameter: 2,
                tessellation: 16
            },
            scene
        );

        this.mesh.position.y = -10000;

        this.mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);

        this.inverseMeshWorld =
            this.mesh.getWorldMatrix().clone();

        this.inverseMeshWorld.invert();


        const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "surfaceMaterial_" + speciesName,
            scene
        );

        material.diffuseColor = color.clone();

        material.specularColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0, 0, 0);

        material.roughness = 1;

        this.mesh.material = material;

        this.mesh.receiveShadows = true;

        this.matrices = [];

        this.buffer = null;

    }


    clear() {

        this.matrices.length = 0;

    }


addPatch(
    position,
    radiusX,
    radiusZ,
    rotation = 0,
    thickness = 0.04,
    normal = BABYLON.Axis.Y
) {
        const scale = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            radiusX,
            thickness,
            radiusZ
        );

const up = normal.normalize();

const yaw = BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationAxis(
    up,
    rotation
);

	const align = DirectionRotation(normal);


const quaternion = align.multiply(yaw);

	const center = position.add(
		normal.normalize().scale(thickness * 0.5)
	);

        const matrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
            scale,
            quaternion,
            center
        );

        this.matrices.push(matrix);

    }


    update() {

        const requiredSize =
            this.matrices.length * 16;

        if (
            !this.buffer ||
            this.buffer.length < requiredSize
        ) {

            this.buffer = new Float32Array(
                Math.max(
                    requiredSize,
                    this.buffer
                        ? this.buffer.length * 2
                        : 1024
                )
            );

        }


        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < this.matrices.length;
            i++
        ) {

            const localMatrix =
                this.matrices[i].multiply(
                    this.inverseMeshWorld
                );

            localMatrix.copyToArray(
                this.buffer,
                i * 16
            );

        }


        this.mesh.thinInstanceSetBuffer(
            "matrix",
            this.buffer.subarray(
                0,
                requiredSize
            ),
            16,
            true
        );

        this.mesh.thinInstanceRefreshBoundingInfo(true);

    }

}
export { SurfaceDepositRenderer }
```

src/rendering/cloud-renderer.js
_______
```
import { ThinInstanceRenderer } from "./thin-instance-renderer.js";

class CloudRenderer extends ThinInstanceRenderer {
    constructor(scene) {
        const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
            "cloudPrototype",
            {
                diameter: 1,
                segments: 10
            },
            scene
        );

        const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "cloudMaterial",
            scene
        );

        material.diffuseColor =
            BABYLON.Color3.White();

        material.emissiveColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(
                0.035,
                0.035,
                0.045
            );

        material.specularColor =
            BABYLON.Color3.Black();

        material.useVertexColor = true;
        material.useVertexAlpha = false;

        material.backFaceCulling = false;

        mesh.material = material;
        mesh.isPickable = false;

        super(scene, "clouds", mesh, null);

        this.instanceColors = [];

        this.cloudCount = 0;
    }

    clear() {
        super.clear();

        this.instanceColors.length = 0;
        this.cloudCount = 0;
    }

    getCloudColor(
        speciesName,
        moistureRatio = 0,
        relativeHeight = 0.5
    ) {
        const moisture = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            moistureRatio,
            0,
            1
        );

        let color;

        switch (speciesName) {
            case "cumulonimbus":
                color = BABYLON.Color3.Lerp(
                    new BABYLON.Color3(
                        0.24, 0.29, 0.39
                    ),
                    new BABYLON.Color3(
                        0.89, 0.92, 0.98
                    ),
                    BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                        relativeHeight,
                        0,
                        1
                    )
                );
                break;

            case "towering":
                color = new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.82, 0.87, 0.95
                );
                break;

            default:
                color = new BABYLON.Color3(
                    0.98, 0.99, 1.0
                );
                break;
        }

        // Saturated clouds become darker.
        const brightness =
            1.0 - moisture * 0.30;

        color.scaleInPlace(brightness);

        return color;
    }

    addMatrix(
        matrix,
        speciesName = "cumulus",
        moistureRatio = 0,
        relativeHeight = 0.5
    ) {
        super.addMatrix(matrix);

        const color = this.getCloudColor(
            speciesName,
            moistureRatio,
            relativeHeight
        );

        this.instanceColors.push(
            color.r,
            color.g,
            color.b,
            1.0
        );

        this.cloudCount++;
    }

    addCloud(
        matrices,
        moistureRatio = 0,
        speciesName = "cumulus",
        cloudPosition = null
    ) {
        for (const matrix of matrices) {
            const position = matrix.getTranslation();

            const relativeHeight = cloudPosition
                ? BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                    (position.y - cloudPosition.y) /
                    Math.max(1, 1500),
                    0,
                    1
                )
                : 0.5;

            this.addMatrix(
                matrix,
                speciesName,
                moistureRatio,
                relativeHeight
            );
        }
    }

    addMatrices(matrices) {
        this.addCloud(matrices);
    }

    update() {
        // Transform and upload instance matrices.
        super.update();

        const expectedColorLength =
            this.matrices.length * 4;

        if (this.instanceColors.length !== expectedColorLength) {
            console.warn(
                "[CloudRenderer] Instance color buffer mismatch:",
                {
                    matrices: this.matrices.length,
                    colors: this.instanceColors.length / 4
                }
            );

            return;
        }

        const colors = new Float32Array(
            this.instanceColors
        );

        this.mesh.thinInstanceSetBuffer(
            "color",
            colors,
            4,
            true
        );

        this.mesh.thinInstanceRefreshBoundingInfo(true);
    }
}

export { CloudRenderer };
```

src/rendering/adaptive-graphics-controller.js
_______
```

export class AdaptiveGraphicsController {
    constructor(engine, camera, world, options = {}) {
        this.engine = engine;
        this.camera = camera;
        this.world = world;

        this.targetFPS = options.targetFPS ?? 55;
        this.minimumFPS = options.minimumFPS ?? 35;

        this.profiles = {
            low: {
                radius: 7,
                detailRadius: 1,
                generationBudget: 3,
                hardwareScale: 1.5
            },

            medium: {
                radius: 14,
                detailRadius: 2,
                generationBudget: 6,
                hardwareScale: 1.0
            },

            high: {
                radius: 18,
                detailRadius: 2,
                generationBudget: 8,
                hardwareScale: 0.85
            }
        };

        this.profileName = this.detectInitialProfile();

        this.fpsHistory = [];
        this.historyLimit = 30;

        this.elapsed = 0;
        this.lowFPSDuration = 0;
        this.highFPSDuration = 0;

        this.setProfile(this.profileName);

        console.log(
            "[Graphics] Initialized:",
            this.getStats()
        );
    }

    detectInitialProfile() {
        const cores = navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 4;
        const memory = navigator.deviceMemory || 4;

        const mobile =
            /Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod/i.test(
                navigator.userAgent
            );

        if (cores <= 2 || memory <= 2) {
            return "low";
        }

        if (
            cores >= 8 &&
            memory >= 8 &&
            !mobile
        ) {
            return "high";
        }

        return "medium";
    }

    setProfile(name) {
        const profile = this.profiles[name];

        if (!profile) return;

        this.profileName = name;

        this.world.renderRadius = profile.radius;

        this.world.highDetailRadius =
            profile.detailRadius;

        this.world.maxChunksPerUpdate =
            profile.generationBudget;

        this.camera.maxZ =
            this.world.chunkSize *
            (profile.radius + 3) * 2;

        this.engine.setHardwareScalingLevel(
            profile.hardwareScale
        );

        console.log("[Graphics] Profile:", name);
    }

    getAverageFPS() {
        if (!this.fpsHistory.length) {
            return this.engine.getFps();
        }

        return this.fpsHistory.reduce(
            (sum, fps) => sum + fps,
            0
        ) / this.fpsHistory.length;
    }

    update(deltaSeconds) {
        this.elapsed += deltaSeconds;

        if (this.elapsed < 2) return;

        const elapsed = this.elapsed;
        this.elapsed = 0;

        const fps = this.engine.getFps();

        if (Number.isFinite(fps) && fps > 0) {
            this.fpsHistory.push(fps);

            if (this.fpsHistory.length > this.historyLimit) {
                this.fpsHistory.shift();
            }
        }

        const average = this.getAverageFPS();

        if (average < this.minimumFPS) {
            this.lowFPSDuration += elapsed;
            this.highFPSDuration = 0;
        } else if (average > this.targetFPS) {
            this.highFPSDuration += elapsed;
            this.lowFPSDuration = 0;
        } else {
            this.lowFPSDuration = Math.max(
                0,
                this.lowFPSDuration - elapsed
            );

            this.highFPSDuration = 0;
        }

        if (this.lowFPSDuration >= 6) {
            if (this.profileName === "high") {
                this.setProfile("medium");
            } else if (this.profileName === "medium") {
                this.setProfile("low");
            }

            this.lowFPSDuration = 0;
            this.highFPSDuration = 0;
        }

        if (this.highFPSDuration >= 25) {
            if (this.profileName === "low") {
                this.setProfile("medium");
            } else if (this.profileName === "medium") {
                this.setProfile("high");
            }

            this.highFPSDuration = 0;
            this.lowFPSDuration = 0;
        }
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            profile: this.profileName,
            renderRadius: this.world.renderRadius,
            averageFPS: Math.round(this.getAverageFPS()),
            targetFPS: this.targetFPS,
            minimumFPS: this.minimumFPS
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        this.fpsHistory.length = 0;
    }
}

```

src/core/genetics.js

```
const Genetics = {

    createGenome() {

        return {

            growthRate: 1.0,
            maxHeight: 1.0,

            // Structural characteristics
            trunkThickness: 1.0,
            branchDensity: 1.0,
            branchThickness: 1.0,
            branchTaper: 0.75,
            branchAngle: 1.0,

            // Foliage characteristics
            leafDensity: 1.0,
            canopyWidth: 1.0,
            leafSize: 1.0,

            // Environmental characteristics
            lightSensitivity: 1.0,

            seedProduction: 1.0,
            lifespan: 1.0
        };

    },


    inherit(parentGenome, mutationRate = 0.8) {

        const child = {};

        for (const gene in parentGenome) {

            const mutation = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * mutationRate;

            child[gene] = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                parentGenome[gene] * (1 + mutation),
                0.1,
                1.5
            );
        }

        return child;
    }

};
export { Genetics };

src/core/noise.js
_______
```
function hashNoise2D(x, z, seed = 1) {

    let n =
        Math.imul(x | 0, 374761393) +
        Math.imul(z | 0, 668265263) +
        Math.imul(seed | 0, 1442695041);

    n = Math.imul(
        n ^ (n >>> 13),
        1274126177
    );

    n ^= n >>> 16;

    return (n >>> 0) / 4294967295;

}


function smoothNoise2D(x, z, seed = 1) {

    const ix = Math.floor(x);
    const iz = Math.floor(z);

    const fx = x - ix;
    const fz = z - iz;

    const u = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx);
    const v = fz * fz * (3 - 2 * fz);

    const a = hashNoise2D(ix, iz, seed);
    const b = hashNoise2D(ix + 1, iz, seed);
    const c = hashNoise2D(ix, iz + 1, seed);
    const d = hashNoise2D(ix + 1, iz + 1, seed);

    const ab = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(a, b, u);
    const cd = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(c, d, u);

    return BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(ab, cd, v);

}


function fractalNoise2D(
    x,
    z,
    seed,
    octaves = 4
) {

    let value = 0;
    let amplitude = 1;
    let frequency = 1;
    let totalAmplitude = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {

        value += smoothNoise2D(
            x * frequency,
            z * frequency,
            seed + i * 1013
        ) * amplitude;

        totalAmplitude += amplitude;

        amplitude *= 0.5;
        frequency *= 2;

    }

    return value / totalAmplitude;

}
export { hashNoise2D, smoothNoise2D, fractalNoise2D };
```

src/core/random.js
_______
```
function CreateSeededRandom(seed) {

    let state = seed >>> 0;

    return function() {

        state += 0x6D2B79F5;

        let t = state;

        t = Math.imul(
            t ^ (t >>> 15),
            t | 1
        );

        t ^= t + Math.imul(
            t ^ (t >>> 7),
            t | 61
        );

        return (
            (t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0
        ) / 4294967296;

    };

}
export { CreateSeededRandom }
```

class MainMenu {
    constructor(gameManager) {
        this.gameManager = gameManager;
        this.container = null;

        this.create();

        this.unsubscribe = gameManager.onModeChanged(
            mode => this.update(mode)
        );

        this.update(gameManager.mode);
    }

    create() {
        this.container = document.createElement("div");
        this.container.id = "somnium-game-ui";

        Object.assign(this.container.style, {
            position: "fixed",
            inset: "0",
            zIndex: "1000",
            pointerEvents: "none",
            fontFamily: "Arial, sans-serif",
            color: "white"
        });

        this.container.innerHTML = `
            <style>
                #somnium-game-ui button {
                    color: white;

                    padding: 14px 22px;

                }

                }

                }

                    min-height: 125px;

                    display: block;

                }

                #somnium-hud {
                    position: absolute;
                    top: 18px;
                    right: 18px;
                    display: none;
                    gap: 8px;
                    pointer-events: auto;
                }

                @media(max-width:520px) {
                    .somnium-options {
                        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
                    }
                }
            </style>

            <div id="somnium-menu" class="somnium-overlay">
                <div class="somnium-panel">
                    <h1 style="font-size:64px;letter-spacing:12px;font-weight:300">
                        SOMNIUM
                    </h1>

                    <p style="color:#9aabbc;letter-spacing:3px">
                        CHOOSE YOUR PERSPECTIVE
                    </p>

                    <div class="somnium-options">
                        <button id="somnium-play-character">
                            <strong>THIRD-PERSON</strong>
                            <small>Explore as a character</small>
                        </button>

                        <button id="somnium-play-flight">
                            <strong>FLIGHT SIMULATOR</strong>
                            <small>Explore freely from above</small>
                        </button>

                        <button id="somnium-play-editor">
                            <strong>BUILDING EDITOR</strong>
                            <small>Design and position architecture</small>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div id="somnium-pause" class="somnium-overlay">
                <div class="somnium-panel">
                    <h1>PAUSED</h1>
                    <button id="somnium-resume">RESUME</button>
                    <button id="somnium-return">MAIN MENU</button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div id="somnium-hud">
                <button id="somnium-switch">SWITCH VIEW (V)</button>
                <button id="somnium-pause-button">PAUSE (ESC)</button>
            </div>
        `;

        document.body.appendChild(this.container);

        this.menu = this.container.querySelector("#somnium-menu");
        this.pausePanel = this.container.querySelector("#somnium-pause");
        this.hud = this.container.querySelector("#somnium-hud");

        this.container.querySelector(
            "#somnium-play-character"
        ).onclick = () => this.gameManager.startThirdPerson();

        this.container.querySelector(
            "#somnium-play-flight"
        ).onclick = () => this.gameManager.startFlight();

        this.container.querySelector(
            "#somnium-play-editor"
        ).onclick = () => this.gameManager.startEditor();

        this.container.querySelector(
            "#somnium-switch"
        ).onclick = () => this.gameManager.togglePerspective();

        this.container.querySelector(
            "#somnium-pause-button"
        ).onclick = () => this.gameManager.pause();

        this.container.querySelector(
            "#somnium-resume"
        ).onclick = () => this.gameManager.resume();

        this.container.querySelector(
            "#somnium-return"
        ).onclick = () => this.gameManager.returnToMenu();
    }

    update(mode) {
        this.menu.style.display =
            mode === "menu" ? "flex" : "none";

        this.pausePanel.style.display =
            mode === "paused" ? "flex" : "none";

        this.hud.style.display =
            mode === "flight" || mode === "thirdPerson"
                ? "flex"
                : "none";
    }

    dispose() {
        this.unsubscribe?.();
        this.container?.remove();
    }
}

export { MainMenu };
