src/ecology/tree.js
_______
```
import { Species } from "../core/species.js";
import { Genetics } from "../core/genetics.js";
import { TreeGenerator } from "./tree-generator.js";
let nextTreeId = 0;

class Tree {

	constructor(
    speciesName,
    position,
    genome = Genetics.createGenome(),
    parentId = null,
    initialAge = 0
) {

    this.id = nextTreeId++;

    this.speciesName = speciesName;

    this.species = Species[speciesName];

    this.position = position.clone();

    this.genome = genome;

    this.parentId = parentId;

    this.age = initialAge;

    this.seedTimer = 0;

this.soilSample = null;
this.soilSampleTimer = 0;
this.soilSuitability = 1.0;
    // Environmental conditions

    this.lightLevel = 1.0;

    this.lightStress = 0;

    this.lowLightYears = 0;

    this.growthMultiplier = 1.0;


    // Hydrological conditions

    this.soilMoisture = 0.35;

    this.moistureMultiplier = 1.0;

    this.droughtYears = 0;

    this.waterloggingYears = 0;


    this.children = [];

    this.alive = true;

}

	get thicknessFactor() {

    return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        this.age / (this.maturityAge * 2),
        0,
        1
    );
}

    get maturityAge() {

        return 10 / this.genome.growthRate;
    }


    get maximumAge() {

        return 100 * this.genome.lifespan;
    }


    get ageFactor() {

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            this.age / this.maturityAge,
            0,
            1
        );
    }


    get isMature() {

        return this.age >= this.maturityAge;
    }


    get isElder() {

        return this.age >= this.maximumAge * 0.85;
    }



update(deltaYears, environment, hydrology) {

    if (!this.alive) {
        return;
    }


    // --------------------------------
    // LIGHT CONDITIONS
    // --------------------------------

    const light = environment.sampleLight(
        this.position
    );

    this.lightLevel = light.intensity;


    const minimumLight =
        this.species.lightSensitivity * 0.20;


    this.growthMultiplier =
        BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(

            0.25 + this.lightLevel * 0.75,

            0.25,

            1.0

        );


    if (this.lightLevel < minimumLight) {

        this.lowLightYears += deltaYears;

    } else {

        this.lowLightYears = Math.max(

            0,

            this.lowLightYears - deltaYears * 0.5

        );

    }


    if (this.lowLightYears >= 15) {

        this.alive = false;

        return;

    }


    // --------------------------------
    // SOIL MOISTURE
    // --------------------------------

	this.soilSampleTimer -= deltaYears;

if (
    hydrology &&
    this.soilSampleTimer <= 0
) {
    this.soilSample = hydrology.sampleSoil(
        this.position
    );

    this.soilSampleTimer = 5.0;
}

	const optimalMoisture = this.species.optimalMoisture;


	const droughtTolerance = this.species.droughtTolerance;


	const waterloggingTolerance = this.species.waterloggingTolerance;

if (hydrology && typeof hydrology.sampleSoil === "function") {
	const soil = this.soilSample;

    if (soil) {
        const affinity = this.species.soilAffinity;

        const textureScore = affinity.preferred.includes(
            soil.surfaceSoil
        ) ? 1.0 : affinity.tolerance;

        const fertilityScore = 1.0 - Math.abs(
            soil.fertility - affinity.fertility
        );

        const drainageScore = 1.0 - Math.abs(
            soil.drainage - affinity.drainage
        );

        const moistureScore = 1.0 - Math.abs(
            this.soilMoisture - affinity.moisture
        );

        this.soilSuitability = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            textureScore * 0.40 +
            fertilityScore * 0.20 +
            drainageScore * 0.20 +
            moistureScore * 0.20,
            0.15,
            1.0
        );
	    }
	}




    // Growth is best near the species'
    // preferred soil moisture level.

    this.moistureMultiplier =
        BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(

            0.45 +
            0.55 * Math.min(
                1,
                this.soilMoisture /
                optimalMoisture
            ),

            0.45,

            1.0

        );


    // Drought stress

    if (
        this.soilMoisture <
        droughtTolerance
    ) {

        this.droughtYears += deltaYears;

    } else {

        this.droughtYears = Math.max(

            0,

            this.droughtYears - deltaYears * 0.5

        );

    }


    // Waterlogging stress

    if (
        this.soilMoisture >
        waterloggingTolerance
    ) {

        this.waterloggingYears += deltaYears;

    } else {

        this.waterloggingYears = Math.max(

            0,

            this.waterloggingYears - deltaYears * 0.5

        );

    }


    // Prolonged environmental stress.

    if (
        this.droughtYears >= 20 ||
        this.waterloggingYears >= 15
    ) {

        this.alive = false;

        return;

    }


    // --------------------------------
    // COMBINED GROWTH
    // --------------------------------

const combinedGrowth =
    this.growthMultiplier *
    this.moistureMultiplier *
    this.soilSuitability;

    this.age += deltaYears * combinedGrowth;


    if (this.age >= this.maximumAge) {

        this.alive = false;

    }


    this.seedTimer += deltaYears;

}


    generateGeometry(environment) {

        const generator = new TreeGenerator(
            this.species,
            environment,
            this.genome
        );

        return generator.generate(
            this.position,
            this.ageFactor
        );
    }

	
produceSeeds() {

    if (!this.alive || !this.isMature || this.isElder) {
        return 0;
    }

    // One reproductive opportunity every five years
    const seedInterval = 5;

    if (this.seedTimer < seedInterval) {
        return 0;
    }

    this.seedTimer -= seedInterval;

    // Genetic influence on reproduction
    const seedProduction = this.genome.seedProduction;

    // Number of seeds produced in this event
    return Math.max(
        1,
        Math.round(2 * seedProduction)
    );
}


}
export { Tree }
```

src/ecology/tree-generator.js
_______
```
function generateCanopy(tree, ageFactor, thicknessFactor = ageFactor) {

    const species = tree.species;
    const genome = tree.genome;

    const canopy = species.canopy;

    const height =
        species.trunkLength *
        ageFactor *
        genome.maxHeight;

    const growth = Math.max(0.04, ageFactor);

    const centerY = tree.position.y + height * (canopy.startHeight + canopy.height * 0.5);

    const canopyHeight =
        height * canopy.height;

    const canopyWidth =
        height *
        canopy.width *
        genome.canopyWidth *
        growth;

    const clusters = [];

    const layers = canopy.layers;

    for (let layer = 0; layer < layers; layer++) {

        const t = layers === 1
            ? 0.5
            : layer / (layers - 1);

        const y = centerY +
            (t - 0.5) * canopyHeight;

        let layerRadius;

        if (canopy.shape === "round") {

            // Broad crown: widest around the middle
            layerRadius =
                canopyWidth *
                Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 -
                    Math.pow(2 * t - 1, 2)));

        } else {

            // Conical crown: widest at the bottom
            layerRadius =
                canopyWidth * (1 - t) * 0.95 +
                canopyWidth * 0.05;

        }

        const count = Math.max(
            3,
            Math.round(8 * canopy.density)
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

            const angle =
                2 * Math.PI * i / count +
                layer * 0.35;

            const radius =
                layerRadius *
                (0.65 + Math.random() * 0.35);

            const position = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                tree.position.x +
                    Math.cos(angle) * radius,

                y,

                tree.position.z +
                    Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );

            const leafScale =
                Math.max(0.04, height * 0.055) *
                genome.leafSize *
                growth;

            clusters.push({
                position,
                scale: leafScale
            });
        }
    }

    return clusters;
}

function DirectionRotation(direction) {

    const up = BABYLON.Axis.Y;

    const dir = direction.normalize();

    const dot = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(up, dir),
        -1,
        1
    );

    if (dot > 0.99999) {
        return BABYLON.Quaternion.Identity();
    }

    if (dot < -0.99999) {
        return BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationAxis(
            BABYLON.Axis.X,
            Math.PI
        );
    }

    const axis = BABYLON.Vector3.Cross(
        up,
        dir
    ).normalize();

    return BABYLON.Quaternion.RotationAxis(
        axis,
        Math.acos(dot)
    );
}



function makeBranchMatrix(
    start,
    direction,
    length,
    baseRadius
) {

    const dir = direction.normalize();

    const rotation = DirectionRotation(dir);

    const center = start.add(
        dir.scale(length / 2)
    );

    // Prototype bottom radius is 0.5
    const radialScale = baseRadius / 0.5;

    const scale = new BABYLON.Vector3(
        radialScale,
        length,
        radialScale
    );

    return BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
        scale,
        rotation,
        center
    );
}


// Create a coordinate system perpendicular to a direction
function getRadialDirection(direction, phi) {

    const reference =
        Math.abs(direction.y) < 0.95
            ? BABYLON.Axis.Y
            : BABYLON.Axis.X;

    const tangent = BABYLON.Vector3.Cross(
        direction,
        reference
    ).normalize();

    const bitangent = BABYLON.Vector3.Cross(
        direction,
        tangent
    ).normalize();

    return tangent.scale(Math.cos(phi))
        .add(bitangent.scale(Math.sin(phi)))
        .normalize();
}

class TreeGenerator {

    constructor(species, environment, genome) {

        this.species = species;
        this.environment = environment;
        this.genome = genome;
	this.currentAgeFactor = 0;

        this.matrices = [];
	this.foliage = [];
    }


generate(
    position,
    ageFactor = 1,
    thicknessFactor = 1,
    detailLevel = 2
) {

	this.matrices = [];
	this.foliage = [];
	this.currentAgeFactor = ageFactor;


const growth = ageFactor * ageFactor *
    (3 - 2 * ageFactor);

const s = this.species;
const g = this.genome;

// Height
const trunkLength =
    s.trunkLength * growth * g.maxHeight;

// Thickness
const trunkRadius =
    s.trunkRadius *
    Math.sqrt(Math.max(ageFactor, 0.001)) *
    g.trunkThickness;

// Branch dimensions
const branchLength =
    s.branchLength * growth * g.maxHeight;

const branchRadius =
    s.branchRadius *
    Math.sqrt(Math.max(ageFactor, 0.001)) *
    g.branchThickness;
//generate the trunk
this.matrices.push(
    makeBranchMatrix(
        position,
        BABYLON.Axis.Y,
        trunkLength,
        trunkRadius
    )
);
        const trunkEnd = position.add(
            BABYLON.Axis.Y.scale(trunkLength)
        );
	const canopyScale =
    growth *
    this.genome.canopyWidth *
    this.genome.leafSize;

	// Simplified distant canopy
if (detailLevel === 0 && ageFactor >= 0.2) {

    this.foliage.push({

        position: trunkEnd.clone(),

        scale: Math.max(
            0.2,
            canopyScale * 2.0
        )

    });

}


if (ageFactor < 0.2) {

    this.foliage.push({
        position: trunkEnd.clone(),
        scale: Math.max(0.08, canopyScale * 1.2)
    });

}

        // Branch complexity increases with age
let maxDepth =
    ageFactor < 0.2 ? 0 :
    ageFactor < 0.5 ? 1 :
    s.maxDepth;

// Distance-based geometry reduction
if (detailLevel === 1) {

    maxDepth = Math.min(maxDepth, 1);

} else if (detailLevel === 0) {

    maxDepth = 0;

}
        this.growBranch(
            trunkEnd,
            BABYLON.Axis.Y,
            branchLength,
            branchRadius,
            0,
            maxDepth
        );

        return this.matrices;
    }


    growBranch(
        start,
        parentDirection,
        length,
        radius,
        depth,
        maxDepth
    ) {

        if (depth >= maxDepth) {
            return;
        }

        const s = this.species;
        const g = this.genome;

        const branchCount = Math.max(
            1,
            Math.round(s.branchCount * g.branchDensity)
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < branchCount; i++) {

            const phi =
                2 * Math.PI * i / branchCount;

            const radial = getRadialDirection(
                parentDirection,
                phi
            );

            const angle =
                s.branchAngle * g.branchAngle;

            const intrinsicDirection =
                parentDirection.scale(
                    Math.cos(angle)
                ).add(
                    radial.scale(
                        Math.sin(angle)
                    )
                ).normalize();

            const light =
                this.environment.sampleLight(start);

            const direction = BABYLON.Vector3.Lerp(
                intrinsicDirection,
                light.direction,
                s.lightSensitivity *
                g.lightSensitivity *
                light.intensity
            ).normalize();

this.matrices.push(
    makeBranchMatrix(
        start,
        direction,
        length,
        radius
    )
);
            const end = start.add(
                direction.scale(length)
            );

		const ageFactor = this.currentAgeFactor;

let foliageScale =
    (0.25 + ageFactor * 0.75) *
    this.genome.leafSize *
    this.genome.canopyWidth;

if (s.branching === "radial") {

    // Oak: broad, substantial foliage clusters
    foliageScale *= 1.5;

} else if (s.branching === "whorled") {

    // Pine: smaller, more compact clusters
    foliageScale *= 1.15;

}

if ( depth === maxDepth - 1 ) {

    this.foliage.push({
        position: end.clone(),
        scale: foliageScale
    });

}

if (s.branching === "whorled") {

    const leader = BABYLON.Vector3.Lerp(
        parentDirection,
        light.direction,
        s.lightSensitivity *
        g.lightSensitivity *
        light.intensity
    ).normalize();

    // Keep the visual leader branch
    this.matrices.push(
        makeBranchMatrix(
            end,
            leader,
            length * s.lengthDecay,
            radius * s.radiusDecay
        )
    );

    // Do not recursively grow another leader branch.
}

// Continue normal branch growth
this.growBranch(
    end,
    direction,
    length * s.lengthDecay,
    radius * s.radiusDecay,
    depth + 1,
    maxDepth
);
        }
    }

}
export { TreeGenerator , DirectionRotation }
```

src/ecology/forest.js
_______
```
import { Environment } from "../environment/environment.js";
import { TerrainSampler } from "../environment/terrain.js";
import { SpatialGrid } from "../environment/spatial-grid.js";
import { GeologicalManager } from "../geology/geological-manager.js";
import { GeologicalField } from "../geology/geological-field.js";
import { TreeRenderer, FoliageRenderer } from "../rendering/tree-renderers.js";
import { RockRenderer } from "../rendering/rock-renderer.js";
import { Tree } from "./tree.js";
import { TreeGenerator } from "./tree-generator.js";
import { Genetics } from "../core/genetics.js";
import { CloudSpecies } from  "../atmosphere/cloud-species.js";
import { CloudGenerator } from "../atmosphere/cloud-generator.js";
import { WaterSystem } from "../water/water-system.js";
import { Hydrology } from "../water/hydrology.js";
import { RainSystem } from "../water/rain-system.js";
import { CloudSystem } from "../atmosphere/cloud-system.js";
import { WorldFieldSystem } from "../world/fields/world-field-system.js";

class Forest {


	constructor(
    scene,
    ground,
    sunDirection,
    shadowGenerator,
    seaLevel = -100
) {
        this.scene = scene;

	this.shadowGenerator = shadowGenerator;

        this.environment = new Environment(sunDirection);

        this.terrain = new TerrainSampler(scene, ground);

	this.lastRenderTime = 0;
	this.renderInterval = 5;
	this.simulationSteps = 0;
	this.renderEverySteps = 3;
	this.renderDirty = true;

        this.minSpacing = 2.5;
	this.spatialGrid = new SpatialGrid(this.minSpacing);
	
        this.trees = [];


	this.geology = new GeologicalManager( scene, this.terrain, shadowGenerator );
	// Initialize centralized environmental fields.

this.worldFields = new WorldFieldSystem({

    terrain: this.terrain,

    geologicalField: this.geology.field

});

        this.renderers = {

oak: new TreeRenderer(
    scene,
    "oakBranches",
    shadowGenerator
),

pine: new TreeRenderer(
    scene,
    "pineBranches",
    shadowGenerator
)
        };
	
this.rockRenderer = new RockRenderer(
    scene,
    shadowGenerator
);



	// Initialize the water system.

this.waterSystem = new WaterSystem(
    scene,
    this.terrain,
    -100
);


// Initialize soil hydrology.

this.hydrology = new Hydrology(
    this.terrain,
    this.waterSystem,
    20
);


// Initialize precipitation rendering.

this.rainSystem = new RainSystem(
    scene
);


// Initialize atmospheric simulation.

this.cloudSystem = new CloudSystem(
    scene,
    this.terrain,
    this.waterSystem,
    this.hydrology,
    this.rainSystem
);

	this.foliageRenderers = {
oak: new FoliageRenderer(
    scene,
    "oakLeaves",
    new BABYLON.Color3(0.18, 0.22, 0.12),
    shadowGenerator
),

pine: new FoliageRenderer(
    scene,
    "pineLeaves",
    new BABYLON.Color3(0.12, 0.32, 0.16),
    shadowGenerator
)

};

        const oakMaterial = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "oakMaterial",
            scene
        );

        oakMaterial.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.30, 0.16, 0.07);

        this.renderers.oak.mesh.material = oakMaterial;

        const pineMaterial = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "pineMaterial",
            scene
        );

        pineMaterial.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.40, 0.27, 0.13);

        this.renderers.pine.mesh.material = pineMaterial;
    }


isPositionAvailable(position) {

    return this.spatialGrid.isAvailable(
        position,
        this.minSpacing
    );

}

addTree(tree) {

    this.trees.push(tree);

    this.spatialGrid.insert(tree);

    return tree;

}

generate(numTrees = 80) {

    let attempts = 0;

    let livingCount = this.trees.reduce(
        (count, tree) => count + (tree.alive ? 1 : 0),
        0
    );

    while (
        livingCount < numTrees &&
        attempts < numTrees * 50
    ) {

        attempts++;

        const position = this.terrain.sample();

        if (!position) continue;
	
	if (this.waterSystem.isUnderwater(position)) {
		continue;
	}

        if (!this.isPositionAvailable(position)) {
            continue;
        }

        const speciesName =
            Math.random() < 0.55 ? "oak" : "pine";

        const tree = new Tree(
            speciesName,
            position,
            Genetics.createGenome(),
            null,
            Math.random() * 20
        );

        this.addTree(tree);

        livingCount++;
    }

// Generate geological formations.
this.geology.generateAll();

// Generate atmospheric objects.
this.generateWorldObjects(35);

// Render the forest.
this.renderAll();
    console.log("Initial forest:", livingCount);
}

    spawnOffspring(parent) {

        const numSeeds = parent.produceSeeds();

        let successful = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < numSeeds; i++) {

// Check available light at the parent location
const parentLight =
    this.environment.sampleLight(parent.position);

// Establish a minimum germination requirement
const minimumGerminationLight = 0.30;

if (parentLight.intensity < minimumGerminationLight) {
    continue;
}

// Germination probability increases with available light
const germinationChance =
    0.65 *
    BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        parentLight.intensity,
        0,
        1
    );

if (Math.random() > germinationChance) {
    continue;
}
// Only successful germination attempts
// need a terrain sample.
const position = this.terrain.sampleNear(
    parent.position,
    10
);

if (!position) continue;

if (this.waterSystem.isUnderwater(position)) {
    continue;
}


// Check actual germination location
const seedLight =
    this.environment.sampleLight(position);

if (seedLight.intensity < minimumGerminationLight) {
    continue;
}

            // Inherit and mutate the parent's genome
            const childGenome = Genetics.inherit(
                parent.genome,
                0.8
            );

            const child = new Tree(
                parent.speciesName,
                position,
                childGenome,
                parent.id,
                0
            );

            this.addTree(child);

            parent.children.push(child.id);

            successful++;
        }

        return successful;
    }


advance(deltaYears) {

	   // Advance water and atmospheric systems.

    this.waterSystem.update(deltaYears);

    this.hydrology.update(deltaYears);

    this.cloudSystem.update(deltaYears);


	// Advance geological processes.
	this.geology.advance(deltaYears);

    const initialTreeCount = this.trees.length;

    // Rebuild environmental canopy information
    this.environment.rebuildCanopyGrid(
        this.trees
    );

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < initialTreeCount;
        i++
    ) {

        const tree = this.trees[i];

        if (!tree.alive) continue;

        const wasAlive = tree.alive;

tree.update(
    deltaYears,
    this.environment,
    this.hydrology
);
        if (!tree.alive && wasAlive) {

            this.renderDirty = true;

        }

        if (tree.alive && tree.isMature) {

            const offspring =
                this.spawnOffspring(tree);

            if (offspring > 0) {
                this.renderDirty = true;
            }

        }

    }

    this.simulationSteps++;

    if (
        this.renderDirty ||
        this.simulationSteps >= this.renderEverySteps
    ) {

        this.renderAll();

        this.renderDirty = false;

        this.simulationSteps = 0;

    }

}


updateRendering() {

    if (!this.renderDirty) return;

    this.renderAll();

    this.renderDirty = false;
}

renderAll() {

    this.renderers.oak.matrices = [];
    this.renderers.pine.matrices = [];

    this.foliageRenderers.oak.matrices = [];
    this.foliageRenderers.pine.matrices = [];

	
const camera = this.scene.activeCamera;

const cameraPosition = camera
    ? camera.globalPosition
    : BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();

const maxRenderDistance = 250;

const nearDistance = 80;
const mediumDistance = 180;

const maxRenderDistanceSquared =
    maxRenderDistance * maxRenderDistance;

const nearDistanceSquared =
    nearDistance * nearDistance;

const mediumDistanceSquared =
    mediumDistance * mediumDistance;

for (const tree of this.trees) {

    if (!tree.alive) continue;

    const dx =
        tree.position.x - cameraPosition.x;

    const dz =
        tree.position.z - cameraPosition.z;

    const distanceSquared =
        dx * dx + dz * dz;

    // Cull distant trees
    if (
        distanceSquared >
        maxRenderDistanceSquared
    ) {
        continue;
    }

    // Determine geometry detail
    let detailLevel;

    if (distanceSquared < nearDistanceSquared) {

        detailLevel = 2;

    } else if (
        distanceSquared < mediumDistanceSquared
    ) {

        detailLevel = 1;

    } else {

        detailLevel = 0;

    }

    const generator = new TreeGenerator(
        tree.species,
        this.environment,
        tree.genome
    );

    const branches = generator.generate(
        tree.position,
        tree.ageFactor,
        tree.thicknessFactor,
        detailLevel
    );

    this.renderers[tree.speciesName]
        .addMatrices(branches);

    const foliage = generator.foliage;

    const renderer =
        this.foliageRenderers[tree.speciesName];

    for (const cluster of foliage) {

        let scale = cluster.scale;

        if (tree.speciesName === "oak") {

            scale *= 1.5;

        } else {

            scale *= 1.15;

        }

        renderer.add(
            cluster.position,
            scale
        );

    }

}


    // Upload branch buffers
    this.renderers.oak.update();
    this.renderers.pine.update();

    // Upload foliage buffers
    this.foliageRenderers.oak.update();
    this.foliageRenderers.pine.update();


	// Update geological formations.
this.rockRenderer.update();
// Update atmospheric rendering.

this.cloudSystem.updateRendering();
}


generateWorldObjects(cloudCount = 35) {

    this.cloudSystem.generate(cloudCount);

}




}
export { Forest }
```

src/world/chunk-ecology-system.js
_______
```

import { ChunkPopulationManager } from "./chunk-population-manager.js";
import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";
import { Tree } from "../ecology/tree.js";
import { Genetics } from "../core/genetics.js";
import { Environment } from "../environment/environment.js";
import { Species } from "../core/species.js";

import {
    TreeRenderer,
    FoliageRenderer
} from "../rendering/tree-renderers.js";

export class ChunkEcologySystem {
    constructor(scene, terrain, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.terrain = terrain;


	this.renderAccumulator = 0;
	this.renderInterval = 0.75;
	
	this.hydrology = options.hydrology ?? null;
	this.geologicalField = options.geologicalField ?? null;

        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;
        this.renderRadius = options.renderRadius ?? 3;
        this.treesPerChunk = options.treesPerChunk ?? 24;
        this.seaLevel = options.seaLevel ?? -100;

        this.worldSeed = options.worldSeed ?? 91827;

        this.environment = new Environment(
            options.sunDirection ??
            new BABYLON.Vector3(-0.5, -1, -0.3)
        );

        this.activeRecords = new Set();

        this.trunkRenderers = {
            oak: new TreeRenderer(
                scene, "chunkOakTrunks", null
            ),
            pine: new TreeRenderer(
                scene, "chunkPineTrunks", null
            )
        };

        this.foliageRenderers = {
            oak: new FoliageRenderer(
                scene,
                "chunkOakFoliage",
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.18, 0.32, 0.13),
                null
            ),
            pine: new FoliageRenderer(
                scene,
                "chunkPineFoliage",
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.10, 0.27, 0.17),
                null
            )
        };

        this.trunkRenderers.oak.mesh.material =
            this.createTrunkMaterial(
                "chunkOakMaterial",
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.30, 0.16, 0.07)
            );

        this.trunkRenderers.pine.mesh.material =
            this.createTrunkMaterial(
                "chunkPineMaterial",
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.40, 0.27, 0.13)
            );

        this.populationManager =
            new ChunkPopulationManager({
                worldSeed: this.worldSeed,
                chunkSize: this.chunkSize,
                maxGenerationPerUpdate: 2,
                maxSimulationPerUpdate: 8,

                adapter: {
                    generate: context =>
                        this.generatePopulation(context),

activate: record => {
    this.activeRecords.add(record);
    this.renderDirty = true;
},

deactivate: record => {
    this.activeRecords.delete(record);
    this.renderDirty = true;
},

                    simulate: (record, deltaYears) =>
                        this.simulatePopulation(
                            record, deltaYears
                        ),

                    serialize: record =>
                        this.serializePopulation(record),

                    deserialize: data =>
                        this.deserializePopulation(data)
                }
            });

        this.renderDirty = true;
        this.disposed = false;
    }

    createTrunkMaterial(name, color) {
        const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            name,
            this.scene
        );

        material.diffuseColor = color;
        material.specularColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.03, 0.03, 0.03);

        return material;
    }

    generatePopulation(context) {
        const random = context.random;

        const trees = [];

        const minX = Math.max(
            context.bounds.minX,
            this.terrain.min.x
        );

        const maxX = Math.min(
            context.bounds.maxX,
            this.terrain.max.x
        );

        const minZ = Math.max(
            context.bounds.minZ,
            this.terrain.min.z
        );

        const maxZ = Math.min(
            context.bounds.maxZ,
            this.terrain.max.z
        );

        if (minX >= maxX || minZ >= maxZ) {
            return { trees };
        }

        const attemptsLimit = this.treesPerChunk * 12;

        let attempts = 0;

        while (
            trees.length < this.treesPerChunk &&
            attempts < attemptsLimit
        ) {
            attempts++;

            const x = minX + random() * (maxX - minX);
            const z = minZ + random() * (maxZ - minZ);

            const profile = this.terrain.sampleProfileAt(
                x, z, 4
            );

            if (!profile) continue;

            if (profile.position.y <= this.seaLevel) {
                continue;
            }

            if (profile.slopeDegrees > 28) {
                continue;
            }

	const soil = this.geologicalField
    ? this.geologicalField.sample(x, z)
    : null;

const speciesName = this.chooseSpeciesForSoil(
    soil,
    random
);

            const genome = Genetics.createGenome();

            // Reproducible genetic variation.
            for (const gene of Object.keys(genome)) {
                genome[gene] *= 0.85 + random() * 0.30;
            }

            const initialAge = random() * 20;

            const tree = new Tree(
                speciesName,
                profile.position,
                genome,
                null,
                initialAge
            );

            tree.soilMoisture =
                0.25 + random() * 0.40;

            trees.push(tree);
        }

        return {
            trees,
            seed: context.seed,
            generatedCount: trees.length
        };
    }

simulatePopulation(record, deltaYears) {
    for (const tree of record.population.trees) {
        if (!tree.alive) continue;

        tree.update(
            deltaYears,
            this.environment,
            this.hydrology
        );
    }

    this.renderDirty = true;
}

    serializePopulation(record) {
        return {
            trees: record.population.trees.map(tree => ({
                speciesName: tree.speciesName,
                position: {
                    x: tree.position.x,
                    y: tree.position.y,
                    z: tree.position.z
                },
                genome: { ...tree.genome },
                parentId: tree.parentId,
                age: tree.age,
                seedTimer: tree.seedTimer,
                soilMoisture: tree.soilMoisture,
                alive: tree.alive,
                droughtYears: tree.droughtYears,
                waterloggingYears: tree.waterloggingYears,
                lowLightYears: tree.lowLightYears
            }))
        };
    }

    deserializePopulation(data) {
        const trees = [];

        for (const item of data.trees ?? []) {
            const tree = new Tree(
                item.speciesName,
                new BABYLON.Vector3(
                    item.position.x,
                    item.position.y,
                    item.position.z
                ),
                { ...item.genome },
                item.parentId,
                item.age
            );

            tree.seedTimer = item.seedTimer ?? 0;
            tree.soilMoisture = item.soilMoisture ?? 0.35;
            tree.alive = item.alive ?? true;

            tree.droughtYears = item.droughtYears ?? 0;
            tree.waterloggingYears =
                item.waterloggingYears ?? 0;
            tree.lowLightYears = item.lowLightYears ?? 0;

            trees.push(tree);
        }

        return { trees };
    }

    rebuildRendering() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.trunkRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.clear();
        }

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.foliageRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.clear();
        }

        const allTrees = [];

        for (const record of this.activeRecords) {
            if (!record.active) continue;

            for (const tree of record.population.trees) {
                if (tree.alive) allTrees.push(tree);
            }
        }

        this.environment.rebuildCanopyGrid(allTrees);

        for (const tree of allTrees) {
            const species = tree.species;
            const age = tree.ageFactor;

            const growth =
                age * age * (3 - 2 * age);

            const height =
                species.trunkLength *
                growth *
                tree.genome.maxHeight;

            const radius =
                species.trunkRadius *
                Math.sqrt(Math.max(age, 0.001)) *
                tree.genome.trunkThickness;

            if (height <= 0.05) continue;

            const trunkMatrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
                new BABYLON.Vector3(
                    radius,
                    height,
                    radius
                ),
                BABYLON.Quaternion.Identity(),
                new BABYLON.Vector3(
                    tree.position.x,
                    tree.position.y + height * 0.5,
                    tree.position.z
                )
            );

            this.trunkRenderers[
                tree.speciesName
            ].addMatrix(trunkMatrix);

            const canopy = species.canopy;

            const canopyHeight = height * canopy.height;

            const canopyWidth =
                height *
                canopy.width *
                tree.genome.canopyWidth;

            const canopyCenter = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                tree.position.x,
                tree.position.y +
                    height * (
                        canopy.startHeight +
                        canopy.height * 0.5
                    ),
                tree.position.z
            );

            const foliageScale = Math.max(
                0.08,
                canopyWidth * 0.42
            );

            const foliageMatrix = BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
                new BABYLON.Vector3(
                    foliageScale * 1.6,
                    Math.max(0.1, canopyHeight * 0.45),
                    foliageScale * 1.6
                ),
                BABYLON.Quaternion.Identity(),
                canopyCenter
            );

            this.foliageRenderers[
                tree.speciesName
            ].addMatrix(foliageMatrix);
        }

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.trunkRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.update();
        }

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.foliageRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.update();
        }

        this.renderDirty = false;
    }


update(cameraPosition, deltaSeconds = 0) {
    if (this.disposed) return;

    // Streaming and simulation continue normally.
    this.populationManager.updateVisibleChunks(
        cameraPosition,
        this.renderRadius
    );

    const deltaYears = deltaSeconds * 0.05;

    if (deltaYears > 0) {
        this.populationManager.updateSimulation(
            deltaYears
        );
    }

    // Batch expensive rendering work.
    this.renderAccumulator += Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(deltaSeconds, 0.25)
    );

    if (
        this.renderDirty &&
        (
            this.renderAccumulator >= this.renderInterval
        )
    ) {
        this.rebuildRendering();
        this.renderAccumulator = 0;
    }
}


    getStats() {
        return {
            ...this.populationManager.getStats(),

            activeTrees: Array.from(
                this.activeRecords
            ).reduce(
                (total, record) =>
                    total + record.population.trees.filter(
                        tree => tree.alive
                    ).length,
                0
            )
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        this.populationManager.dispose();

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.trunkRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.mesh.dispose();
        }

        for (const renderer of Object.values(
            this.foliageRenderers
        )) {
            renderer.mesh.dispose();
        }

        this.activeRecords.clear();
    }


chooseSpeciesForSoil(soil, random) {
    const fallback = random() < 0.55 ? "oak" : "pine";

    if (!soil) {
        return fallback;
    }

    const calculateScore = (speciesName) => {
        const species = Species[speciesName];

        // Some species definitions may not yet have
        // geological affinity data.
        if (!species || !species.soilAffinity) {
            return 0.5;
        }

        const affinity = species.soilAffinity;

        const preferred = Array.isArray(affinity.preferred)
            ? affinity.preferred
            : [];

        const texture = preferred.includes(soil.surfaceSoil)
            ? 1.0
            : (affinity.tolerance ?? 0.5);

        const fertility = 1 - Math.abs(
            (soil.fertility ?? 0.5) -
            (affinity.fertility ?? 0.5)
        );

        const drainage = 1 - Math.abs(
            (soil.drainage ?? 0.5) -
            (affinity.drainage ?? 0.5)
        );

        const moisture = 1 - Math.abs(
            (soil.moisture ?? 0.5) -
            (affinity.moisture ?? 0.5)
        );

        return (
            texture * 0.45 +
            fertility * 0.20 +
            drainage * 0.20 +
            moisture * 0.15
        );
    };

    const oakScore = calculateScore("oak");
    const pineScore = calculateScore("pine");

    const total = oakScore + pineScore;

    if (total <= 0) {
        return fallback;
    }

    return random() < oakScore / total
        ? "oak"
        : "pine";
}

}
```

src/world/chunk-cloud-system.js
