```
import { Cloud } from "../atmosphere/cloud.js";
import { CloudSpecies } from "../atmosphere/cloud-species.js";
import { CloudGenerator } from "../atmosphere/cloud-generator.js";
import { CloudRenderer } from "../rendering/cloud-renderer.js";
import { CreateSeededRandom } from "../core/random.js";

export class ChunkCloudSystem {
    constructor(
        scene,
        terrain,
        waterSystem,
        hydrology,
        rainSystem,
        options = {}
    ) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.waterSystem = waterSystem;
        this.hydrology = hydrology;
        this.rainSystem = rainSystem;

	this.windTime = 0;
this.windUpdateInterval = 2.0;
this.windAccumulator = 0;

        // Chunk configuration.
        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;
        this.renderRadius = options.renderRadius ?? 4;
        this.cloudsPerChunk = options.cloudsPerChunk ?? 8;
        this.maxChunksPerUpdate = options.maxChunksPerUpdate ?? 2;
        this.worldSeed = options.worldSeed ?? 91827;

        // Update intervals.
        this.renderInterval = options.renderInterval ?? 0.5;
        this.simulationInterval = options.simulationInterval ?? 0.25;

        // Storage.
        this.records = new Map();
        this.activeChunks = new Set();

        // Rendering.
        this.renderer = new CloudRenderer(scene);

        // Timing.
        this.renderAccumulator = 0;
        this.simulationAccumulator = 0;

        // Diagnostics.
        this.visibleCloudCount = 0;
        this.totalGeneratedClouds = 0;
        this.totalGeneratedStorms = 0;

        // State.
        this.renderDirty = true;
        this.disposed = false;
        this.rainingCloud = null;

        console.log(
            "[ChunkCloudSystem] Initialized with cumulonimbus support."
        );
    }

    getChunkCoordinates(position) {
        return {
            x: Math.floor(position.x / this.chunkSize),
            z: Math.floor(position.z / this.chunkSize)
        };
    }

    getChunkKey(x, z) {
        return `${x},${z}`;
    }

    getChunkSeed(x, z) {
        return (
            this.worldSeed ^
            Math.imul(x, 73856093) ^
            Math.imul(z, 19349663)
        ) >>> 0;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CLOUD SPECIES DISTRIBUTION
    // ------------------------------------------------

selectSpecies(random) {
    const value = random();

    if (value < 0.12 && CloudSpecies.cumulonimbus) {
        return "cumulonimbus";
    }

    if (value < 0.32 && CloudSpecies.towering) {
        return "towering";
    }

    if (CloudSpecies.cumulus) {
        return "cumulus";
    }

    // Fall back to whichever species exists.
    const availableSpecies =
        Object.keys(CloudSpecies);

    if (availableSpecies.length === 0) {
        throw new Error(
            "[ChunkCloudSystem] No cloud species configured."
        );
    }

    return availableSpecies[0];
}







    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CHUNK GENERATION
    // ------------------------------------------------

    generateChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {
        const key = this.getChunkKey(
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        );

        if (this.records.has(key)) {
            return this.records.get(key);
        }

        const seed = this.getChunkSeed(
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        );

        const random = CreateSeededRandom(seed);

        const minX = Math.max(
            chunkX * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.min.x
        );

        const maxX = Math.min(
            (chunkX + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.max.x
        );

        const minZ = Math.max(
            chunkZ * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.min.z
        );

        const maxZ = Math.min(
            (chunkZ + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.max.z
        );

        const clouds = [];

        if (minX >= maxX || minZ >= maxZ) {
            const record = {
                key,
                chunkX,
                chunkZ,
                seed,
                clouds
            };

            this.records.set(key, record);

            return record;
        }

        let attempts = 0;

        const attemptsLimit =
            this.cloudsPerChunk * 6;

        while (
            clouds.length < this.cloudsPerChunk &&
            attempts < attemptsLimit
        ) {
            attempts++;

            const x =
                minX + random() * (maxX - minX);

            const z =
                minZ + random() * (maxZ - minZ);

            const terrainPosition =
                this.terrain.sampleAt(x, z);

            if (!terrainPosition) {
                continue;
            }

		let speciesName =
    this.selectSpecies(random);

let config =
    CloudSpecies[speciesName];

if (!config) {
    console.error(
        "[ChunkCloudSystem] Missing cloud species:",
        speciesName,
        "Available species:",
        Object.keys(CloudSpecies)
    );

    speciesName = "cumulus";
    config = CloudSpecies.cumulus;
}

if (!config) {
    throw new Error(
        "[ChunkCloudSystem] Cumulus configuration is missing."
    );
}

// Give every cloud a substantially different size.
//
// Storm systems have a narrower variation range so
// their already enormous dimensions remain controlled.

const sizeVariation =
    speciesName === "cumulonimbus"
        ? 0.78 + random() * 0.62
        : 0.62 + random() * 1.08;

const scale = config.scale * (0.75 + random() * 0.55);

const altitude = speciesName === "cumulonimbus"
    ? 4200 + random() * 1800
    : speciesName === "towering"
        ? 3400 + random() * 1500
        : 3000 + random() * 1400;
            const position =
                terrainPosition.add(
                    new BABYLON.Vector3(
                        0,
                        altitude,
                        0
                    )
                );

            const cloud = new Cloud(
                position,
                speciesName,
                scale,
                config
            );

	
const wind = this.sampleWind(position, 0);

cloud.velocity.copyFrom(wind);
cloud.windTarget.copyFrom(wind);
cloud.waterContent =
    random() * cloud.maxWaterCapacity * 0.25;


            cloud.waterContent =
                random() *
                cloud.maxWaterCapacity *
                0.25;

            cloud.baseScale = scale;

            cloud.geometrySeed =
                (seed ^ Math.imul(
                    clouds.length + 1,
                    2654435761
                )) >>> 0;

            cloud.moistureAtLastRender = -1;

            clouds.push(cloud);

            if (speciesName === "cumulonimbus") {
                this.totalGeneratedStorms++;
            }
        }

        const record = {
            key,
            chunkX,
            chunkZ,
            seed,
            clouds
        };

        this.records.set(key, record);

        this.totalGeneratedClouds += clouds.length;

        this.renderDirty = true;

        console.log(
            `[ChunkCloudSystem] Generated ${clouds.length} clouds in ${key}`,
            {
                storms: clouds.filter(
                    cloud => cloud.isStormCloud
                ).length
            }
        );

        return record;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CHUNK STREAMING
    // ------------------------------------------------

    updateStreaming(cameraPosition) {
        const center =
            this.getChunkCoordinates(cameraPosition);

        const desiredChunks = new Set();
        const candidates = [];

        for (
            let x = center.x - this.renderRadius;
            x <= center.x + this.renderRadius;
            x++
        ) {
            for (
                let z = center.z - this.renderRadius;
                z <= center.z + this.renderRadius;
                z++
            ) {
                const dx = x - center.x;
                const dz = z - center.z;

                if (
                    dx * dx + dz * dz >
                    this.renderRadius * this.renderRadius
                ) {
                    continue;
                }

                const minX = x * this.chunkSize;
                const minZ = z * this.chunkSize;

                const maxX = minX + this.chunkSize;
                const maxZ = minZ + this.chunkSize;

                if (
                    maxX <= this.terrain.min.x ||
                    minX >= this.terrain.max.x ||
                    maxZ <= this.terrain.min.z ||
                    minZ >= this.terrain.max.z
                ) {
                    continue;
                }

                const key = this.getChunkKey(x, z);

                desiredChunks.add(key);

                if (!this.records.has(key)) {
                    candidates.push({
                        x,
                        z,
                        distance: dx * dx + dz * dz
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        candidates.sort(
            (a, b) => a.distance - b.distance
        );

        let generated = 0;

        for (const candidate of candidates) {
            if (
                generated >= this.maxChunksPerUpdate
            ) {
                break;
            }

            this.generateChunk(
                candidate.x,
                candidate.z
            );

            generated++;
        }

        const nextActive = new Set();

        for (const key of desiredChunks) {
            if (this.records.has(key)) {
                nextActive.add(key);
            }
        }

        const changed =
            nextActive.size !== this.activeChunks.size ||
            [...nextActive].some(
                key => !this.activeChunks.has(key)
            );

        if (changed) {
            this.activeChunks = nextActive;
            this.renderDirty = true;
        }
    }

	    // ------------------------------------------------
    // ATMOSPHERIC MOISTURE
    // ------------------------------------------------

    getLocalMoisture(cloud) {
        if (
            !this.hydrology ||
            typeof this.hydrology.sampleMoisture !== "function"
        ) {
            return 0.35;
        }

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            this.hydrology.sampleMoisture(
                cloud.position
            ),
            0,
            1
        );
    }

    getEvaporationPotential(cloud) {
        if (
            !this.waterSystem ||
            typeof this.waterSystem.getEvaporationPotential !== "function"
        ) {
            return 0.2;
        }

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            this.waterSystem.getEvaporationPotential(
                cloud.position
            ),
            0,
            1
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CLOUD SIMULATION
    // ------------------------------------------------

updateCloud(cloud, deltaSeconds) {
    cloud.position.addInPlace(
        cloud.velocity.scale(deltaSeconds * 0.5)
    );

    // Environmental sampling is more expensive than
    // the simple cloud-water simulation.
    // Refresh it once per second instead of every tick.

    cloud.environmentSampleTimer =
        (cloud.environmentSampleTimer ?? 0) -
        deltaSeconds;

    if (cloud.environmentSampleTimer <= 0) {
        cloud.localMoisture =
            this.getLocalMoisture(cloud);

        cloud.evaporationPotential =
            this.getEvaporationPotential(cloud);

        cloud.environmentSampleTimer = 1.0;
    }

    const moistureAvailability =
        BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            cloud.localMoisture * 0.65 +
            cloud.evaporationPotential * 0.35,
            0,
            1
        );

    if (!cloud.isRaining) {
        cloud.waterContent +=
            cloud.absorptionRate *
            moistureAvailability *
            deltaSeconds *
            0.05;
    }

    cloud.waterContent = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        cloud.waterContent,
        0,
        cloud.maxWaterCapacity
    );

    const moistureRatio = cloud.moistureRatio;

    if (
        !cloud.isRaining &&
        moistureRatio >= 0.95
    ) {
        this.startRain(cloud);
    }

    if (cloud.isRaining) {
        cloud.rainRemaining -= deltaSeconds;

        if (cloud.rainRemaining <= 0) {
            cloud.isRaining = false;
            cloud.rainIntensity = 0;
            cloud.rainRemaining = 0;
            this.renderDirty = true;
        }
    }

    const previous = cloud.moistureAtLastRender;

    if (
        previous < 0 ||
        Math.abs(moistureRatio - previous) >= 0.08
    ) {
        this.renderDirty = true;
    }
}
    // ------------------------------------------------
    // PRECIPITATION
    // ------------------------------------------------

    startRain(cloud) {
        cloud.isRaining = true;

        cloud.rainRemaining =
            cloud.rainDuration;

        const moistureRatio =
            cloud.moistureRatio;

        const stormMultiplier =
            cloud.isStormCloud ? 2.0 : 1.0;

        cloud.rainIntensity =
            (0.5 + moistureRatio * 1.5) *
            stormMultiplier;

        const releasedWater =
            cloud.maxWaterCapacity * 0.45;

        cloud.waterContent = Math.max(
            0,
            cloud.waterContent - releasedWater
        );

        if (
            this.hydrology &&
            typeof this.hydrology.addRain === "function"
        ) {
            const rainfallAmount =
                0.25 * cloud.rainIntensity;

            const rainfallRadius =
                cloud.scale *
                (cloud.isStormCloud ? 5 : 4);

            this.hydrology.addRain(
                cloud.position,
                rainfallRadius,
                rainfallAmount
            );
        }

        this.renderDirty = true;

        console.log(
            "[ChunkCloudSystem] Precipitation initiated:",
            {
                species: cloud.speciesName,
                position: cloud.position.asArray(),
                moistureRatio,
                intensity: cloud.rainIntensity
            }
        );
    }


updateSimulation(deltaSeconds) {
    let strongestRainCloud = null;

    // Only update currently active chunks.
    for (const key of this.activeChunks) {
        const record = this.records.get(key);

        if (!record) continue;

        for (const cloud of record.clouds) {
		this.windTime += deltaSeconds;

for (const key of this.activeChunks) {
    const record = this.records.get(key);
    if (!record) continue;

    for (const cloud of record.clouds) {
        const wind = this.sampleWind(
            cloud.position,
            this.windTime
        );

        cloud.updateWind(wind, deltaSeconds);

        this.updateCloudEnvironment(cloud, deltaSeconds);
    }
}
this.renderDirty = true;

            if (
                cloud.isRaining &&
                (
                    !strongestRainCloud ||
                    cloud.rainIntensity >
                    strongestRainCloud.rainIntensity
                )
            ) {
                strongestRainCloud = cloud;
            }
        }
    }

    this.rainingCloud = strongestRainCloud;

    if (strongestRainCloud) {
        const rainPosition =
            strongestRainCloud.position.clone();

        rainPosition.y -=
            strongestRainCloud.isStormCloud
                ? 300
                : 10;

        this.rainSystem.start(
            rainPosition,
            strongestRainCloud.rainIntensity
        );
    } else {
        this.rainSystem.stop();
    }
}

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CLOUD DIMENSIONS
    // ------------------------------------------------

getCloudRenderScale(cloud) {
    const moisture = cloud.moistureRatio;

    const expansion = 1.0 + moisture * 0.15;

    const verticalExpansion =
        cloud.isStormCloud
            ? 1.0 + moisture * 0.40
            : cloud.speciesName === "towering"
                ? 1.0 + moisture * 0.30
                : 1.0 + moisture * 0.10;

    return {
        horizontal: cloud.baseScale * expansion,
        vertical:
            cloud.baseScale *
            expansion *
            verticalExpansion
    };
}

	    // ------------------------------------------------
    // CLOUD RENDERING
    // ------------------------------------------------

    rebuildRendering() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.renderer.clear();

        let visibleCount = 0;

        for (const key of this.activeChunks) {
            const record = this.records.get(key);

            if (!record) continue;

            for (const cloud of record.clouds) {
                const config =
                    CloudSpecies[cloud.speciesName];

                if (!config) continue;

                // Generate persistent geometry once.
                if (!cloud.baseGeometryMatrices) {
                    const generator =
                        new CloudGenerator(config);

                    cloud.baseGeometryMatrices =
                        generator.generate(
                            cloud.position,
                            cloud.baseScale,
                            config.recursionDepth,
                            cloud.geometrySeed
                        );

                    cloud.baseGeometryPosition =
                        cloud.position.clone();
                }

                const dimensions =
                    this.getCloudRenderScale(cloud);

                const horizontalScale =
                    dimensions.horizontal /
                    cloud.baseScale;

                const verticalScale =
                    dimensions.vertical /
                    cloud.baseScale;

                const offset =
                    cloud.position.subtract(
                        cloud.baseGeometryPosition
                    );

                for (
                    const matrix of cloud.baseGeometryMatrices
                ) {
                    const scaling =
                        new BABYLON.Vector3();

                    const rotation =
                        new BABYLON.Quaternion();

                    const translation =
                        new BABYLON.Vector3();

                    matrix.decompose(
                        scaling,
                        rotation,
                        translation
                    );

                    scaling.x *= horizontalScale;
                    scaling.z *= horizontalScale;
                    scaling.y *= verticalScale;

                    translation.addInPlace(offset);

                    const adjustedMatrix =
                        BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
                            scaling,
                            rotation,
                            translation
                        );

                    this.renderer.addMatrix(
                        adjustedMatrix,
                        cloud.speciesName,
                        cloud.moistureRatio,
                        cloud.position
                    );
                }

                cloud.moistureAtLastRender =
                    cloud.moistureRatio;

                visibleCount++;
            }
        }

        this.renderer.update();

        this.visibleCloudCount = visibleCount;
        this.renderDirty = false;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // MAIN UPDATE
    // ------------------------------------------------

    update(cameraPosition, deltaSeconds) {
        if (this.disposed || !cameraPosition) {
            return;
        }

        const elapsed = Math.min(
            Math.max(deltaSeconds, 0),
            1
        );

        this.updateStreaming(cameraPosition);

        this.simulationAccumulator += elapsed;

        while (
            this.simulationAccumulator >=
            this.simulationInterval
        ) {
            this.updateSimulation(
                this.simulationInterval
            );

            this.simulationAccumulator -=
                this.simulationInterval;
        }

        this.renderAccumulator += elapsed;

        if (
            this.renderDirty ||
            this.renderAccumulator >=
            this.renderInterval
        ) {
            this.rebuildRendering();

            this.renderAccumulator = 0;
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // DIAGNOSTICS
    // ------------------------------------------------

    getStats() {
        let totalClouds = 0;
        let rainingClouds = 0;
        let stormClouds = 0;
        let averageMoisture = 0;
        let moistureSamples = 0;

        for (const record of this.records.values()) {
            for (const cloud of record.clouds) {
                totalClouds++;

                if (cloud.isRaining) {
                    rainingClouds++;
                }

                if (cloud.isStormCloud) {
                    stormClouds++;
                }

                averageMoisture +=
                    cloud.moistureRatio;

                moistureSamples++;
            }
        }

        return {
            generatedChunks: this.records.size,
            activeChunks: this.activeChunks.size,

            retainedChunks: this.records.size,

            totalClouds,
            visibleClouds: this.visibleCloudCount,

            stormClouds,
            totalGeneratedStorms:
                this.totalGeneratedStorms,

            rainingClouds,

            averageMoisture:
                moistureSamples > 0
                    ? averageMoisture /
                      moistureSamples
                    : 0,

            renderRadius: this.renderRadius,
            chunkSize: this.chunkSize,

            simulationInterval:
                this.simulationInterval,

            renderInterval:
                this.renderInterval,

            renderDirty: this.renderDirty
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // DISPOSAL
    // ------------------------------------------------

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        if (this.rainSystem) {
            this.rainSystem.stop();
        }

        if (this.renderer) {
            this.renderer.mesh.dispose(
                false,
                true
            );
        }

        this.records.clear();
        this.activeChunks.clear();

        this.rainingCloud = null;

        this.visibleCloudCount = 0;
        this.totalGeneratedClouds = 0;
        this.totalGeneratedStorms = 0;

        console.log(
            "[ChunkCloudSystem] Disposed."
        );
    }


sampleWind(position, time) {
    const x = position.x;
    const z = position.z;

    // Large-scale atmospheric circulation.
    const broad = Math.sin(
        x * 0.000035 + z * 0.000018 + time * 0.018
    );

    const secondary = Math.cos(
        z * 0.000045 - x * 0.000012 + time * 0.012
    );

    const direction = new BABYLON.Vector3(
        0.75 + broad * 0.20,
        0,
        0.30 + secondary * 0.25
    ).normalize();

    const speed = 4 + 9 * (
        0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(
            x * 0.00002 + z * 0.000025 + time * 0.01
        )
    );

    return direction.scale(speed);
}

updateCloudEnvironment(cloud, deltaSeconds) {
    // Keep the existing moisture and precipitation simulation.
    // These values should be sampled periodically, not every frame.

    cloud.environmentSampleTimer =
        (cloud.environmentSampleTimer || 0) + deltaSeconds;

    const sampleInterval = 2.0;

    if (cloud.environmentSampleTimer >= sampleInterval) {
        cloud.environmentSampleTimer = 0;

        const position = cloud.position;

        // Use the existing environmental sampling functions
        // from your original updateCloud implementation.
        if (this.moistureField) {
            cloud.localMoisture =
                this.moistureField.sample(
                    position.x,
                    position.z
                );
        }
    }

    // Preserve cloud water capacity.
    cloud.waterContent = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        cloud.waterContent,
        0,
        cloud.maxWaterCapacity
    );

    // Existing precipitation lifecycle.
    if (cloud.isRaining) {
        cloud.rainRemaining -= deltaSeconds;

        if (cloud.rainRemaining <= 0) {
            cloud.isRaining = false;
            cloud.rainIntensity = 0;
            cloud.rainRemaining = 0;
        }
    }
}

}
```

src/world/fields/geometry/spatial-transform.js
_______
```

/**
 * SpatialTransform
 *
 * Coordinates world geometry transformations.
 *
 * Separates:
 * - Authoritative world coordinates.
 * - Mathematical geometry coordinates.
 * - Rendering coordinates.
 *
 * Supports Euclidean and Poincare geometry.
 */

import { PoincareProjection } from "./poincare-projection.js";
import { PoincareMetric } from "./poincare-metric.js";

import { EuclideanGeometry } from "./euclidean-geometry.js";
import { MetricTensor } from "../metric-tensor.js";

class SpatialTransform {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.geometryName =
            options.geometry ?? "poincare";

        this.renderScale =
            options.renderScale ?? 10000;

        this.enabled =
            options.enabled ?? true;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.renderScale) ||
            this.renderScale <= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid geometry render scale."
            );
        }

        this.euclidean =
            new EuclideanGeometry();

        this.projection =
            options.projection ??
            new PoincareProjection(
                options.bounds ?? {}
            );

        this.metric =
            options.metric ??
            new PoincareMetric(
                this.projection.curvature
            );

        this.setGeometry(this.geometryName);
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // GEOMETRY SELECTION
    // -----------------------------------------

    setGeometry(name) {

        if (
            name !== "euclidean" &&
            name !== "poincare"
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported geometry: ${name}`
            );
        }

        this.geometryName = name;

        console.log(
            "[SpatialTransform] Geometry:",
            name
        );

    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // WORLD TO GEOMETRY COORDINATES
    // -----------------------------------------

    worldToGeometry(position) {

        if (
            !this.enabled ||
            this.geometryName === "euclidean"
        ) {
            return {
                x: position.x,
                y: position.y,
                z: position.z
            };
        }

        return this.projection.worldToBall(position);
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // GEOMETRY TO WORLD COORDINATES
    // -----------------------------------------

    geometryToWorld(position) {

        if (
            !this.enabled ||
            this.geometryName === "euclidean"
        ) {
            return { ...position };
        }

        return this.projection.ballToWorld(position);
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // WORLD TO RENDER COORDINATES
    // -----------------------------------------

    worldToRender(position) {

        const point =
            this.worldToGeometry(position);

        if (
            !this.enabled ||
            this.geometryName === "euclidean"
        ) {
            return point;
        }

        return {
            x: point.x * this.renderScale,
            y: point.y * this.renderScale,
            z: point.z * this.renderScale
        };
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // RENDER TO WORLD COORDINATES
    // -----------------------------------------

    renderToWorld(position) {

        if (
            !this.enabled ||
            this.geometryName === "euclidean"
        ) {
            return { ...position };
        }

        return this.geometryToWorld({
            x: position.x / this.renderScale,
            y: position.y / this.renderScale,
            z: position.z / this.renderScale
        });
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // NUMERICAL JACOBIAN
    // -----------------------------------------

    getJacobian(position, options = {}) {

        const h = options.step ?? 0.01;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(h) ||
            h <= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid Jacobian step."
            );
        }

        const origin =
            this.worldToRender(position);

        const columns = [];

        for (const axis of ["x", "y", "z"]) {

            const forward = { ...position };
            const backward = { ...position };

            forward[axis] += h;
            backward[axis] -= h;

            const a = this.worldToRender(forward);
            const b = this.worldToRender(backward);

            columns.push([
                (a.x - b.x) / (2 * h),
                (a.y - b.y) / (2 * h),
                (a.z - b.z) / (2 * h)
            ]);
        }

        return [
            columns[0][0],
            columns[1][0],
            columns[2][0],

            columns[0][1],
            columns[1][1],
            columns[2][1],

            columns[0][2],
            columns[1][2],
            columns[2][2]
        ];
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DIRECTION TRANSFORMATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    transformDirection(position, direction) {

        const J = this.getJacobian(position);

        return {
            x:
                J[0] * direction.x +
                J[1] * direction.y +
                J[2] * direction.z,

            y:
                J[3] * direction.x +
                J[4] * direction.y +
                J[5] * direction.z,

            z:
                J[6] * direction.x +
                J[7] * direction.y +
                J[8] * direction.z
        };
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // SPATIAL METRIC
    // -----------------------------------------

	
    metricAtWorld(position) {

        if (
            !this.enabled ||
            this.geometryName === "euclidean"
        ) {
            return this.euclidean.metricAt(position);
        }

        const ballPosition =
            this.projection.worldToBall(position);

        const geometryMetric =
            this.metric.metricAt(ballPosition).values;

        // Convert the rendering Jacobian into
        // the mathematical projection Jacobian.

        const J = this.getJacobian(position).map(
            value => value / this.renderScale
        );

        // Pull back G into world coordinates:
        // G_world = transpose(J) * G_ball * J

        const values = new Array(9).fill(0);

        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) {

                for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
                    for (let l = 0; l < 3; l++) {

                        values[i * 3 + j] +=
                            J[k * 3 + i] *
                            geometryMetric[k * 3 + l] *
                            J[l * 3 + j];
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return new MetricTensor(values);
    }



    // -----------------------------------------
    // HYPERBOLIC DISTANCE
    // -----------------------------------------

    distance(a, b) {

        if (this.geometryName === "euclidean") {
            return this.euclidean.distance(a, b);
        }

        const pointA =
            this.projection.worldToBall(a);

        const pointB =
            this.projection.worldToBall(b);

        return this.metric.distance(
            pointA,
            pointB
        );
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DIAGNOSTICS
    // -----------------------------------------

    getInfo() {

        return {
            geometry: this.geometryName,

            enabled: this.enabled,

            renderScale: this.renderScale,

            projection:
                this.projection.getInfo(),

            metric:
                this.metric.getInfo()
        };
    }

}

export { SpatialTransform };

```

src/world/fields/geometry/geometry-renderer.js
_______
```

/**
 * GeometryRenderer
 *
 * Applies spatial transformations to Babylon.js
 * mesh geometry.
 *
 * Initial target:
 * - Streamed terrain meshes.
 *
 * Preserves:
 * - Original vertex positions.
 * - Original vertex normals.
 * - Mesh materials.
 * - Vertex colors.
 * - Mesh indices.
 *
 * Simulation coordinates remain unchanged.
 */

class GeometryRenderer {

    constructor(scene, spatialTransform, options = {}) {

        if (!scene || !spatialTransform) {
            throw new Error(
                "GeometryRenderer requires a scene and SpatialTransform."
            );
        }

        this.scene = scene;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.enabled = options.enabled ?? true;

        this.meshCache = new WeakMap();

        this.statistics = {
            transformedMeshes: 0,
            transformedVertices: 0,
            restoredMeshes: 0
        };

        console.log(
            "[GeometryRenderer] Initialized:",
            this.transform.getInfo()
        );
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // MESH TRANSFORMATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    applyMesh(mesh) {

        if (!this.enabled || !mesh) {
            return false;
        }

        if (this.transform.geometryName === "euclidean") {
            return false;
        }

        const cached = this.meshCache.get(mesh);

        if (cached) {
            return false;
        }

        const positions =
            mesh.getVerticesData(
                BABYLON.VertexBuffer.PositionKind
            );

        const indices = mesh.getIndices();

        if (
            !positions ||
            !indices ||
            positions.length === 0
        ) {
            return false;
        }

        // Preserve authoritative mesh geometry.

        const originalPositions =
            new Float32Array(positions);

        const originalNormals =
            mesh.getVerticesData(
                BABYLON.VertexBuffer.NormalKind
            );

        const cache = {
            positions: originalPositions,

            normals: originalNormals
                ? new Float32Array(originalNormals)
                : null
        };

        this.meshCache.set(mesh, cache);

        // Transform every vertex.

        const transformedPositions =
            new Float32Array(positions.length);

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < positions.length;
            i += 3
        ) {

            const projected =
                this.transform.worldToRender({
                    x: positions[i],
                    y: positions[i + 1],
                    z: positions[i + 2]
                });

            transformedPositions[i] = projected.x;
            transformedPositions[i + 1] = projected.y;
            transformedPositions[i + 2] = projected.z;
        }

        // Recalculate normals in the new geometry.

        const transformedNormals =
            new Float32Array(positions.length);

        BABYLON.VertexData.ComputeNormals(
            transformedPositions,
            indices,
            transformedNormals
        );

        mesh.setVerticesData(
            BABYLON.VertexBuffer.PositionKind,
            transformedPositions,
            true,
            3
        );

        mesh.setVerticesData(
            BABYLON.VertexBuffer.NormalKind,
            transformedNormals,
            true,
            3
        );

        mesh.refreshBoundingInfo();

        mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);

        mesh.metadata = {
            ...(mesh.metadata || {}),
            spatialGeometry: this.transform.geometryName,
            spatiallyTransformed: true
        };

        this.statistics.transformedMeshes++;

        this.statistics.transformedVertices +=
            positions.length / 3;

        return true;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // RESTORE ORIGINAL GEOMETRY
    // -----------------------------------------

    restoreMesh(mesh) {

        if (!mesh) return false;

        const cache = this.meshCache.get(mesh);

        if (!cache) return false;

        mesh.setVerticesData(
            BABYLON.VertexBuffer.PositionKind,
            cache.positions,
            true,
            3
        );

        if (cache.normals) {

            mesh.setVerticesData(
                BABYLON.VertexBuffer.NormalKind,
                cache.normals,
                true,
                3
            );
        }

        mesh.refreshBoundingInfo();

        mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);

        if (mesh.metadata) {

            mesh.metadata.spatiallyTransformed = false;

            delete mesh.metadata.spatialGeometry;
        }

        this.meshCache.delete(mesh);

        this.statistics.restoredMeshes++;

        return true;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // RESTORE ALL KNOWN CHUNKS
    // -----------------------------------------

    restoreWorld(worldManager) {

        if (!worldManager) return;

        for (const chunk of worldManager.chunks.values()) {

            if (chunk.mesh) {
                this.restoreMesh(chunk.mesh);
            }
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // REPROJECT ALL KNOWN CHUNKS
    // -----------------------------------------

    reprojectWorld(worldManager) {

        if (!worldManager) return;

        for (const chunk of worldManager.chunks.values()) {

            if (!chunk.mesh) continue;

            this.restoreMesh(chunk.mesh);

            this.applyMesh(chunk.mesh);
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CONFIGURATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    setEnabled(enabled, worldManager = null) {

        this.enabled = !!enabled;

        if (!worldManager) return;

        if (this.enabled) {
            this.reprojectWorld(worldManager);
        } else {
            this.restoreWorld(worldManager);
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DIAGNOSTICS
    // -----------------------------------------

    getStats() {

        return {
            enabled: this.enabled,

            geometry:
                this.transform.geometryName,

            ...this.statistics
        };
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DISPOSAL
    // -----------------------------------------

    dispose(worldManager = null) {

        if (worldManager) {
            this.restoreWorld(worldManager);
        }

        this.enabled = false;

        console.log(
            "[GeometryRenderer] Disposed:",
            this.getStats()
        );
    }
	

    // -----------------------------------------
    // THIN INSTANCE TRANSFORMATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    transformInstanceMatrix(matrix) {

        if (
            !this.enabled ||
            this.transform.geometryName === "euclidean"
        ) {
            return matrix;
        }

        if (
            !matrix ||
            !matrix.m ||
            !Array.from(matrix.m).every(Number.isFinite)
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        // Extract translation directly from the matrix.

        const translation = matrix.getTranslation();

        if (
            !translation ||
            !Number.isFinite(translation.x) ||
            !Number.isFinite(translation.y) ||
            !Number.isFinite(translation.z)
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        // Reject positions outside the authoritative
        // projection domain.

        let projected;

        try {

            projected =
                this.transform.worldToRender({
                    x: translation.x,
                    y: translation.y,
                    z: translation.z
                });

        } catch (error) {

            return null;
        }

        const scaling = new BABYLON.Vector3();
        const rotation = new BABYLON.Quaternion();
        const decomposedTranslation =
            new BABYLON.Vector3();

        if (
            !matrix.decompose(
                scaling,
                rotation,
                decomposedTranslation
            )
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        // Validate the local transformation Jacobian.

        let J;

        try {

            J = this.transform.getJacobian({
                x: translation.x,
                y: translation.y,
                z: translation.z
            });

        } catch (error) {

            return null;
        }

        if (!J.every(Number.isFinite)) {
            return null;
        }

        // Estimate volume scaling.

        const determinant =
            J[0] * (J[4] * J[8] - J[5] * J[7]) -
            J[1] * (J[3] * J[8] - J[5] * J[6]) +
            J[2] * (J[3] * J[7] - J[4] * J[6]);

        if (!Number.isFinite(determinant)) {
            return null;
        }

        const scaleFactor = Math.cbrt(
            Math.max(Math.abs(determinant), 1e-18)
        );

        const adjustedScale =
            scaling.scale(scaleFactor);

        return BABYLON.Matrix.Compose(
            adjustedScale,
            rotation,
            new BABYLON.Vector3(
                projected.x,
                projected.y,
                projected.z
            )
        );
    }

	
    // -----------------------------------------
    // CAMERA BOUNDARY PROTECTION
    // -----------------------------------------

    constrainCamera(camera) {

        if (
            !camera ||
            this.transform.geometryName !== "poincare"
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const scale = this.transform.renderScale;
        const radius = this.transform.projection.radius;

        const x = camera.position.x / scale;
        const y = camera.position.y / scale;
        const z = camera.position.z / scale;

        const distance = Math.hypot(x, y, z);

        const maximumRadius =
            radius * (1 - 1e-9);

        if (distance >= maximumRadius) {

            const factor =
                maximumRadius / Math.max(distance, 1e-12);

            camera.position.scaleInPlace(factor);
        }
    }

    getWorldCameraPosition(camera) {

        this.constrainCamera(camera);

        return this.transform.renderToWorld({
            x: camera.position.x,
            y: camera.position.y,
            z: camera.position.z
        });
    }




}

export { GeometryRenderer };

```

src/world/fields/geometry/hyperbolic-camera-controller.js
_______
```
/**
 * HyperbolicCameraController
 *
 * Converts ordinary Babylon.js camera input into
 * hyperbolically distance-limited geodesic movement.
 *
 * Authoritative world coordinates remain unchanged.
 */

class HyperbolicCameraController {
    constructor(camera, spatialTransform, options = {}) {
        if (!camera || !spatialTransform) {
            throw new Error(
                "HyperbolicCameraController requires a camera and SpatialTransform."
            );
        }

        this.camera = camera;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;
        this.projection = spatialTransform.projection;
        this.metric = spatialTransform.metric;

        this.enabled = options.enabled ?? true;

        this.maxHyperbolicSpeed =
            options.maxHyperbolicSpeed ?? 2.5;

        this.boundaryMargin =
            options.boundaryMargin ?? 1e-7;

        this.maxDeltaSeconds =
            options.maxDeltaSeconds ?? 0.05;

        this.statistics = {
            updates: 0,
            geodesicMoves: 0,
            boundaryCorrections: 0,
            limitedMoves: 0
        };

        this.previousPosition = this.getBallPosition();

        this.constrainCamera();

        this.previousPosition = this.getBallPosition();

        console.log(
            "[HyperbolicCameraController] Initialized:",
            this.getInfo()
        );
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // POSITION CONVERSION
    // -----------------------------------------

    getBallPosition() {
        const scale = this.transform.renderScale;

        return {
            x: this.camera.position.x / scale,
            y: this.camera.position.y / scale,
            z: this.camera.position.z / scale
        };
    }

    setBallPosition(position) {
        const scale = this.transform.renderScale;

        this.camera.position.set(
            position.x * scale,
            position.y * scale,
            position.z * scale
        );
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // OPEN BALL PROTECTION
    // -----------------------------------------

    constrainPosition(position) {
        const radius = this.projection.radius;

        const maximumRadius =
            radius * (1 - this.boundaryMargin);

        const distance = Math.hypot(
            position.x,
            position.y,
            position.z
        );

        if (distance < maximumRadius) {
            return {
                x: position.x,
                y: position.y,
                z: position.z
            };
        }

        const factor =
            maximumRadius / Math.max(distance, 1e-15);

        this.statistics.boundaryCorrections++;

        return {
            x: position.x * factor,
            y: position.y * factor,
            z: position.z * factor
        };
    }

    constrainCamera() {
        const position = this.constrainPosition(
            this.getBallPosition()
        );

        this.setBallPosition(position);

        return position;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // GEODESIC CAMERA MOVEMENT
    // -----------------------------------------

    update(deltaSeconds) {
        if (!this.enabled) {
            return;
        }

        if (!Number.isFinite(deltaSeconds) ||
            deltaSeconds <= 0) {
            return;
        }

        const elapsed = Math.min(
            deltaSeconds,
            this.maxDeltaSeconds
        );

        const start = this.previousPosition;

        // Babylon.js has already processed ordinary
        // keyboard and mouse camera input.
        const target = this.constrainPosition(
            this.getBallPosition()
        );

        let distance;

        try {
            distance = this.metric.distance(
                start,
                target
            );
        } catch (error) {
            this.setBallPosition(start);
            return;
        }

        if (!Number.isFinite(distance)) {
            this.setBallPosition(start);
            return;
        }

        if (distance < 1e-10) {
            this.setBallPosition(target);
            this.previousPosition = target;
            return;
        }

        // Limit movement by hyperbolic distance.
        const maximumStep =
            this.maxHyperbolicSpeed * elapsed;

        const fraction = Math.min(
            1,
            maximumStep / distance
        );

        if (fraction < 1) {
            this.statistics.limitedMoves++;
        }

        // Analytic Poincare geodesic interpolation.
        const nextPosition =
            this.metric.interpolateGeodesic(
                start,
                target,
                fraction
            );

        const safePosition =
            this.constrainPosition(nextPosition);

        this.setBallPosition(safePosition);

        this.previousPosition = safePosition;

        this.statistics.updates++;
        this.statistics.geodesicMoves++;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // AUTHORITATIVE WORLD POSITION
    // -----------------------------------------

    getWorldPosition() {
        this.constrainCamera();

        return this.transform.renderToWorld({
            x: this.camera.position.x,
            y: this.camera.position.y,
            z: this.camera.position.z
        });
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TELEPORT / POSITION SYNCHRONIZATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    synchronize() {
        this.constrainCamera();
        this.previousPosition = this.getBallPosition();
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CONFIGURATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    setEnabled(enabled) {
        this.enabled = !!enabled;

        this.synchronize();
    }

    getInfo() {
        return {
            enabled: this.enabled,
            geometry: "poincare",
            movement: "hyperbolic-geodesic",
            maxHyperbolicSpeed: this.maxHyperbolicSpeed,
            boundaryMargin: this.boundaryMargin,
            boundaryReachable: false,
            statistics: { ...this.statistics }
        };
    }

    getStats() {
        return this.getInfo();
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DISPOSAL
    // -----------------------------------------

    dispose() {
        this.enabled = false;
        this.camera = null;
        this.previousPosition = null;
    }
}

export { HyperbolicCameraController };
```

src/world/fields/geometry/geometry-shader-manager.js
_______
```

/**
 * GeometryShaderManager
 *
 * GPU-side spatial projection for Babylon.js.
 *
 * Initial implementation:
 * - Three-dimensional Poincare projection.
 * - Configurable curvature.
 * - Configurable world bounds.
 * - Basic diffuse lighting.
 * - Independent shader material management.
 *
 * Initial limitation:
 * - Ordinary meshes only.
 * - Thin instances require a separate shader path.
 */

class GeometryShaderManager {

    constructor(scene, spatialTransform, options = {}) {

        if (!scene || !spatialTransform) {
            throw new Error(
                "GeometryShaderManager requires a scene and SpatialTransform."
            );
        }

        this.scene = scene;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.enabled = options.enabled ?? true;

        this.materials = new Set();
        this.originalMaterials = new WeakMap();

        this.renderScale =
            options.renderScale ??
            spatialTransform.renderScale;

        this.initializeShaders();

        console.log(
            "[GeometryShaderManager] Initialized."
        );
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // SHADER REGISTRATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    initializeShaders() {

        BABYLON.Effect.ShadersStore[
            "somniumPoincareVertexShader"
        ] = this.getVertexShader();

        BABYLON.Effect.ShadersStore[
            "somniumPoincareFragmentShader"
        ] = this.getFragmentShader();
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // VERTEX SHADER
    // -----------------------------------------

    getVertexShader() {

        return `
            precision highp float;

            attribute vec3 position;
            attribute vec3 normal;

            uniform mat4 world;
	    uniform mat4 viewProjection;

            uniform vec3 worldCenter;
            uniform vec3 worldHalfBounds;

            uniform float ballRadius;
            uniform float characteristicLength;
            uniform float renderScale;
            uniform float boundaryEpsilon;

            varying vec3 vNormal;
            varying vec3 vWorldPosition;

            float safeTanh(float value) {

                float x = clamp(value, 0.0, 20.0);

                float e = exp(2.0 * x);

                return (e - 1.0) / (e + 1.0);
            }

            vec3 poincareProject(vec3 worldPosition) {

                vec3 delta =
                    worldPosition - worldCenter;

                float distanceFromCenter =
                    length(delta);

                if (distanceFromCenter < 0.000001) {
                    return vec3(0.0);
                }

                vec3 direction =
                    delta / distanceFromCenter;

                vec3 safeDirection =
                    max(abs(direction), vec3(0.000001));

                vec3 boundaryDistances =
                    worldHalfBounds / safeDirection;

                float boundaryDistance =
                    min(
                        boundaryDistances.x,
                        min(
                            boundaryDistances.y,
                            boundaryDistances.z
                        )
                    );

                float u =
                    distanceFromCenter /
                    boundaryDistance;

                u = clamp(
                    u,
                    0.0,
                    1.0 - boundaryEpsilon
                );

                float hyperbolicDistance =
                    characteristicLength *
                    u / (1.0 - u);

                float projectedRadius =
                    ballRadius *
                    safeTanh(
                        hyperbolicDistance /
                        (2.0 * ballRadius)
                    );

                return direction * projectedRadius;
            }

	void main(void) {

    vec4 worldPosition =
        world * vec4(position, 1.0);

    vec3 projectedPosition =
        poincareProject(worldPosition.xyz);

    vWorldPosition =
        projectedPosition * renderScale;

    vNormal =
        normalize(mat3(world) * normal);

    gl_Position =
        viewProjection *
        vec4(vWorldPosition, 1.0);
}

        `;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // FRAGMENT SHADER
    // -----------------------------------------

    getFragmentShader() {

        return `
            precision highp float;

            uniform vec4 baseColor;
            uniform vec3 lightDirection;
            uniform float ambientStrength;

            varying vec3 vNormal;
            varying vec3 vWorldPosition;

            void main(void) {

                vec3 normal =
                    normalize(vNormal);

                float diffuse =
                    max(
                        dot(normal, normalize(-lightDirection)),
                        0.0
                    );

                float illumination =
                    ambientStrength +
                    (1.0 - ambientStrength) * diffuse;

                vec3 color =
                    baseColor.rgb * illumination;

                gl_FragColor =
                    vec4(color, baseColor.a);
            }
        `;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // MATERIAL CREATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    createMaterial(name, options = {}) {

        const material =
            new BABYLON.ShaderMaterial(
                name,
                this.scene,
                {
                    vertex: "somniumPoincare",
                    fragment: "somniumPoincare"
                },
                {
                    attributes: [
                        "position",
                        "normal"
                    ],

                    uniforms: [
                        "world",
                        "worldViewProjection",
                        "worldCenter",
                        "worldHalfBounds",
                        "ballRadius",
                        "characteristicLength",
                        "renderScale",
                        "boundaryEpsilon",
                        "baseColor",
                        "lightDirection",
                        "ambientStrength"
                    ]
                }
            );

        const projection =
            this.transform.projection;

        const bounds = projection.bounds;

        material.setVector3(
            "worldCenter",
            new BABYLON.Vector3(
                projection.center.x,
                projection.center.y,
                projection.center.z
            )
        );

        material.setVector3(
            "worldHalfBounds",
            new BABYLON.Vector3(
                (bounds.maxX - bounds.minX) / 2,
                (bounds.maxY - bounds.minY) / 2,
                (bounds.maxZ - bounds.minZ) / 2
            )
        );

        material.setFloat(
            "ballRadius",
            projection.radius
        );

        material.setFloat(
            "characteristicLength",
            projection.characteristicLength
        );

        material.setFloat(
            "renderScale",
            this.renderScale
        );

        material.setFloat(
            "boundaryEpsilon",
            projection.boundaryEpsilon
        );

        material.setColor4(
            "baseColor",
            options.color ??
                new BABYLON.Color3(0.35, 0.55, 0.3),
            options.alpha ?? 1
        );

        material.setVector3(
            "lightDirection",
            options.lightDirection ??
                new BABYLON.Vector3(-0.5, -1, -0.3).normalize()
        );

        material.setFloat(
            "ambientStrength",
            options.ambientStrength ?? 0.3
        );

        material.backFaceCulling =
            options.backFaceCulling ?? true;

material.onBindObservable.add(mesh => {

    const camera = this.scene.activeCamera;

    if (!camera) return;

    const world = mesh.getWorldMatrix();

    const viewProjection =
        camera.getViewMatrix().multiply(
            camera.getProjectionMatrix()
        );

    material.setMatrix("world", world);

    material.setMatrix(
        "viewProjection",
        viewProjection
    );
});
        this.materials.add(material);

        return material;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // APPLY TO MESH
    // -----------------------------------------

    apply(mesh, options = {}) {

        if (!this.enabled || !mesh) {
            return false;
        }

        if (
            mesh.metadata?.spatiallyTransformed
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Cannot apply GPU projection to a CPU-projected mesh."
            );
        }

        if (
            mesh.metadata?.geometryShaderApplied
        ) {
            return false;
        }

        if (!this.originalMaterials.has(mesh)) {

            this.originalMaterials.set(
                mesh,
                mesh.material
            );
        }

        const material =
            this.createMaterial(
                options.name ??
                    `poincare-${mesh.name}`,
                options
            );

        mesh.material = material;

        mesh.metadata = {
            ...(mesh.metadata || {}),
            geometryShaderApplied: true
        };

        return true;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // RESTORE ORIGINAL MATERIAL
    // -----------------------------------------

    restore(mesh) {

        if (!mesh) return false;

        if (!this.originalMaterials.has(mesh)) {
            return false;
        }

        mesh.material =
            this.originalMaterials.get(mesh);

        this.originalMaterials.delete(mesh);

        if (mesh.metadata) {
            delete mesh.metadata.geometryShaderApplied;
        }

        return true;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CONFIGURATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = !!enabled;
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DIAGNOSTICS
    // -----------------------------------------

    getStats() {

        return {
            enabled: this.enabled,
            geometry: this.transform.geometryName,
            activeMaterials: this.materials.size,
            renderScale: this.renderScale
        };
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // DISPOSAL
    // -----------------------------------------

    dispose() {

        for (const material of this.materials) {
            material.dispose();
        }

        this.materials.clear();

        console.log(
            "[GeometryShaderManager] Disposed."
        );
    }
}

export { GeometryShaderManager };
```

src/world/fields/geometry/poincare-geometry.js
_______
```

import { GeometryModel } from "./geometry-model.js";
import { MetricTensor } from "../metric-tensor.js";

class PoincareGeometry extends GeometryModel {

    constructor(curvature = -1) {

        super("poincare");

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(curvature) ||
            curvature >= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Poincare curvature must be negative."
            );
        }

        this.curvature = curvature;

        this.k = -curvature;

        this.radius = 1 / Math.sqrt(this.k);

    }

    isInside(position) {

        const radiusSquared =
            position.x * position.x +
            position.y * position.y +
            position.z * position.z;

        return radiusSquared <
            this.radius * this.radius;

    }

    metricAt(position) {

        if (!this.isInside(position)) {

            throw new RangeError(
                "Position lies outside the Poincare ball."
            );

        }

        const r2 =
            position.x * position.x +
            position.y * position.y +
            position.z * position.z;

        const denominator =
            1 - this.k * r2;

        const factor =
            4 / (denominator * denominator);

        return new MetricTensor([

            factor, 0, 0,

            0, factor, 0,

            0, 0, factor

        ]);

    }

    distance(a, b) {

        if (
            !this.isInside(a) ||
            !this.isInside(b)
        ) {

            throw new RangeError(
                "Both positions must lie inside the Poincare ball."
            );

        }

        const dot = 
            a.x * b.x +
            a.y * b.y +
            a.z * b.z;

        const normA =
            a.x * a.x +
            a.y * a.y +
            a.z * a.z;

        const normB =
            b.x * b.x +
            b.y * b.y +
            b.z * b.z;

        const differenceSquared =

            (a.x - b.x) ** 2 +

            (a.y - b.y) ** 2 +

            (a.z - b.z) ** 2;

        const numerator =
            this.k * differenceSquared;

        const denominator =
            (1 - this.k * normA) *
            (1 - this.k * normB);

        const argument =
            1 + 2 * numerator / denominator;

        return Math.acosh(
            Math.max(1, argument)
        ) / Math.sqrt(this.k);

    }

}

export { PoincareGeometry };

```

src/world/fields/geometry/poincare-projection.js
_______
```

/**
 * PoincareProjection
 *
 * Maps Somnium's finite 3D world into an open
 * three-dimensional Poincare ball.
 *
 * All three spatial dimensions participate
 * in the transformation.
 *
 * The world boundary approaches the ball
 * boundary asymptotically.
 */

class PoincareProjection {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.bounds = {
            minX: options.minX ?? -500000,
            maxX: options.maxX ?? 500000,

            minY: options.minY ?? -2000,
            maxY: options.maxY ?? 1000,

            minZ: options.minZ ?? -500000,
            maxZ: options.maxZ ?? 500000
        };

        this.curvature = options.curvature ?? -1;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.curvature) ||
            this.curvature >= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Poincare curvature must be negative."
            );
        }

        this.k = -this.curvature;

        this.radius = 1 / Math.sqrt(this.k);

	this.characteristicLength =
options.characteristicLength ?? 100000;

        this.boundaryEpsilon =
            options.boundaryEpsilon ?? 1e-9;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.characteristicLength) ||
            this.characteristicLength <= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid characteristic length."
            );
        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.boundaryEpsilon) ||
            this.boundaryEpsilon <= 0 ||
            this.boundaryEpsilon >= 0.01
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid boundary epsilon."
            );
        }

        this.center = {
            x: (this.bounds.minX + this.bounds.maxX) / 2,
            y: (this.bounds.minY + this.bounds.maxY) / 2,
            z: (this.bounds.minZ + this.bounds.maxZ) / 2
        };

        this.validateBounds();
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // VALIDATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    validateBounds() {

        const values = Object.values(this.bounds);

        if (
            !values.every(Number.isFinite) ||
            this.bounds.minX >= this.bounds.maxX ||
            this.bounds.minY >= this.bounds.maxY ||
            this.bounds.minZ >= this.bounds.maxZ
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid three-dimensional world bounds."
            );
        }
    }

    validatePosition(position) {

        if (
            !position ||
            !Number.isFinite(position.x) ||
            !Number.isFinite(position.y) ||
            !Number.isFinite(position.z)
        ) {
            throw new TypeError(
                "Invalid three-dimensional position."
            );
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // WORLD BOUNDARY DISTANCE
    // -----------------------------------------

    getBoundaryDistance(direction) {

        const b = this.bounds;

        const distances = [];

        if (direction.x > 0) {
            distances.push(
                (b.maxX - this.center.x) / direction.x
            );
        } else if (direction.x < 0) {
            distances.push(
                (b.minX - this.center.x) / direction.x
            );
        }

        if (direction.y > 0) {
            distances.push(
                (b.maxY - this.center.y) / direction.y
            );
        } else if (direction.y < 0) {
            distances.push(
                (b.minY - this.center.y) / direction.y
            );
        }

        if (direction.z > 0) {
            distances.push(
                (b.maxZ - this.center.z) / direction.z
            );
        } else if (direction.z < 0) {
            distances.push(
                (b.minZ - this.center.z) / direction.z
            );
        }

        return Math.min(...distances);
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // WORLD TO POINCARE BALL
    // -----------------------------------------

    worldToBall(position) {

        this.validatePosition(position);

        const dx = position.x - this.center.x;
        const dy = position.y - this.center.y;
        const dz = position.z - this.center.z;

        const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz);

        if (distance === 0) {
            return { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
        }

        const direction = {
            x: dx / distance,
            y: dy / distance,
            z: dz / distance
        };

        const boundaryDistance =
            this.getBoundaryDistance(direction);

        const normalizedRadius =
            distance / boundaryDistance;

        if (
            normalizedRadius < 0 ||
            normalizedRadius > 1 + 1e-10
        ) {
            throw new RangeError(
                "Position lies outside world bounds."
            );
        }

        // Clamp exact boundary positions slightly inward.
        // The mathematical boundary remains unreachable.

        const u = Math.min(
            normalizedRadius,
            1 - this.boundaryEpsilon
        );

        // Compactify the finite world.

        const hyperbolicDistance =
            this.characteristicLength *
            u / (1 - u);

        // Hyperbolic radial projection.

const projectedRadius = Math.min(
    this.radius * Math.tanh(
        hyperbolicDistance / (2 * this.radius)
    ),
    this.radius * (1 - this.boundaryEpsilon)
);
	
        return {
            x: direction.x * projectedRadius,
            y: direction.y * projectedRadius,
            z: direction.z * projectedRadius
        };
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // POINCARE BALL TO WORLD
    // -----------------------------------------

    ballToWorld(position) {

        this.validatePosition(position);

        const radius = Math.hypot(
            position.x,
            position.y,
            position.z
        );

        if (radius >= this.radius) {
            throw new RangeError(
                "Position is outside the open Poincare ball."
            );
        }

        if (radius === 0) {
            return { ...this.center };
        }

        const direction = {
            x: position.x / radius,
            y: position.y / radius,
            z: position.z / radius
        };

        const ratio = radius / this.radius;

        const hyperbolicDistance =
            2 * this.radius * Math.atanh(ratio);

        const u =
            hyperbolicDistance /
            (
                this.characteristicLength +
                hyperbolicDistance
            );

        const boundaryDistance =
            this.getBoundaryDistance(direction);

        const worldDistance =
            u * boundaryDistance;

        return {
            x: this.center.x +
                direction.x * worldDistance,

            y: this.center.y +
                direction.y * worldDistance,

            z: this.center.z +
                direction.z * worldDistance
        };
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // PROJECTION INFORMATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    getInfo() {

        return {
            geometry: "poincare-3d",

            dimensions: 3,

            curvature: this.curvature,

            radius: this.radius,

            center: { ...this.center },

            bounds: { ...this.bounds },

            characteristicLength:
                this.characteristicLength,

            boundaryReachable: false
        };
    }
}

export { PoincareProjection };

```

```
import { GeometryModel } from "./geometry-model.js";
import { MetricTensor } from "../metric-tensor.js";

class EuclideanGeometry extends GeometryModel {

    constructor() {

        super("euclidean");

        this.metric = MetricTensor.identity();

    }

    metricAt() {

        return this.metric;

    }

    distance(a, b) {

        return Math.hypot(
            b.x - a.x,
            b.y - a.y,
            b.z - a.z
        );

    }

}

export { EuclideanGeometry };
```
