src/world/world-manager.js
_______
```

import { WorldChunk } from "./world-chunk.js";

export class WorldManager {
    constructor(scene, terrain, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.terrain = terrain;

	this.fallbackScheduled = false;

        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;
        this.renderRadius = options.renderRadius ?? 14;
        this.highDetailRadius = options.highDetailRadius ?? 2;

        this.maxChunksPerUpdate =
            options.maxChunksPerUpdate ?? 6;

        this.chunks = new Map();

        this.enabled = true;

        this.stats = {
            generated: 0,
            unloaded: 0,
            loaded: 0,
            workerCompleted: 0,
            workerErrors: 0,
            pendingMeshes: 0
        };

        this.minChunkX = Math.floor(
            terrain.min.x / this.chunkSize
        );

        this.maxChunkX = Math.ceil(
            terrain.max.x / this.chunkSize
        ) - 1;

        this.minChunkZ = Math.floor(
            terrain.min.z / this.chunkSize
        );

        this.maxChunkZ = Math.ceil(
            terrain.max.z / this.chunkSize
        ) - 1;

this.material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
    "worldTerrainMaterial",
    scene
);

this.material.diffuseColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
    1.0, 1.0, 1.0
);

this.material.ambientColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
    1, 1, 1
);

// Preserve vertex colors, but allow actual illumination.
this.material.useVertexColors = true;
this.material.disableLighting = false;

// Small nighttime visibility contribution.
this.material.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
    0.1, 0.1, 0.1
);

this.material.backFaceCulling = false;

this.material.specularColor = BABYLON.Color3.Black();
this.material.roughness = 1.0;
	
        this.meshQueue = [];
        this.worker = null;
        this.workerReady = false;
        this.workerBusy = false;
        this.nextTaskId = 1;

        this.initializeWorker();
    }

    getKey(x, z) {
        return `${x},${z}`;
    }

    getChunk(x, z) {
        return this.chunks.get(this.getKey(x, z)) ?? null;
    }

    getCoordinates(position) {
        return {
            x: Math.floor(position.x / this.chunkSize),
            z: Math.floor(position.z / this.chunkSize)
        };
    }

    isInsideWorld(x, z) {
        return (
            x >= this.minChunkX &&
            x <= this.maxChunkX &&
            z >= this.minChunkZ &&
            z <= this.maxChunkZ
        );
    }

	initializeWorker() {
    if (typeof Worker === "undefined") {
        console.warn(
            "[WorldManager] Workers unavailable. Using fallback."
        );
        return;
    }

    try {
        this.worker = new Worker(
            new URL("./terrain-mesh-worker.js", import.meta.url),
            { type: "module" }
        );

        this.worker.onmessage = event => {
            const message = event.data;

            if (message.type === "ready") {
                if (this.workerInitTimer) {
                    clearTimeout(this.workerInitTimer);
                    this.workerInitTimer = null;
                }

                this.workerReady = true;

                console.log(
                    "[WorldManager] Terrain worker ready."
                );

                this.pumpWorker();
                return;
            }

            this.handleWorkerMessage(message);
        };

        this.worker.onerror = error => {
            this.failWorker(error);
        };

        this.worker.onmessageerror = error => {
            this.failWorker(error);
        };

        const pixels = this.terrain.pixels.slice();

        this.worker.postMessage({
            type: "init",
            terrain: {
                width: this.terrain.width,
                height: this.terrain.height,
                worldSize: this.terrain.worldSize,
                minX: this.terrain.min.x,
                minZ: this.terrain.min.z,
                minHeight: this.terrain.minHeight,
                maxHeight: this.terrain.maxHeight,
                heightChannel:
                    this.terrain.heightChannel ?? "average"
            },
            pixels: pixels.buffer
        }, [pixels.buffer]);

        this.workerInitTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            if (!this.workerReady && this.worker) {
                this.failWorker(
                    "Worker initialization timed out."
                );
            }
        }, 5000);

    } catch (error) {
        this.failWorker(error);
    }
}

    handleWorkerMessage(message) {
        if (message.type === "ready") {
            this.workerReady = true;
            this.pumpWorker();
            return;
        }

        if (
            message.type === "complete" ||
            message.type === "error"
        ) {
            const task = this.currentTask;

            this.workerBusy = false;
            this.currentTask = null;

            if (
                task &&
                task.id === message.id
            ) {
                const chunk = this.chunks.get(task.key);

                if (
                    chunk &&
                    !chunk.disposed &&
                    chunk.revision === message.revision
                ) {
                    if (message.type === "complete") {
                        chunk.applyMeshData(
                            message.data,
                            task.lod,
                            this.material
                        );

                        this.stats.workerCompleted++;
                    } else {
                        this.stats.workerErrors++;

                        console.error(
                            "[WorldManager] Mesh generation failed:",
                            message.message
                        );

                        chunk.generateMesh(
                            task.lod,
                            this.material
                        );
                    }
                }
            }

            this.pumpWorker();
        }
    }

    scheduleMesh(chunk, lod) {
        if (!chunk || chunk.disposed) return;

        const neighbors = this.getNeighbors(chunk);

        chunk.setNeighbors(neighbors);
        chunk.lod = lod;
        chunk.revision++;

        const job = {
            id: this.nextTaskId++,
            key: this.getKey(chunk.x, chunk.z),
            revision: chunk.revision,
            lod,

            x: chunk.x,
            z: chunk.z,
            size: chunk.size,

            neighborLODs: {
                north: neighbors.north?.lod ?? lod,
                south: neighbors.south?.lod ?? lod,
                east: neighbors.east?.lod ?? lod,
                west: neighbors.west?.lod ?? lod
            }
        };

        this.meshQueue = this.meshQueue.filter(
            item => item.key !== job.key
        );

        this.meshQueue.push(job);

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;

        this.pumpWorker();
    }

    pumpWorker() {
        if (
            !this.worker ||
            !this.workerReady ||
            this.workerBusy ||
            this.meshQueue.length === 0
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const job = this.meshQueue.shift();

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;

        const chunk = this.chunks.get(job.key);

        if (
            !chunk ||
            chunk.disposed ||
            chunk.revision !== job.revision
        ) {
            this.pumpWorker();
            return;
        }

        this.currentTask = job;
        this.workerBusy = true;

        this.worker.postMessage({
            type: "generate",
            id: job.id,
            revision: job.revision,
            job
        });
    }

	processFallbackQueue() {
    if (
        this.fallbackScheduled ||
        this.meshQueue.length === 0 ||
        !this.enabled
    ) {
        return;
    }

    this.fallbackScheduled = true;

    setTimeout(() => {
        this.fallbackScheduled = false;

        const job = this.meshQueue.shift();

        if (job) {
            const chunk = this.chunks.get(job.key);

            if (
                chunk &&
                !chunk.disposed &&
                chunk.revision === job.revision
            ) {
                try {
                    chunk.generateMesh(
                        job.lod,
                        this.material
                    );
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error(
                        "[WorldManager] Synchronous terrain generation failed:",
                        error
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;
        this.processFallbackQueue();

    }, 0);
}

    getNeighbors(chunk) {
        return {
            north: this.getChunk(chunk.x, chunk.z - 1),
            south: this.getChunk(chunk.x, chunk.z + 1),
            east: this.getChunk(chunk.x + 1, chunk.z),
            west: this.getChunk(chunk.x - 1, chunk.z)
        };
    }

    rebuildChunk(chunk) {
        this.scheduleMesh(
            chunk,
            chunk.lod < 0 ? 3 : chunk.lod
        );
    }

    createChunk(x, z, lod) {
        if (!this.isInsideWorld(x, z)) return null;

        const chunk = new WorldChunk(
            x,
            z,
            this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain,
            this.scene
        );

        this.chunks.set(
            this.getKey(x, z),
            chunk
        );

        this.stats.generated++;
        this.stats.loaded++;

        this.scheduleMesh(chunk, lod);

        // Existing neighbors may need new boundary stitching.
        for (const neighbor of Object.values(
            this.getNeighbors(chunk)
        )) {
            if (neighbor) {
                this.rebuildChunk(neighbor);
            }
        }

        return chunk;
    }

    getLOD(x, z, cameraPosition) {
        const centerX = (x + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;
        const centerZ = (z + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;

        const distance = Math.hypot(
            centerX - cameraPosition.x,
            centerZ - cameraPosition.z
        );

if (
    distance <=
    this.chunkSize * (this.highDetailRadius + 0.5)
) return 0;

if (
    distance <=
    this.chunkSize * (this.highDetailRadius + 2)
) return 1;

if (distance <= this.chunkSize * 8) return 2;

        return 3;
    }

    getPriority(x, z, cameraPosition, forward) {
        const centerX = (x + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;
        const centerZ = (z + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;

        const dx = centerX - cameraPosition.x;
        const dz = centerZ - cameraPosition.z;

        const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dz);

        const length = Math.max(distance, 1);

        const directionDot = (
            dx / length * forward.x +
            dz / length * forward.z
        );

        // Lower scores are generated first.
        return distance -
            Math.max(0, directionDot) *
            this.chunkSize * 4;
    }

    update(cameraPosition, forward = null) {
        if (!this.enabled || !this.terrain.ready) return;

        const center = this.getCoordinates(cameraPosition);

        const direction = forward ?? {
            x: 0,
            z: 1
        };

        const required = [];

        for (
            let x = Math.max(
                this.minChunkX,
                center.x - this.renderRadius
            );
            x <= Math.min(
                this.maxChunkX,
                center.x + this.renderRadius
            );
            x++
        ) {
            for (
                let z = Math.max(
                    this.minChunkZ,
                    center.z - this.renderRadius
                );
                z <= Math.min(
                    this.maxChunkZ,
                    center.z + this.renderRadius
                );
                z++
            ) {
                const dx = x - center.x;
                const dz = z - center.z;

                if (
                    dx * dx + dz * dz >
                    this.renderRadius * this.renderRadius
                ) continue;

                required.push({
                    x,
                    z,
                    priority: this.getPriority(
                        x,
                        z,
                        cameraPosition,
                        direction
                    ),
                    lod: this.getLOD(
                        x,
                        z,
                        cameraPosition
                    )
                });
            }
        }

        required.sort(
            (a, b) => a.priority - b.priority
        );

        let scheduled = 0;

        for (const item of required) {
            const chunk = this.getChunk(item.x, item.z);

            if (!chunk) {
                if (scheduled >= this.maxChunksPerUpdate) {
                    continue;
                }

                this.createChunk(
                    item.x,
                    item.z,
                    item.lod
                );

                scheduled++;
                continue;
            }

            if (chunk.lod !== item.lod) {
                if (scheduled >= this.maxChunksPerUpdate) {
                    continue;
                }

                this.scheduleMesh(chunk, item.lod);
                scheduled++;
            }
        }

        const unloadRadius = this.renderRadius + 1;

        for (const [key, chunk] of this.chunks) {
            const dx = chunk.x - center.x;
            const dz = chunk.z - center.z;

            if (
                dx * dx + dz * dz >
                unloadRadius * unloadRadius
            ) {
                chunk.dispose();
                this.chunks.delete(key);

                this.meshQueue = this.meshQueue.filter(
                    task => task.key !== key
                );

                this.stats.unloaded++;
                this.stats.loaded--;
            }
        }

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;

        if (!this.worker) {
            this.processFallbackQueue();
        } else {
            this.pumpWorker();
        }
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            ...this.stats,
            currentChunks: this.chunks.size,
            chunkSize: this.chunkSize,
            renderRadius: this.renderRadius,
            worldSize: this.terrain.worldSize,
            workerActive: !!this.worker,
            workerReady: this.workerReady
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        this.enabled = false;

        if (this.worker) {
            this.worker.terminate();
            this.worker = null;
        }

        for (const chunk of this.chunks.values()) {
            chunk.dispose();
        }

        this.chunks.clear();
        this.meshQueue.length = 0;

        this.material.dispose();

        this.stats.loaded = 0;
        this.stats.pendingMeshes = 0;
    }

failWorker(reason) {
    console.error(
        "[WorldManager] Worker failure:",
        reason
    );

    this.stats.workerErrors++;

    if (this.workerInitTimer) {
        clearTimeout(this.workerInitTimer);
        this.workerInitTimer = null;
    }

    const failedTask = this.currentTask;
    this.currentTask = null;

    if (this.worker) {
        this.worker.terminate();
        this.worker = null;
    }

    this.workerReady = false;
    this.workerBusy = false;

    // Retry the outstanding task synchronously.
    if (failedTask) {
        const chunk = this.chunks.get(failedTask.key);

        if (
            chunk &&
            !chunk.disposed &&
            chunk.revision === failedTask.revision
        ) {
            this.meshQueue.unshift(failedTask);
        }
    }

    this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;
    this.processFallbackQueue();
}
}

```

src/world/terrain-mesh-worker.js
_______
```

let terrain = null;

function clamp(value, min, max) {
    return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
}

function lerp(a, b, t) {
    return a + (b - a) * t;
}


function hashNoise2D(x, z, seed = 1) {
    let n =
        Math.imul(x | 0, 374761393) +
        Math.imul(z | 0, 668265263) +
        Math.imul(seed | 0, 1442695041);

    n = Math.imul(
        n ^ (n >>> 13),
        1274126177
    );

    n ^= n >>> 16;

    return (n >>> 0) / 4294967295;
}

function smoothNoise2D(x, z, seed = 1) {
    const ix = Math.floor(x);
    const iz = Math.floor(z);

    const fx = x - ix;
    const fz = z - iz;

    const u = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx);
    const v = fz * fz * (3 - 2 * fz);

    const a = hashNoise2D(ix, iz, seed);
    const b = hashNoise2D(ix + 1, iz, seed);
    const c = hashNoise2D(ix, iz + 1, seed);
    const d = hashNoise2D(ix + 1, iz + 1, seed);

    return lerp(
        lerp(a, b, u),
        lerp(c, d, u),
        v
    );
}

function fractalNoise2D(x, z, seed, octaves = 3) {
    let value = 0;
    let amplitude = 1;
    let frequency = 1;
    let totalAmplitude = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        value += smoothNoise2D(
            x * frequency,
            z * frequency,
            seed + i * 1013
        ) * amplitude;

        totalAmplitude += amplitude;
        amplitude *= 0.5;
        frequency *= 2;
    }

    return value / totalAmplitude;
}

function mixColor(a, b, t) {
    return [
        lerp(a[0], b[0], t),
        lerp(a[1], b[1], t),
        lerp(a[2], b[2], t)
    ];
}


function pixelValue(x, z) {
    const t = terrain;

    x = clamp(x, 0, t.width - 1);
    z = clamp(z, 0, t.height - 1);

    const index = (Math.floor(z) * t.width + Math.floor(x)) * 4;
    const p = t.pixels;

    const r = p[index];
    const g = p[index + 1];
    const b = p[index + 2];

    switch (t.heightChannel) {
        case "red": return r / 255;
        case "green": return g / 255;
        case "blue": return b / 255;
        case "luminance":
            return (
                0.2126 * r +
                0.7152 * g +
                0.0722 * b
            ) / 255;
        case "average":
        default:
            return (r + g + b) / 765;
    }
}

function heightAt(x, z) {
    const u = clamp(
        (x - terrain.minX) / terrain.worldSize,
        0, 1
    );

    const v = clamp(
        (z - terrain.minZ) / terrain.worldSize,
        0, 1
    );

    const px = u * (terrain.width - 1);
    const pz = v * (terrain.height - 1);

    const x0 = Math.floor(px);
    const z0 = Math.floor(pz);

    const x1 = Math.min(x0 + 1, terrain.width - 1);
    const z1 = Math.min(z0 + 1, terrain.height - 1);

    const tx = px - x0;
    const tz = pz - z0;

    const a = lerp(
        pixelValue(x0, z0),
        pixelValue(x1, z0),
        tx
    );

    const b = lerp(
        pixelValue(x0, z1),
        pixelValue(x1, z1),
        tx
    );

    return lerp(
        terrain.minHeight,
        terrain.maxHeight,
        lerp(a, b, tz)
    );
}


function terrainColor(height, slope, worldX, worldZ) {
    const t = terrain;

    const elevation = clamp(
        (height - t.minHeight) /
        (t.maxHeight - t.minHeight),
        0,
        1
    );

    const slopeDegrees =
        Math.atan(slope) * 180 / Math.PI;

    const seed = 91827;

    const moisture = fractalNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.0025,
        worldZ * 0.0025,
        seed + 1000,
        3
    ) * 0.7 + fractalNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.009,
        worldZ * 0.009,
        seed + 3000,
        3
    ) * 0.3;

    const geology = fractalNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.00375,
        worldZ * 0.00375,
        seed + 2000,
        3
    );

    const variation = fractalNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.025,
        worldZ * 0.025,
        seed + 5000,
        3
    );

    const exposure = clamp(
        slopeDegrees / 42 * 0.78 +
        elevation * 0.12 +
        (1 - moisture) * 0.10,
        0,
        1
    );

const soilType = soilTypeAt(worldX, worldZ);

const soilColors = {
    clay:        [0.48, 0.29, 0.21],
    sand:        [0.92, 0.73, 0.39],
    fertileSoil: [0.43, 0.29, 0.18],
    loam:        [0.35, 0.25, 0.16],
    organic:     [0.19, 0.13, 0.085],
    scree:       [0.43, 0.41, 0.37]
};

let soil = soilColors[soilType];
    
let bedrock;

    if (geology < 0.28) {
        bedrock = [0.63, 0.60, 0.51];
    } else if (geology < 0.48) {
        bedrock = [0.65, 0.43, 0.29];
    } else if (geology > 0.84) {
        bedrock = [0.24, 0.23, 0.22];
    } else {
        bedrock = [0.39, 0.37, 0.34];
    }

    if (elevation > 0.70 && geology > 0.65) {
        bedrock = [0.39, 0.37, 0.34];
    }

    // Blend surface material into exposed bedrock.
    let color = mixColor(
        soil,
        bedrock,
        clamp((exposure - 0.25) / 0.65)
    );

    // Small-scale geological surface variation.
    const texture = 0.92 + variation * 0.16;

    color = color.map(value =>
        clamp(value * texture, 0, 1)
    );

    // Snow and frost at the highest elevations.
    if (elevation > 0.92) {
        const snow = [0.82, 0.84, 0.82];

        color = mixColor(
            color,
            snow,
            clamp((elevation - 0.92) / 0.08)
        );
    }

    return color;
}


function terrainColor(height, slope, worldX, worldZ) {
    const elevation = clamp(
        (height - terrain.minHeight) /
        (terrain.maxHeight - terrain.minHeight),
        0,
        1
    );

    // Deterministic regional geological variation.
    const geology = smoothNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.0018,
        worldZ * 0.0018,
        91827
    );

    const detail = fractalNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.012,
        worldZ * 0.012,
        48129,
        3
    );

    const moisture = fractalNoise2D(
        worldX * 0.0025,
        worldZ * 0.0025,
        28193,
        3
    );

    const slopeDegrees = Math.atan(slope) * 180 / Math.PI;

    let color;

    // Underlying geological material.
    if (geology < 0.22) {
        // Limestone
        color = [0.72, 0.68, 0.57];
    } else if (geology < 0.43) {
        // Sandstone
        color = [0.68, 0.45, 0.28];
    } else if (geology > 0.82) {
        // Basalt
        color = [0.36, 0.38, 0.41];
    } else {
        // Granite
        color = [0.52, 0.52, 0.51];
    }

    // Surface soil classification.
    let soil;

    if (moisture < 0.25) {
        // Dry sand.
        soil = [0.83, 0.68, 0.38];
    } else if (moisture < 0.43) {
        // Mineral-rich sandy loam.
        soil = [0.65, 0.48, 0.31];
    } else if (moisture < 0.62) {
        // Fertile brown soil.
        soil = [0.43, 0.29, 0.18];
    } else if (moisture < 0.79) {
        // Grey-blue clay.
        soil = [0.43, 0.49, 0.56];
    } else {
        // Organic soil.
        soil = [0.28, 0.22, 0.16];
    }

    // Exposed rock on steep terrain.
    const exposure = clamp(
        (slopeDegrees - 12) / 30 +
        elevation * 0.25,
        0,
        1
    );

    const rockBlend = smoothstep(
        0.25,
        0.85,
        exposure
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        color[i] = lerp(
            soil[i],
            color[i],
            rockBlend
        );
    }

    // Natural local color variation.
    const texture = 0.94 + detail * 0.12;

    color = color.map(channel =>
        clamp(channel * texture, 0.02, 1)
    );

    // Snow at extreme elevations.
    if (elevation > 0.93) {
        const snowBlend = smoothstep(
            0.93,
            0.99,
            elevation
        );

        color = mixColor(
            color,
            [0.88, 0.89, 0.87],
            snowBlend
        );
    }

    return color;
}

function smoothstep(min, max, value) {
    const t = clamp((value - min) / (max - min));
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}



function generateMesh(job) {
    const {
        x,
        z,
        size,
        lod,
        neighborLODs
    } = job;

    const resolution = [32, 16, 8, 4][
        clamp(lod, 0, 3)
    ];

    const stride = size / resolution;

    const count = (resolution + 1) ** 2;

    const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
    const normals = new Float32Array(count * 3);
    const colors = new Float32Array(count * 4);

    const indices = new Uint32Array(
        resolution * resolution * 6
    );

    const originX = x * size;
    const originZ = z * size;

    const edgeHeight = (edge, index) => {
        const otherLOD = neighborLODs[edge];

        const worldDistance = index * stride;

        if (otherLOD <= lod) {
            return edge === "north" || edge === "south"
                ? heightAt(
                    originX + worldDistance,
                    originZ + (edge === "south" ? size : 0)
                )
                : heightAt(
                    originX + (edge === "east" ? size : 0),
                    originZ + worldDistance
                );
        }

        const coarseResolution = [32, 16, 8, 4][
            clamp(otherLOD, 0, 3)
        ];

        const coarseStride = size / coarseResolution;

        const lower =
            Math.floor(worldDistance / coarseStride) *
            coarseStride;

        const upper = Math.min(
            lower + coarseStride,
            size
        );

        const t = upper === lower
            ? 0
            : (worldDistance - lower) / (upper - lower);

        let ax, az, bx, bz;

        if (edge === "north" || edge === "south") {
            az = bz = originZ +
                (edge === "south" ? size : 0);

            ax = originX + lower;
            bx = originX + upper;
        } else {
            ax = bx = originX +
                (edge === "east" ? size : 0);

            az = originZ + lower;
            bz = originZ + upper;
        }

        return lerp(
            heightAt(ax, az),
            heightAt(bx, bz),
            t
        );
    };

    for (let iz = 0; iz <= resolution; iz++) {
        for (let ix = 0; ix <= resolution; ix++) {
            const worldX = originX + ix * stride;
            const worldZ = originZ + iz * stride;

            let h = heightAt(worldX, worldZ);

            if (iz === 0) h = edgeHeight("north", ix);
            if (iz === resolution) h = edgeHeight("south", ix);
            if (ix === 0) h = edgeHeight("west", iz);
            if (ix === resolution) h = edgeHeight("east", iz);

            const spacing = Math.max(2, stride * 0.25);

            const dx = (
                heightAt(worldX + spacing, worldZ) -
                heightAt(worldX - spacing, worldZ)
            ) / (2 * spacing);

            const dz = (
                heightAt(worldX, worldZ + spacing) -
                heightAt(worldX, worldZ - spacing)
            ) / (2 * spacing);

            const length = Math.hypot(dx, 1, dz);

            const index = iz * (resolution + 1) + ix;
            const offset = index * 3;

            positions[offset] = worldX;
            positions[offset + 1] = h;
            positions[offset + 2] = worldZ;

            normals[offset] = -dx / length;
            normals[offset + 1] = 1 / length;
            normals[offset + 2] = -dz / length;

		const color = terrainColor(
    h,
    Math.hypot(dx, dz),
    worldX,
    worldZ
);

            const colorOffset = index * 4;

            colors[colorOffset] = color[0];
            colors[colorOffset + 1] = color[1];
            colors[colorOffset + 2] = color[2];
            colors[colorOffset + 3] = 1;
        }
    }

    let cursor = 0;

    for (let iz = 0; iz < resolution; iz++) {
        for (let ix = 0; ix < resolution; ix++) {
            const a = iz * (resolution + 1) + ix;
            const b = a + 1;
            const c = a + resolution + 1;
            const d = c + 1;

            indices[cursor++] = a;
            indices[cursor++] = c;
            indices[cursor++] = b;

            indices[cursor++] = b;
            indices[cursor++] = c;
            indices[cursor++] = d;
        }
    }

    return {
        positions,
        normals,
        colors,
        indices
    };
}

self.onmessage = event => {
    const message = event.data;

    if (message.type === "init") {
        terrain = {
            ...message.terrain,
            pixels: new Uint8ClampedArray(message.pixels)
        };

        self.postMessage({ type: "ready" });
        return;
    }

    if (message.type === "generate" && terrain) {
        try {
            const data = generateMesh(message.job);

            self.postMessage({
                type: "complete",
                id: message.id,
                revision: message.revision,
                data: {
                    positions: data.positions,
                    normals: data.normals,
                    colors: data.colors,
                    indices: data.indices
                }
            }, [
                data.positions.buffer,
                data.normals.buffer,
                data.colors.buffer,
                data.indices.buffer
            ]);
        } catch (error) {
            self.postMessage({
                type: "error",
                id: message.id,
                revision: message.revision,
                message: error.message
            });
        }
    }
};

function soilTypeAt(worldX, worldZ) {
    const t = terrain;

    const u = clamp(
        (worldX - t.minX) / t.worldSize,
        0,
        1
    );

    const v = clamp(
        (worldZ - t.minZ) / t.worldSize,
        0,
        1
    );

    const mapValue = pixelValue(
        u * (t.width - 1),
        v * (t.height - 1)
    );

    if (mapValue < 0.18) return "clay";
    if (mapValue < 0.38) return "sand";
    if (mapValue < 0.58) return "fertileSoil";
    if (mapValue < 0.76) return "loam";
    if (mapValue < 0.90) return "organic";

    return "scree";
}

class Hydrology {

    constructor(terrain, waterSystem, cellSize = 20) {

        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.waterSystem = waterSystem;

        this.cellSize = cellSize;

        this.cells = new Map();

        this.defaultMoisture = 0.35;

        this.evaporationRate = 0.015;

        this.rainfallRetention = 1.0;
	this.geologicalField = null;

    }


    getCellCoordinates(x, z) {

        return {

            x: Math.floor(x / this.cellSize),

            z: Math.floor(z / this.cellSize)

        };

    }


    getCellKey(x, z) {

        return `${x},${z}`;

    }


	getCell(x, z) {
    const coordinates = this.getCellCoordinates(x, z);

    const key = this.getCellKey(
        coordinates.x,
        coordinates.z
    );

    if (!this.cells.has(key)) {
        const worldX = (coordinates.x + 0.5) * this.cellSize;
        const worldZ = (coordinates.z + 0.5) * this.cellSize;

        const soil = this.geologicalField
            ? this.geologicalField.sample(worldX, worldZ)
            : null;

        const capacity = soil
            ? 0.25 + soil.moistureCapacity * 0.75
            : 0.75;

        this.cells.set(key, {
            moisture: this.defaultMoisture * capacity,
            capacity,
            infiltration: soil?.infiltration ?? 0.65,
            drainage: soil?.drainage ?? 0.5,
            fertility: soil?.fertility ?? 0.5,
            soilType: soil?.surfaceSoil ?? "loam",
            rainfall: 0,
            evaporation: 0
        });
    }

    return this.cells.get(key);
}

sampleSoil(position) {
    if (!this.geologicalField || !position) {
        return null;
    }

    return this.geologicalField.sample(
        position.x,
        position.z
    );
}


sampleMoisture(position) {
    if (this.waterSystem.isUnderwater(position)) {
        return 1.0;
    }

    const cell = this.getCell(
        position.x,
        position.z
    );

    return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
        cell.moisture / Math.max(cell.capacity, 0.001),
        0,
        1
    );
}


    addRain(position, radius, amount) {

        const minX = Math.floor(
            (position.x - radius) / this.cellSize
        );

        const maxX = Math.floor(
            (position.x + radius) / this.cellSize
        );

        const minZ = Math.floor(
            (position.z - radius) / this.cellSize
        );

        const maxZ = Math.floor(
            (position.z + radius) / this.cellSize
        );


        for (let x = minX; x <= maxX; x++) {

            for (let z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++) {

                const worldX =
                    (x + 0.5) * this.cellSize;

                const worldZ =
                    (z + 0.5) * this.cellSize;

                const dx = worldX - position.x;
                const dz = worldZ - position.z;

                const distance = Math.sqrt(
                    dx * dx + dz * dz
                );

                if (distance > radius) continue;


                const terrainPosition =
                    this.terrain.sampleAt(
                        worldX,
                        worldZ
                    );

                if (!terrainPosition) continue;


                // Rain falling into the ocean
                // does not increase soil moisture.

                if (
                    this.waterSystem.isUnderwater(
                        terrainPosition
                    )
                ) {
                    continue;
                }


                const falloff =
                    1 - distance / radius;

                const cell = this.getCell(
                    worldX,
                    worldZ
                );

const depositedWater =
    amount *
    falloff *
    this.rainfallRetention *
    cell.infiltration;

const availableCapacity = Math.max(
    0,
    cell.capacity - cell.moisture
);

const storedWater = Math.min(
    depositedWater,
    availableCapacity
);

cell.moisture += storedWater;

// Water that cannot infiltrate or be stored
// becomes runoff rather than disappearing
// into the soil moisture reservoir.
const runoff = depositedWater - storedWater;

cell.rainfall += storedWater;
cell.runoff = (cell.runoff ?? 0) + runoff;
                cell.rainfall += depositedWater;

            }

        }

    }


update(deltaYears) {
    for (const cell of this.cells.values()) {
        const evaporation =
            this.evaporationRate *
            (0.35 + cell.drainage * 0.65) *
            deltaYears;

        const drainage =
            cell.drainage *
            0.025 *
            deltaYears;

        cell.moisture = Math.max(
            0,
            cell.moisture - evaporation - drainage
        );

        cell.evaporation += evaporation;

        cell.rainfall = Math.max(
            0,
            cell.rainfall - deltaYears * 0.1
        );

        cell.runoff = Math.max(
            0,
            (cell.runoff ?? 0) - deltaYears * 0.1
        );
    }
}

    getStatistics() {

        let totalMoisture = 0;

        let count = 0;

        for (const cell of this.cells.values()) {

            totalMoisture += cell.moisture;

            count++;

        }

        return {

            cells: count,

            averageMoisture:
                count > 0
                    ? totalMoisture / count
                    : this.defaultMoisture

        };

    }

}

export { Hydrology };

```

export class WaterSystem {
    constructor(scene, terrain, seaLevel = -100, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.seaLevel = seaLevel;

        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;
        this.renderRadius = options.renderRadius ?? 3;

        this.evaporationRate = 0.12;

        this.tiles = new Map();
        this.disposed = false;

        this.material = this.createWaterMaterial();

        console.log("[Water] Regional water system initialized.");
    }

    createWaterMaterial() {
        const material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "regionalOceanMaterial",
            this.scene
        );

        material.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.025, 0.19, 0.30);

        material.specularColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.35, 0.45, 0.55);

        material.specularPower = 64;

        material.emissiveColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.005, 0.025, 0.04);

        material.backFaceCulling = false;

        return material;
    }

    getChunkKey(x, z) {
        return `${x},${z}`;
    }

    createOceanTile(chunkX, chunkZ) {
        const minX = Math.max(
            chunkX * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.min.x
        );

        const maxX = Math.min(
            (chunkX + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.max.x
        );

        const minZ = Math.max(
            chunkZ * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.min.z
        );

        const maxZ = Math.min(
            (chunkZ + 1) * this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain.max.z
        );

        const width = maxX - minX;
        const depth = maxZ - minZ;

        if (width <= 0 || depth <= 0) return null;

        const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateGround(
            `ocean_${chunkX}_${chunkZ}`,
            {
                width,
                height: depth,
                subdivisions: 1
            },
            this.scene
        );

        mesh.position.set(
            (minX + maxX) * 0.5,
            this.seaLevel,
            (minZ + maxZ) * 0.5
        );

        mesh.material = this.material;
        mesh.isPickable = false;
        mesh.receiveShadows = false;

        mesh.metadata = {
            waterType: "ocean",
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        };

        this.tiles.set(
            this.getChunkKey(chunkX, chunkZ),
            mesh
        );

        return mesh;
    }

    removeTile(key) {
        const mesh = this.tiles.get(key);

        if (!mesh) return;

        mesh.dispose();
        this.tiles.delete(key);
    }

    update(cameraPosition, renderRadius = this.renderRadius) {
        if (this.disposed || !cameraPosition) return;

        const centerX = Math.floor(
            cameraPosition.x / this.chunkSize
        );

        const centerZ = Math.floor(
            cameraPosition.z / this.chunkSize
        );

        const desired = new Set();

        for (
            let x = centerX - renderRadius;
            x <= centerX + renderRadius;
            x++
        ) {
            for (
                let z = centerZ - renderRadius;
                z <= centerZ + renderRadius;
                z++
            ) {
                const minX = x * this.chunkSize;
                const minZ = z * this.chunkSize;

                const maxX = minX + this.chunkSize;
                const maxZ = minZ + this.chunkSize;

                // Ignore chunks outside the terrain.
                if (
                    maxX <= this.terrain.min.x ||
                    minX >= this.terrain.max.x ||
                    maxZ <= this.terrain.min.z ||
                    minZ >= this.terrain.max.z
                ) {
                    continue;
                }

                const key = this.getChunkKey(x, z);

                desired.add(key);

                if (!this.tiles.has(key)) {
                    this.createOceanTile(x, z);
                }
            }
        }

        // Remove distant water meshes.
        for (const key of this.tiles.keys()) {
            if (!desired.has(key)) {
                this.removeTile(key);
            }
        }
    }

    isUnderwater(position) {
        if (!position) return false;

        return position.y < this.seaLevel;
    }

    getTerrainWaterDepth(position) {
        if (!position) return 0;

        return Math.max(
            0,
            this.seaLevel - position.y
        );
    }

    getEvaporationPotential(position) {
        const terrainPosition = this.terrain.sampleAt(
            position.x,
            position.z
        );

        if (!terrainPosition) return 0.2;

        const distanceToSeaLevel = Math.abs(
            terrainPosition.y - this.seaLevel
        );

        const coastalInfluence =
            1 - BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                distanceToSeaLevel / 300,
                0,
                1
            );

        const oceanInfluence =
            terrainPosition.y < this.seaLevel
                ? 1
                : coastalInfluence;

        return BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            0.2 + oceanInfluence * 0.8,
            0.2,
            1
        );
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            activeOceanTiles: this.tiles.size,
            chunkSize: this.chunkSize,
            seaLevel: this.seaLevel,
            renderRadius: this.renderRadius
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        for (const key of Array.from(this.tiles.keys())) {
            this.removeTile(key);
        }

        this.material.dispose();
    }
}

class RainSystem {

    constructor(scene) {

        this.scene = scene;

        this.active = false;

        this.particles = new BABYLON.ParticleSystem(
            "rainParticles",
            2000,
            scene
        );


        // Generate a tiny procedural particle texture.

        const texture = new BABYLON.DynamicTexture(
            "rainTexture",
            {
                width: 8,
                height: 16
            },
            scene,
            false
        );

        const context = texture.getContext();

        context.clearRect(0, 0, 8, 16);

        context.fillStyle = "#c5e5ff";

        context.fillRect(3, 0, 2, 16);

        texture.update();

        this.particles.particleTexture = texture;


        this.particles.emitter =
            BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();

        this.particles.minEmitBox =
            new BABYLON.Vector3(-25, 0, -25);

        this.particles.maxEmitBox =
            new BABYLON.Vector3(25, 0, 25);


        this.particles.color1 =
            new BABYLON.Color4(0.65, 0.8, 1, 0.65);

        this.particles.color2 =
            new BABYLON.Color4(0.8, 0.9, 1, 0.8);

        this.particles.colorDead =
            new BABYLON.Color4(0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 0);


        this.particles.minSize = 0.08;

        this.particles.maxSize = 0.14;

        this.particles.minLifeTime = 0.5;

        this.particles.maxLifeTime = 1.2;


        this.particles.emitRate = 1200;

        this.particles.minEmitPower = 20;

        this.particles.maxEmitPower = 35;

        this.particles.updateSpeed = 0.015;


        this.particles.direction1 =
            new BABYLON.Vector3(-1, -30, -1);

        this.particles.direction2 =
            new BABYLON.Vector3(1, -30, 1);


        this.particles.gravity =
            new BABYLON.Vector3(0, -9.81, 0);


        this.particles.blendMode =
            BABYLON.ParticleSystem.BLENDMODE_STANDARD;


        this.particles.minAngularSpeed = 0;

        this.particles.maxAngularSpeed = 0;

    }


    start(position, intensity = 1) {

        this.particles.emitter.copyFrom(position);

        this.particles.emitRate =
            1200 * BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                intensity,
                0.2,
                2
            );

        if (!this.active) {

            this.particles.start();

            this.active = true;

        }

    }


    stop() {

        if (!this.active) return;

        this.particles.stop();

        this.active = false;

    }


    update() {

        // Babylon.js updates the particle system
        // automatically during scene rendering.

    }


    dispose() {

        this.particles.dispose();

    }

}

export { RainSystem };

src/game/game-manager.js

class GameManager {
    constructor(scene, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.canvas = options.canvas;

        this.flightCamera = options.flightCamera;
        this.thirdPersonCamera = null;
        this.editorCamera = null;

        this.playerController = null;
        this.characterCameraController = null;

        this.mode = "menu";
        this.resumeMode = "flight";

        this.callbacks = new Set();
        this.disposed = false;

        if (!this.flightCamera) {
            throw new Error("Flight camera is required.");
        }

        this.flightCamera.detachControl();

        this.onKeyDown = event => {
            if (event.repeat) return;

            if (event.code === "KeyV") {
                if (
                    this.mode === "flight" ||
                    this.mode === "thirdPerson"
                ) {
                    this.togglePerspective();
                }
            }

            if (event.code === "Escape") {
                if (this.mode === "editor") {
                    this.exitEditor();
                } else if (
                    this.mode === "flight" ||
                    this.mode === "thirdPerson"
                ) {
                    this.pause();
                } else if (this.mode === "paused") {
                    this.resume();
                }
            }
        };

        window.addEventListener("keydown", this.onKeyDown);
    }

    registerThirdPerson(camera, controller, cameraController) {
        this.thirdPersonCamera = camera;
        this.playerController = controller;
        this.characterCameraController = cameraController;

        camera.detachControl();
        this.applyMode();
    }

    registerEditor(camera) {
        this.editorCamera = camera;
        camera.detachControl();
    }

    setMode(mode) {
        const valid = [
            "menu",
            "flight",
            "thirdPerson",
            "paused",
            "editor"
        ];

        if (!valid.includes(mode)) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid game mode: ${mode}`);
        }

        if (
            mode === "thirdPerson" &&
            (!this.thirdPersonCamera || !this.playerController)
        ) {
            throw new Error("Third-person systems are not registered.");
        }

        if (mode === "editor" && !this.editorCamera) {
            throw new Error("Editor camera is not registered.");
        }

        if (this.mode === mode) return;

        const previous = this.mode;

        if (
            previous === "flight" ||
            previous === "thirdPerson"
        ) {
            this.resumeMode = previous;
        }

        this.mode = mode;
        this.applyMode();

        for (const callback of this.callbacks) {
            callback(mode, previous);
        }
    }

    applyMode() {
        this.flightCamera.detachControl();
        this.thirdPersonCamera?.detachControl();
        this.editorCamera?.detachControl();

        this.playerController?.setEnabled(false);
        this.characterCameraController?.setEnabled(false);

        if (this.mode === "flight") {
            this.scene.activeCamera = this.flightCamera;
            this.flightCamera.attachControl(this.canvas, true);
        }

        if (this.mode === "thirdPerson") {
            this.scene.activeCamera = this.thirdPersonCamera;
            this.playerController.setEnabled(true);
            this.characterCameraController.setEnabled(true);
        }

        if (this.mode === "editor") {
            this.scene.activeCamera = this.editorCamera;
            this.editorCamera.attachControl(this.canvas, true);
        }

        if (this.mode === "menu" || this.mode === "paused") {
            this.scene.activeCamera = this.flightCamera;
        }
    }

    startFlight() {
        this.setMode("flight");
    }

    startThirdPerson() {
        this.setMode("thirdPerson");
    }

    startEditor() {
        this.setMode("editor");
    }

    exitEditor() {
        if (this.mode !== "editor") return;

        this.setMode(this.resumeMode);
    }

    togglePerspective() {
        if (this.mode === "flight") {
            this.startThirdPerson();
        } else if (this.mode === "thirdPerson") {
            this.startFlight();
        }
    }

    pause() {
        if (
            this.mode !== "flight" &&
            this.mode !== "thirdPerson"
        ) return;

        this.resumeMode = this.mode;
        this.setMode("paused");
    }

    resume() {
        if (this.mode === "paused") {
            this.setMode(this.resumeMode);
        }
    }

    returnToMenu() {
        this.setMode("menu");
    }

    onModeChanged(callback) {
        this.callbacks.add(callback);
        return () => this.callbacks.delete(callback);
    }

    getState() {
        return {
            mode: this.mode,
            resumeMode: this.resumeMode
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        window.removeEventListener("keydown", this.onKeyDown);

        this.flightCamera.detachControl();
        this.thirdPersonCamera?.detachControl();
        this.editorCamera?.detachControl();

        this.callbacks.clear();
    }
}

export { GameManager };

```

class PlayerCharacter {
    constructor(scene, spatialTransform, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        this.worldPosition = {
            x: options.x ?? 0,
            y: 0,
            z: options.z ?? 0
        };

        this.yaw = 0;

        const sample = options.terrain.sampleAt(
            this.worldPosition.x,
            this.worldPosition.z
        );

        if (!sample) {
            throw new Error("Unable to sample initial player terrain.");
        }

        this.worldPosition.y = sample.y;

        this.root = new BABYLON.TransformNode(
            "somniumPlayer",
            scene
        );

        this.body = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateCapsule(
            "playerBody",
            {
                height: 1.8,
                radius: 0.35,
                tessellation: 12
            },
            scene
        );

        this.body.parent = this.root;
        this.body.position.y = 0.9;

        this.head = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere(
            "playerHead",
            {
                diameter: 0.48,
                segments: 12
            },
            scene
        );

        this.head.parent = this.root;
        this.head.position.y = 1.95;

        this.material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "playerMaterial",
            scene
        );

        this.material.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.18, 0.48, 0.78);

        this.body.material = this.material;

        this.head.material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
            "playerHeadMaterial",
            scene
        );

        this.head.material.diffuseColor =
            new BABYLON.Color3(0.82, 0.69, 0.55);

        this.syncRenderPosition();
    }

    syncRenderPosition() {
        const projected = this.transform.worldToRender(
            this.worldPosition
        );

        this.root.position.set(
            projected.x,
            projected.y,
            projected.z
        );

        this.root.rotation.y = this.yaw;
    }

    setWorldPosition(position) {
        this.worldPosition = {
            x: position.x,
            y: position.y,
            z: position.z
        };

        this.syncRenderPosition();
    }

    dispose() {
        this.root.dispose(false, true);
        this.material.dispose();
    }
}

export { PlayerCharacter };
```

class PlayerController {
    constructor(character, terrain, canvas, options = {}) {
        this.character = character;
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.canvas = canvas;

        this.walkSpeed = options.walkSpeed ?? 180;
        this.runSpeed = options.runSpeed ?? 360;
        this.turnSpeed = options.turnSpeed ?? 2.2;

        this.keys = new Set();

        this.enabled = true;
        this.disposed = false;

        this.mouseSensitivity = 0.004;
        this.dragging = false;
        this.previousMouseX = 0;

        this.onKeyDown = event => {
            this.keys.add(event.code);

            if (
                ["KeyW", "KeyA", "KeyS", "KeyD", "ShiftLeft"]
                    .includes(event.code)
            ) {
                event.preventDefault();
            }
        };

        this.onKeyUp = event => {
            this.keys.delete(event.code);
        };

        this.onBlur = () => {
            this.keys.clear();
            this.dragging = false;
        };

        this.onPointerDown = event => {
            if (event.button !== 0) return;

            this.dragging = true;
            this.previousMouseX = event.clientX;
        };

        this.onPointerMove = event => {
            if (!this.dragging || !this.enabled) return;

            const dx = event.clientX - this.previousMouseX;

            this.previousMouseX = event.clientX;

            this.character.yaw +=
                dx * this.mouseSensitivity;
        };

        this.onPointerUp = () => {
            this.dragging = false;
        };

        window.addEventListener("keydown", this.onKeyDown);
        window.addEventListener("keyup", this.onKeyUp);
        window.addEventListener("blur", this.onBlur);

        canvas.addEventListener(
            "pointerdown",
            this.onPointerDown
        );

        window.addEventListener(
            "pointermove",
            this.onPointerMove
        );

        window.addEventListener(
            "pointerup",
            this.onPointerUp
        );
    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
        this.keys.clear();
        this.dragging = false;
    }

    update(deltaSeconds) {
        if (!this.enabled || this.disposed) return;

        const dt = Math.min(deltaSeconds, 0.05);

        if (dt <= 0) return;

        const forward =
            (this.keys.has("KeyW") ? 1 : 0) -
            (this.keys.has("KeyS") ? 1 : 0);

        const turn =
            (this.keys.has("KeyA") ? 1 : 0) -
            (this.keys.has("KeyD") ? 1 : 0);

        this.character.yaw +=
            turn * this.turnSpeed * dt;

        const speed = this.keys.has("ShiftLeft")
            ? this.runSpeed
            : this.walkSpeed;

        const yaw = this.character.yaw;

        const dx = Math.sin(yaw) * forward * speed * dt;
        const dz = Math.cos(yaw) * forward * speed * dt;

        if (dx === 0 && dz === 0) {
            return;
        }

        const current = this.character.worldPosition;

        const nextX = current.x + dx;
        const nextZ = current.z + dz;

        // Respect the authoritative world bounds.
        if (
            nextX < this.terrain.min.x ||
            nextX > this.terrain.max.x ||
            nextZ < this.terrain.min.z ||
            nextZ > this.terrain.max.z
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const sample = this.terrain.sampleAt(
            nextX,
            nextZ
        );

        if (!sample) return;

        // Initial terrain-following implementation.
        // Steep-slope collision will be added separately.
        this.character.setWorldPosition({
            x: nextX,
            y: sample.y,
            z: nextZ
        });
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;

        window.removeEventListener(
            "keydown",
            this.onKeyDown
        );

        window.removeEventListener(
            "keyup",
            this.onKeyUp
        );

        window.removeEventListener(
            "blur",
            this.onBlur
        );

        this.canvas.removeEventListener(
            "pointerdown",
            this.onPointerDown
        );

        window.removeEventListener(
            "pointermove",
            this.onPointerMove
        );

        window.removeEventListener(
            "pointerup",
            this.onPointerUp
        );

        this.keys.clear();
    }
}

export { PlayerController };
```

src/game/camera-controller.js
_______
```

class CharacterCameraController {
    constructor(
        scene,
        camera,
        character,
        spatialTransform,
        options = {}
    ) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.camera = camera;
        this.character = character;
        this.transform = spatialTransform;

        // Initial camera framing.
        this.distance = options.distance ?? 24;
        this.height = options.height ?? 8;

        // Zoom constraints.
        this.minDistance = options.minDistance ?? 6;
        this.maxDistance = options.maxDistance ?? 120;
        this.zoomSensitivity = options.zoomSensitivity ?? 0.001;

        // Camera smoothing.
        this.smoothing = options.smoothing ?? 8;

        this.enabled = false;
        this.disposed = false;

        this.onWheel = event => {
            if (!this.enabled || this.disposed) return;

            event.preventDefault();

            // Normalize zoom according to wheel movement.
            const zoomFactor = Math.exp(
                event.deltaY * this.zoomSensitivity
            );

            this.distance = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
                this.distance * zoomFactor,
                this.minDistance,
                this.maxDistance
            );
        };

        this.canvas = scene.getEngine().getRenderingCanvas();

        if (this.canvas) {
            this.canvas.addEventListener(
                "wheel",
                this.onWheel,
                { passive: false }
            );
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CAMERA POSITION
    // -----------------------------------------

    getDesiredPosition() {
        const player = this.character.worldPosition;
        const yaw = this.character.yaw;

        const desiredWorld = {
            x: player.x - Math.sin(yaw) * this.distance,
            y: player.y + this.height,
            z: player.z - Math.cos(yaw) * this.distance
        };

        return this.transform.worldToRender(desiredWorld);
    }

    getTargetPosition() {
        const player = this.character.worldPosition;

        return this.transform.worldToRender({
            x: player.x,
            y: player.y + 1.3,
            z: player.z
        });
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // UPDATE
    // -----------------------------------------

    update(deltaSeconds) {
        if (!this.enabled || this.disposed) return;

        const desired = this.getDesiredPosition();
        const target = this.getTargetPosition();

        const alpha = 1 - Math.exp(
            -this.smoothing * Math.max(0, deltaSeconds)
        );

        const desiredVector = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            desired.x,
            desired.y,
            desired.z
        );

        BABYLON.Vector3.LerpToRef(
            this.camera.position,
            desiredVector,
            alpha,
            this.camera.position
        );

        this.camera.setTarget(
            new BABYLON.Vector3(
                target.x,
                target.y,
                target.z
            )
        );
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // ENABLE / DISABLE
    // -----------------------------------------

    setEnabled(enabled) {
        this.enabled = !!enabled && !this.disposed;

        if (this.enabled) {
            // Immediately initialize the camera position
            // when entering third-person mode.
            const desired = this.getDesiredPosition();

            this.camera.position.set(
                desired.x,
                desired.y,
                desired.z
            );

            const target = this.getTargetPosition();

            this.camera.setTarget(
                new BABYLON.Vector3(
                    target.x,
                    target.y,
                    target.z
                )
            );
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // ZOOM CONFIGURATION
    // -----------------------------------------

    setDistance(distance) {
        this.distance = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            distance,
            this.minDistance,
            this.maxDistance
        );
    }

    getZoomInfo() {
        return {
            distance: this.distance,
            height: this.height,
            minDistance: this.minDistance,
            maxDistance: this.maxDistance
        };
    }

    // -----------------------------------------
    // CLEANUP
    // -----------------------------------------

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.disposed = true;
        this.enabled = false;

        if (this.canvas) {
            this.canvas.removeEventListener(
                "wheel",
                this.onWheel
            );
        }

        this.camera = null;
        this.character = null;
    }
}

export { CharacterCameraController };
